### [1] [Carbon-made Lobachevsky surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14602)
*R. Gabbrielli,P. Pais,P. Castorina,T. P. C. Klaver,D. Legut,O. De Luca,R. G. Agostino,A. Iorio*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文综述并推广了用碳材料构建常负高斯曲率曲面（即罗巴切夫斯基平面的实验室实现）的方法，核心原理是平衡的Gauss–Bonnet定理：拓扑决定的曲率在disclination缺陷、弹性变形六边形与边界之间分配。作者据此构造了三类赝球面的缺陷排布，并与实际碳结构及模拟纳米挤压结果对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实验室中实现常负高斯曲率的碳表面，可用于类比引力（椭圆、贝尔特拉米、双曲赝球面对应BTZ黑洞三种静态相）以及海水淡化等应用。作者希望围绕"平衡的Gauss–Bonnet定理"这一统一原理，系统回顾并拓展这些碳结构的构造方法。

Method: 以平衡的Gauss–Bonnet定理为统一框架：把由拓扑固定的总曲率在disclination缺陷、弹性变形的六边形和边界之间进行分配。利用$SL(2,\mathbb{R})$的椭圆、抛物、双曲单参数子群来生成三类赝球面上的缺陷排布——作者构造了椭圆族的排布，恢复了已知的贝尔特拉米赝球面的排布，并提出了双曲赝球面的一种排布；同时通过显式多面体构造说明"仅由六边形构成的球面必为穿孔球面"。

Result: 给出了椭圆族赝球面的缺陷排布构造，恢复了贝尔特拉米赝球面的已知排布，并为双曲赝球面提出新的缺陷排布方案；显式多面体构造表明，仅含六边形的球面拓扑上等价于穿孔球面。将理想缺陷排布与设计所得及模拟纳米挤压所得的碳结构进行了比较。

Conclusion: 碳制常负曲率曲面的构造可由平衡的Gauss–Bonnet定理统一理解，缺陷排布由$SL(2,\mathbb{R})$的子群导出；作者推测无穷多个Fuchsian群可能对应于无穷多个罗巴切夫斯基曲面，为后续实验与理论对比提供了方向。

Abstract: Carbon-made surfaces of constant negative Gaussian curvature realize portions of the Lobachevsky plane in the laboratory, with motivations ranging from analogue gravity, where the elliptic, Beltrami and hyperbolic pseudospheres correspond to the three static phases of the Bañados--Teitelboim--Zanelli black hole, to water desalination. We review and extend their construction around a single principle, the balanced Gauss--Bonnet theorem: the curvature fixed by the topology is shared among disclination defects, elastically deformed hexagons and boundaries. Curvature without defects cannot change the topology: a sphere made of hexagons only is a punctured sphere, as an explicit polyhedral construction shows. The arrangements of the defects on the three pseudospheres stem from the elliptic, parabolic and hyperbolic one-parameter subgroups of $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$: we construct them for the elliptic family, recover the known one of the Beltrami pseudosphere, and propose one for the hyperbolic pseudosphere. We compare these ideal arrangements with carbon structures obtained by design and by simulated nano-extrusion, and suggest that the infinitely many Fuchsian groups may correspond to the infinitely many Lobachevsky surfaces.

</details>


### [2] [Certifying Majorana persistence through Hamiltonian learning from boundary measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16832)
*Tohid Farajollahpour,Alireza Qaiumzadeh*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅通过链端有限测量即可认证局域量子动力学（马约拉纳零模的持续存在）而无需确定体拓扑的方法，结合哈密顿量学习与对易子边界，对Kitaev链给出状态无关的自关联下界，并在实验与仿真中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认证拓扑/马约拉纳零模通常依赖体拓扑性质或全局哈密顿量，本文旨在证明仅利用链端局域测量与局域键标定即可可靠认证局域马约拉纳算符的持久性，从而降低认证对体信息的依赖，并给出可量化的失效率（prescribed failure probability）保证。

Method: 将哈密顿量学习与对易子界（commutator bounds）相结合：利用时间分辨的单次边界测量与局域键标定构造局域哈密顿系数的一致置信集；在明确的器件假设下，对容许的延拓（compatible continuations）推导状态无关的对称化自关联下界；当首键跃迁与配对相等且无额外边界项时，通过一个精确恒等式仅从末端量子比特即可推断，即使其他地方存在任意时间无关相互作用。

Result: 在边界“甜点”（sweet spot）附近的合成基准中，可在约60倍测量窗口的时长上认证局域马约拉纳算符的持续存在；三量子比特与六量子比特的IBM实验记录加强了编程相干哈密顿量的条件保证；对量子点模型的应用将调谐精度与持续时间和失谐引起的劈裂联系起来。

Conclusion: 链端有限测量与局域键标定足以在明确器件假设下以受控失效率认证局域马约拉纳算符的持续存在，无需确定体拓扑；该方法对Kitaev链及量子点模型具有实用意义，并将调谐精度与相干持续时间、失谐劈裂定量关联。

Abstract: Finite measurements at a chain end can certify local quantum dynamics without determining bulk topology. We combine Hamiltonian learning with commutator bounds to certify the persistence of localized Majorana operators in Kitaev chains. Time-resolved single-shot boundary measurements and local bond calibrations define joint confidence sets for local Hamiltonian coefficients. Under explicit device assumptions, these sets yield state-independent lower bounds on symmetrized autocorrelations with a prescribed failure probability, valid for all compatible continuations. With equal hopping and pairing on the first bond and no additional boundary terms, an exact identity enables inference from the end qubit alone, even with arbitrary time-independent interactions elsewhere. Synthetic benchmarks near a boundary sweet spot certify persistence for up to about 60 times the measurement window. Three- and six-qubit IBM records strengthen conditional guarantees for the programmed coherent Hamiltonian. Applications to quantum-dot models relate tuning precision to persistence times and detuning-induced splitting.

</details>


### [3] [High-pressure behavior of the low-frequency mode of water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17096)
*Maria Rescigno,Guillaume Radtke,Umbertoluca Ranieri,Richard Gaal,Luigi Paolasini,Andrea Piovano,Ferdinando Formisano,Paola Giura,Livia Eleonora Bove*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 利用高分辨率非弹性X射线散射（IXS）研究液态水（H₂O/D₂O）在室温及0.1–1 GPa压缩下的低频（~10 meV）太赫兹模式，发现该模式始终接近临界阻尼且频率随压力显著硬化，通过与Ice VI的IXS和从头算模拟对比，提出其并非单一明确激发，而是多个色散微弱的分支叠加形成的混合特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液态水太赫兹谱中约10 meV的低频（LF）模式虽已发现数十年，但其微观起源仍存在争议：主要解释包括横向声学类激发、横向光学类模式以及模式-模式耦合机制。作者旨在厘清该模式在压缩条件下的真实物理本质。

Method: 采用能量分辨率达1.6 meV的高分辨率IXS，探测室温下H₂O和D₂O在0.1至1 GPa范围内的集体动力学；同时将结果与晶体Ice VI的IXS测量及从头算（ab initio）分子动力学模拟进行对比，分析低频模式随压力的演化及其色散特性。

Result: 在整个研究压力范围内，低频模式始终处于重阻尼、接近临界阻尼状态；其频率随压力显著硬化，硬化幅度远超横向声学模式的预期。与Ice VI的对比显示，在相同能量区间存在一簇色散微弱的模式，表明液态水低频模式源于多个分支叠加的混合特征，而非单一良定义的激发。

Conclusion: 液态水的低频太赫兹模式并非单一的横向声学或光学类激发，而是由若干弱色散分支叠加形成的混合模式，这为理解水在压缩条件下异常太赫兹动力学提供了一个自洽的微观图景。

Abstract: The low-frequency (LF) mode observed in the THz spectrum of liquid water near 10 meV has been known for decades, yet its microscopic origin remains debated. Leading interpretations assign it to a transverse acoustic-like excitation, a transverse optic-like mode, or a mode-mode coupling mechanism. We address this question by probing the collective dynamics of H$_2$O and D$_2$O at room temperature and pressures from 0.1 to 1 GPa using high-resolution (1.6 meV) inelastic X-ray scattering (IXS). The LF mode remains heavily damped, close to critical damping, throughout the explored range, while its frequency hardens markedly with pressure, much more than expected for a transverse acoustic mode. Comparison with IXS measurements and \textit{ab initio} simulations of crystalline Ice VI reveals a cluster of weakly dispersive modes in the same energy range, suggesting that the low-frequency mode in liquid water reflects a mixed character arising from the superposition of several branches rather than a single well-defined excitation, providing a coherent microscopic picture of water's anomalous THz dynamics under compression.

</details>

### [4] [Singlet-triplet superconductivity from repulsive interactions in a $p$-wave magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14609)
*Hanbit Oh,Laura Classen,Daniel F. Agterberg*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用parquet重整化群(pRG)研究p波磁体中范霍夫奇点附近电子在纯排斥相互作用下的不稳定性竞争，发现超导是全程主导序，所得超导态为自旋单态与三重态的混合，并在双子晶格模型中进一步出现有限动量等自旋配对密度波，机制可推广至奇宇称磁体并通向节点拓扑超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期p波磁体（宿主奇宇称自旋劈裂）提议的启发，作者旨在厘清范霍夫奇点附近的电子在局域排斥相互作用下，p波自旋劈裂的对称性如何决定各竞争不稳定性的优劣，从而为奇宇称磁体实现非常规超导提供机制。

Method: 采用parquet重整化群(pRG)分析。先构建单子晶格p波磁体模型进行研究，再推广到更实际的双子晶格模型；通过对比各不稳定性的重整化流，判定超导为领先不稳定性，并分析配对通道的自旋单态/三重态混合及有限动量配对。

Result: 在纯排斥相互作用下，超导在整个相图中都是领先不稳定性。所得超导态一般为自旋单态与自旋三重态配对混合；当Tc远小于范霍夫电子与其反演伴之间的能量偏移时，趋向等权重混合。在双子晶格模型中超导仍居领先地位，而子晶格自由度导致各单态-三重态配对通道竞争，甚至出现有限动量等自旋配对密度波(PDW)。

Conclusion: 该机制广泛适用于奇宇称磁体，为节点拓扑超导提供了一条自然实现路径，并揭示了p波自旋劈裂对称性对超导配对态结构的决定性作用。

Abstract: Motivated by recent proposals of $p$-wave magnets, which host odd-parity spin splitting, we study electrons near Van Hove singularities with local repulsive interactions. We demonstrate how the symmetry of the $p$-wave spin splitting governs the competition among instabilities. Using a parquet renormalization group (pRG) analysis of a simple one-sublattice model of a $p$-wave magnet, we find that superconductivity is the leading instability over the entire phase diagram for purely repulsive interactions. The resulting superconducting state is generically a mixture of spin-singlet and spin-triplet pairing and approaches an equal-weight mixture when $T_c$ is small compared with the energy offset between each Van Hove electron and its inversion partner. Extending the pRG analysis to a more realistic two-sublattice model, we find that superconductivity remains the leading instability, while the sublattice degree of freedom leads to a competition among various singlet-triplet pairing channels and even a finite-momentum equal-spin pair-density wave. Our mechanism applies broadly to odd-parity magnets and opens a natural route to nodal topological superconductivity.

</details>


### [5] [Superlattice Stabilization of the Topological Quantum Hall Ferromagnet in Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14679)
*Tiago Cecchi,Ahmed Khalifa,Shubhayu Chatterjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究超晶格势对石墨烯中自旋与谷序竞争的影响，证明可调超晶格势能在电荷中性点稳定铁磁量子霍尔拓扑绝缘体，无需大面内磁场或强的介电屏蔽抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯中自旋序与谷序的竞争使其成为在量子霍尔区工程化关联拓扑相的多功能平台，但稳定铁磁量子霍尔拓扑绝缘体通常需要苛刻条件（大面内磁场或强介电屏蔽）。作者希望寻找一种更可控的手段——超晶格势——来重组这一竞争并稳定该拓扑相。

Method: 首先采用计入超晶格势的广义重整化群（RG）方法计算决定不同竞争态选择的相互作用各向异性；随后指出超晶格势引入的色散倾向于部分自旋极化的补偿半金属；最后用重整化各向异性进行自洽的动量空间 Hartree-Fock 计算，确定绝缘自旋极化铁磁态的稳定区间，并通过绝热连接验证其拓扑特性。

Result: 超晶格势抑制了与自旋极化相竞争的各向异性，同时引入色散诱发部分自旋极化的补偿半金属；在重整化各向异性下的自洽 Hartree-Fock 计算中确定了绝缘自旋极化铁磁态的稳定区间，并验证该铁磁态绝热连接到量子霍尔铁磁体，从而确认其拓扑性质。

Conclusion: 超晶格势可作为一种控制相互作用石墨烯相图、并进入其螺旋（helical）量子霍尔区的手段。

Abstract: Competition between spin and valley orders makes graphene a versatile platform for engineering correlated topological phases in the quantum Hall regime. We show that a tunable superlattice potential reorganizes this competition, and stabilizes the ferromagnetic quantum Hall topological insulator at charge neutrality without requiring a large in-plane magnetic field or strong dielectric suppression of Coulomb interactions. The interaction anisotropies responsible for choosing between different competing states are first computed by a generalized renormalization group procedure that accounts for the superlattice potential. We find that the potential suppresses anisotropies that compete with spin-polarization. At the same time, the potential introduces dispersion which prefers a partly spin-polarized compensated semi-metal. Therefore, we carry out a self-consistent momentum-space Hartree-Fock computation with renormalized aniostropies to establish the regime of stability of the insulating spin-polarized ferromagnet. Finally, we verify that this ferromagnet is adiabatically connected to the quantum Hall ferromagnet, thereby confirming its topological character. Our results establish superlattice potentials as a means of controlling the phase diagram of interacting graphene and accessing its helical quantum Hall regime.

</details>


### [6] [Termination-selective Kondo hybridization of topological surface states in CeCuAs$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15022)
*Bo Chen,Chen Zhang,Hao Liu,Qi-Yi Wu,Zhong-Tuo Fu,Ying Zhou,Chun-Hui Lyu,Yang Luo,Zi-Jie Xu,Hai-Long Deng,Zheng-Tai Liu,Mao Ye,Zhen-Hua Chen,Yao-Bo Huang,Sheng-Tao Cui,Zhe Sun,Da-Wei Shen,Jun He,Yu-Xia Duan,Jian-Qiao Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用端面分辨ARPES、Ce 4d→4f共振光电子能谱及表面电子结构计算，研究了Kondo晶格化合物CeCuAs2。发现As1和CeAs2两种端面具有不同的表面电子结构；其中CeAs2端面上拓扑Dirac表面态与近费米面的弱色散Ce 4f带共存。共振光电子能谱确认了低能4f特征及晶体场相关精细结构，降温时4f与Dirac区域谱重均增强，为端面选择性的f-Dirac杂化及拓扑表面态中4f特征的发展提供了光谱证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kondo晶格中的强关联拓扑态是凝聚态物理的前沿问题，边界电子与局域f态之间的耦合可以重塑低能电子结构。作者旨在探究表面端面如何微观地调控Kondo关联与拓扑边界电子之间的相互作用。

Method: 结合端面分辨的角分辨光电子能谱(ARPES)、Ce 4d→4f共振光电子能谱以及表面电子结构计算，对Kondo晶格化合物CeCuAs2进行研究。通过费米面几何特征与端面特异性计算，将两种表面电子结构分别归属于As1和CeAs2端面。

Result: 在CeAs2端面上，与计算所得非平庸体拓扑相联系的Dirac表面态，与近费米面处的弱色散Ce 4f带共存。共振光电子能谱识别出低能4f特征，并揭示Dirac分支所穿越能量范围内的晶体场相关精细结构。降温时，4f区域和Dirac区域的谱重均明显增强，而参考的导带区域变化甚微，表明存在端面选择性的f-Dirac杂化，并在拓扑表面态中发展出4f特征。

Conclusion: 表面端面可作为调控Kondo关联与拓扑边界电子之间相互作用的微观手段，为拓扑表面态中关联效应的形成提供了端面选择性的光谱证据。

Abstract: Strongly correlated topological states in Kondo lattices represent a frontier in condensed matter physics, where the coupling between boundary electrons and localized f states can reshape the low-energy electronic structure. Here, we combine termination-resolved angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), Ce 4$d \rightarrow 4f$ resonant photoemission, and surface electronic structure calculations to investigate the Kondo lattice compound CeCuAs$_2$. Two distinct surface electronic structures are assigned to the As1 and CeAs2 terminations through their characteristic Fermi-surface geometries and termination-specific calculations. On CeAs2, a Dirac surface state associated with the calculated nontrivial bulk topology coexists with a weakly dispersive Ce 4$f$ band near $E_F$. Resonant photoemission identifies the low-energy 4$f$ character and reveals crystal-field-related fine structure in the energy range traversed by the Dirac branch. On cooling, the 4$f$ and Dirac regions both gain spectral weight, whereas a reference conduction-band region changes much less. Together, these observations provide spectroscopic evidence for termination-selective $f$-Dirac hybridization and the development of 4$f$ character in a topological surface state. Our results identify surface termination as a microscopic means of controlling the interplay between Kondo correlations and topological boundary electrons.

</details>


### [7] [Maximally Chiral Eigenstate from Inverse Thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15081)
*Tigran A. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文展示一种有序量子态可在某一方向支持有限速度的波，而在另一方向能量呈指数衰减；作者为自旋-1/2海森堡本征态指定该能谱并重构出非局域哈密顿量，发现该有序载流态为基态、磁响应斜率发散、熵对数衰减并含黄金比例，并提出谱学与热力学探测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否通过设计相互作用，在有序量子态中实现方向依赖的动力学（单向有限速度、反方向能量指数小）以及相应的反常热力学行为，从而建立相互作用设计与定向动力学、异常热力学之间的联系。

Method: 对自旋-1/2海森堡体系，预先指定具有单向传播和反向指数衰减特征的能谱，并据此逆问题重构一个非局域哈密顿量；通过热力学构造选出有序、携带电流的基态；计算磁矩、弱场响应、单位自旋熵及其对数温度依赖（系数精确含黄金比例）；进一步解析自旋响应获得可调能量的束缚对，并引入交换作用与更平滑的模式选择在最低能量处恢复二次型激发。

Result: 有序载流态可被选为基态；其磁矩保持有限，但对弱场的响应斜率发散；单位自旋熵仅以温度对数的倒数方式趋于零，且精确系数含黄金比例；发现一个能量可调而强度不变的束缚对；加入交换和更平滑的模式选择后，最低能量处恢复二次型激发。

Conclusion: 相互作用的设计与定向动力学及异常热力学之间可建立直接联系；这些反直觉特征可由动量分辨的自旋谱学、弱场磁强测量和低温熵测量加以探测。

Abstract: We show that an ordered quantum state can support waves with a finite speed in one direction and exponentially small energies in the other. We prescribe this spectrum for a spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg eigenstate and reconstruct a nonlocal Hamiltonian. The thermodynamic construction selects the ordered, current-carrying state as a ground state. Its magnetic moment remains finite while its response to a weak field has a divergent slope. The entropy per spin vanishes only inversely with a logarithm of temperature, with an exact coefficient containing the golden ratio. QUBO constructs complete candidate spaces under fixed electron and orbital counts, while the POMDP selects successive calculations, updates its belief from each completed result, and thereby corrects and refines the active orbitals. Abstract: The realization of integer and fractional Chern insulators in moiré systems provides a lattice setting for exploring how the structure of Bloch wave functions influences the interplay between topology and disorder. Here we investigate how layer-dependent disorder shapes Hall plateau widths and transitions. Using a realistic three-orbital model of twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$, we compare Hall and bulk transport under onsite-potential, displacement-field, and twist-angle disorder. At equal bare disorder strength, onsite-potential disorder produces broad integer plateaus with nearly direct transitions, whereas displacement-field disorder yields narrow plateaus separated by conducting regions with pronounced Hall-resistance dips. These contrasting behaviors arise from the layer composition of the Bloch states: contributions from different layer under displacement-field disorder partially cancel, weakening the bulk localization. Composite-fermion mean-field theory further yields fractional plateaus at $ν_h=2/3$ and $3/5$ with the same disorder dependence of plateau widths. These findings identify layer-dependent disorder as an important mechanism shaping Chern plateau stability in moiré systems beyond bare disorder strength alone.

Abstract: One of the most important challenges in condensed matter physics is understanding the macroscopic properties of strange metals, particularly why their resistivity remains linear in temperature over several decades. We propose that a (2+1)d theory of fermions coupled to underdamped quantum-critical bosons through a spatially disordered interaction provides this long-sought-after theoretical explanation. The key novelty of our work is to establish the appropriate separation of scales so that the bosons are underdamped. Using this framework, we obtain two distinct forms for the fermionic self-energy: a conformal-limit solution, $Σ\sim\sqrtω$, and a marginal Fermi-liquid solution, $Σ\simω\log(1/ω)$, corresponding to regimes above and below the Mott-Ioffe-Regel limit, respectively. These solutions produce Planckian scattering, exhibit exact $T$-linear resistivity, $ω/T$ scaling in the optical conductivity, as well as $\sim T\log(1/T)$ heat capacity at low temperatures. Moreover, our theory naturally unifies strange- and bad-metal phenomena within a single framework, suggesting that we are now approaching a new epoch in understanding the physics of strongly correlated matter.

Abstract: The last couple of decades have seen enormous theoretical and experimental research efforts aimed at finding quantum spin liquids (QSLs) in geometrically frustrated (GF) magnetic materials. Experimental demonstrations of QSLs are challenging due to the indirect character of possible QSL signatures and the presence of impurities in materials, which may destroy or obscure a QSL. At the same time, theoretical literature often lacks consensus about the ground states of quantum spins on even the simplest GF magnetic lattices. We summarize experimental evidence that in the impurity-free limit, available GF magnetic materials that do not develop magnetic order at low temperatures would form {\it a quantum singlet liquid}, i.e. a disordered state of non-magnetic excitations. We further confirm the existence of such a singlet liquid and its gapless character with theoretical arguments. Such a liquid manifests itself in the thermodynamics of GF magnets while having no magnetic features at low temperatures.

Abstract: We develop a microscopic characterization of crystalline symmetry anomalies in bosonic lattice systems in (1+1) and (2+1) dimensions with a combination of crystalline and finite unitary internal symmetry. We construct anomaly indices of symmetry operators and derive their cocycle conditions and coboundary ambiguities. For generalized crystalline symmetries combining permutations of onsite Hilbert spaces with quantum cellular automata, anomaly indices take values in $H^3(B\widetilde Γ,U(1)_r)$ and $H^4(B\widetilde Γ,U(1)_r)$ respectively, where $\widetilde Γ$ is the total group of crystalline and internal symmetries, and the coefficient twist $r$ accounts for spatial orientation reversal. When crystalline symmetries act solely by permuting onsite Hilbert spaces, the anomaly classes restrict trivially to the spatial subgroup. These results establish a crystalline equivalence principle for bosonic anomalies at the microscopic level, without assuming a continuum description or emergent Lorentz invariance. We prove that a nontrivial total anomaly index obstructs any symmetric invertible phase. The framework incorporates modulated symmetries where spatial symmetries permute internal symmetries. Our results reproduce LSM and SPT--LSM constraints, with the latter illustrating how spectral-sequence differentials can trivialize candidate LSM anomaly indices.

Abstract: Identifying the microscopic interactions governing a quantum system is essential for developing predictive theories of materials and validating quantum technologies. Despite considerable progress, learning Hamiltonians from realistic experimental data remains a challenge. Motivated by neutron scattering and quantum simulations, we study the problem of learning a quantum Hamiltonian from the dynamical structure factor (DSF), which captures two-point correlation data in quantum magnets. We show that the DSF encodes sufficient information to efficiently reconstruct broad classes of quantum Hamiltonians. Our approach exploits simple relations between low-order spectral moments of the DSF and microscopic couplings, giving rise to three complementary Hamiltonian tomography protocols based on the high-temperature expansion, strong-field polarization, and the fluctuation-dissipation theorem, respectively. The framework we introduce establishes practical and robust Hamiltonian learning from common experimental data, with potentially immediate application to neutron scattering experiments.

Abstract: The Hall effect provides a sensitive probe of electronic correlations in strongly interacting metals, yet its microscopic interpretation beyond quasiparticle transport remains poorly understood. Our results highlight the sensitivity of Hall transport to coherent many-body dynamics and establish a connection between lattice geometry, electronic correlations, and transverse charge response.

It will modify the tricritical universality class leading to elastic tricriticality, characterized by a compressibility that diverges in a universal manner with the correlation length. To test this scenario experimentally, we use single crystals of EuPd$_2$(Si$_{1-x}$Ge$_x$)$_2$, where, depending on $x$ and pressure, magnetic order collapses due to strong valence fluctuations. First, we confirm the presence of a TCP for $x \approx 0.1$ by using complementary probes, in particular, measurements of relative length changes under control of temperature and He-gas pressure. Second, by studying the elastic response, we uncover clear signatures of elastic tricriticality, notably a drastically enhanced compressibility.

Abstract: Nodal-line semimetals provide a natural setting to investigate how the geometry of a nodal manifold affects interaction-driven quantum phase transitions. Here, we study the Hubbard model on a cubic lattice supporting either nodal-point (cubic $π$-flux model) or nodal-line semimetallic states using mean-field theory, determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations, and a weak-coupling renormalization-group (RG) analysis. In both cases, antiferromagnetic order develops only above a finite interaction strength, with the nodal-line system exhibiting a smaller critical coupling due to its enhanced low-energy density of states and markedly stronger finite-size effects. This two-magnon signal decays with a time constant of $28\pm11$ ps, exceeding the previously reported two-magnon dephasing time by more than two orders of magnitude. These results establish long-lived light-driven bimagnon correlations beyond the one-magnon state, akin to the higher-order correlations measured in ultracold gases.

Abstract: Quantized charge pumping in quantum Hall systems is a bulk topological property, typically manifesting on gapless boundaries as spectral flow through chiral edge modes upon flux insertion. When the boundary is anomaly-free, it can be symmetrically gapped by symmetric mass generation (SMG) interactions. However, the effects on quantized charge pumping at an interacting gapped boundary are unclear. We address this using density-matrix renormalization group simulations of a four-leg ladder quasi-(2+1)d realization of the (1+1)d 3-4-5-0 lattice chiral fermion model with six-fermion interactions at one edge. We find gapless modes near the interacting edge for both weak and strong couplings, but the edge can be in a fully gapped SMG phase at intermediate coupling. We also identify spontaneous breaking of the model's $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry above a critical interaction strength. Across all three boundary phases, we find quantized charge pumping with the same pumped charge per flux quantum. The spatial distribution of the pumped charge varies between phases. Besides charge pumping, we study how a single injected fermion propagates under open boundary conditions. In the SMG phase, an interaction-induced flavor-mixing current develops at the endpoints of the gapped edge, which is absent in the weak- and strong-coupling limits. This current reveals a charge-redistribution mechanism unique to the SMG phase. Additionally, we propose an analogy between charge pumping in this model and a topological leptogenesis scenario in the (3+1)d Standard Model. The anomaly-free chiral U(1) symmetries of the 3-4-5-0 model play a role analogous to the baryon-minus-lepton symmetry of the Standard Model. In both settings, charge pumping can be associated with a continuous deformation along a classically defined but quantum-anomalous direction, while a discrete subgroup remains an exact quantum symmetry.

</details>

The gauge anomaly of the ensuing field theory is derived and demonstrated to vanish for prequantisable $ω$. A general anomaly-cancellation mechanism is proposed, which couples the bare model - rewritten as the holonomy of a connection-dependent trivial gerbe on $F_0$ - to a background gerbe over $M$ endowed with a $G$-equivariant structure. The coupling takes the form of the tensor product of the two gerbes and the extended PSM is defined as the holonomy of the product gerbe. An essential generalisation of the theory is then proposed - to shadows $F$ of principaloid bundles $P$ of arbitrary topology, in keeping with the interpretation of the gauging as descent to the orbistack $M//G$. The generalisation uses local product gerbes depending on local connections over trivialisations of $F$. The $G$-equivariant structure is proven to ensure a 2-stacky descent of the local gerbes to a global one over $F$. Its holonomy is taken as the definition of the generalised PSM. A cohomological discussion of obstructions against the descent is provided. Finally, a reformulation of the generalised PSM as a field theory with a topological gauge-symmetry defect is given, in which the $G$-equivariant structure is employed - alongside the transition cocycle of $P$ - as the datum of the defect.

In fact, it is demonstrated that the behavior of particle correlations governs the dynamics of states in quantum systems with an infinite number of particles.

*J. A. Capitán,A. Megías,C. Sardón*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明 Lotka–Volterra 系统存在度量-辛(metriplectic)结构当且仅当满足 Volterra 耗散条件,并由此构造带乘性环境噪声的随机 GENERIC 系统,其平稳分布为独立 Gamma 分布的乘积。文章指出:Gamma 型、不相关的丰度波动并不能证明物种间无相互作用,但相互作用在时间(协方差动态)中可见;此外还证明了任意噪声下的精确 Lyapunov 型恒等式,给出种间截面协方差的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微生物群落中常观察到 Gamma 分布且不相关的丰度波动,这一现象常被(可能错误地)解读为物种之间缺乏相互作用的证据。作者希望澄清:这种看似独立的平稳统计特性,实际上可以与丰富的物种间相互作用(包括捕食-被捕食等非对称相互作用)兼容,从而纠正仅凭稳态分布判断"无相互作用"的误区。

Method: 作者利用度量-辛(metriplectic/GENERIC)形式:以 Volterra 函数 V_C 为能量、环境熵变量,通过对数-典范(log-canonical)Poisson 双向量显式构造结构;当且仅当 Volterra 耗散条件 CA+A^T C≤0 成立时该结构存在。通过随机 GENERIC 的涨落-耗散关系引入乘性环境噪声,协方差由 AC^{-1} 的对称部分确定;利用平稳分布为独立 Gamma 乘积的 Gibbs 测度进行分析。进一步证明精确恒等式 AK+K^T A^T+Q=0(K=Cov(x,log x)),并用一阶求导得到对数丰度协方差的零滞后导数为 A(C/k)^{-1},其反对称部分即 Poisson 部分,承载全部平稳熵产生(闭式给出)。

Result: 平稳分布(独立 Gamma 乘积)仅由平衡点和 C/k 决定:n^2 维开锥中的任意相互作用矩阵,与相应兼容噪声配合,都能产生与无耦合随机 logistic 方程相同的统计规律。Gamma 不相关丰度波动因此不能证明无相互作用。但相互作用在时间上可观测:对数丰度协方差的零滞后导数等于 A(C/k)^{-1},反对称部分对应 Poisson 部分并承载全部平稳熵产生。对一般噪声,精确 Lyapunov 型恒等式 AK+K^T A^T+Q=0 成立,由此得到:种群相互独立当且仅当噪声是度量-辛兼容的;并给出种间截面协方差关于 Q 到兼容族距离的显式下界。捕食-被捕食模型的模拟表明该下界几乎尖锐(达到紧致)。

Conclusion: Lotka–Volterra 系统的度量-辛结构与 Volterra 耗散条件严格等价,据此可构造乘性噪声并得到独立 Gamma 乘积的 Gibbs 平稳测度,即使对非对称(含捕食-被捕食)相互作用也成立。这一结果揭示了"稳态 Gamma 不相关"与"存在物种相互作用"之间的非一一对应关系:丰度平稳统计的简单性不代表系统无相互作用,相互作用信息体现在时间协方差动态和熵产生中。对一般噪声的 Lyapunov 型恒等式提供了种群独立性判据与截面协方差下界,并经捕食-被捕食模拟验证其近尖锐性。

Abstract: We show that a Lotka--Volterra system with an interior equilibrium admits a metriplectic structure, with a Volterra function $V_C$ as energy and an entropy variable for the environment, if and only if Volterra's dissipativity condition $CA+A^{\top}C\le0$ holds, and we construct it explicitly with a log-canonical Poisson bivector. Adding noise through the fluctuation--dissipation relation of stochastic GENERIC yields multiplicative environmental noise, with covariance fixed by the symmetric part of $AC^{-1}$, whose stationary law is an explicit Gibbs measure: a product of independent Gamma distributions. Unlike known Gibbs measures for stochastic Lotka--Volterra systems, this holds for nonsymmetric interactions, predator--prey included. The stationary law determines only the equilibrium and $C/k$: every interaction matrix in an open cone of dimension $n^2$, in particular any Poisson part, produces with its compatible noise the same law as uncoupled stochastic logistic equations. Gamma-distributed, uncorrelated abundance fluctuations, as observed in microbial communities, are therefore no evidence of absence of interactions. The interactions are, however, visible in time: the zero-lag derivative of the log-abundance covariance equals $A(C/k)^{-1}$, and its antisymmetric part is the Poisson part, which carries all the stationary entropy production, computed in closed form. Finally, for general noise we prove an exact Lyapunov-type identity $AK+K^\top A^\top+Q=0$ for $K=\mathrm{Cov}(x,\log x)$ at any stationary solution. It implies that the populations are independent if and only if the noise is metriplectically compatible, and gives an explicit lower bound on interspecies cross-covariances in terms of the distance of $Q$ to the compatible family. Simulations of a predator--prey model show that the bound is nearly sharp.

</details>

Abstract: This is the first of two papers devoted to spatial Fréchet structures on AF algebras and their derivations. Here, we study the simplest example of such a structure: the Fréchet algebra of almost-local compact operators associated with a fixed orthonormal basis. We revisit the characterization of its continuous derivations in terms of infinite matrices and their operator-theoretic implementation on rapidly decreasing vectors. Reformulating this description in the language of 0-chains, borrowed from the theory of quantum many-body systems, we establish a sufficient condition for continuity in terms of spatial growth. Abstract: We construct two-parameter Baxter $\mathbb{Q}$-operators for the transfer matrices of noncompact open $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ BC-type spin chains with general boundary interactions, treating both one-boundary and two-boundary models. Abstract: We give a probabilistic characterization of the ideals of resolvent algebras. Abstract: The two-dimensional one-component plasma is a system of point charges that repel each other logarithmically, in a uniform neutralizing background. This constitutes part of the fluid phase predicted by physicists. As a consequence, we obtain the existence of an infinite-volume limit, extending the Ginibre point process, which we call $\mathrm{Gin}_β$. Abstract: The action of closed bosonic string field theory is non-polynomial, and for more than thirty years a no-go theorem of Sonoda and Zwiebach (1990) has excluded a cubic formulation with a single string field, a symmetric vertex and the canonical propagator. We evade it with a Hubbard--Stratonovich transformation and provide an explicit construction of a cubic closed string field theory at all orders. The transformation introduces an infinite tower of auxiliary $k$-string fields $Σ_k$, entering at order $g_s^{2 k - 2}$, which turn the vertices with four or more legs into kinetic terms and cubic couplings. The action is cubic, at most quadratic in each auxiliary field, and triangular: eliminating the auxiliary fields by Gaussian integrals recovers the original action order by order at tree level, under invertibility and kernel conditions on the auxiliary kinetic operators. The original gauge symmetry lifts to the cubic action with no new parameter, and we argue that the auxiliary fields add no physical state at generic momentum, wherever their kinetic operators are invertible. On the worldsheet, $Σ_2$ is an entangled two-string state: by an operator product expansion, the quartic form reduces to a gluing of two asymmetric cubic vertices dressed by the motion of the local coordinates and defined on states without level matching. We expect this picture to generalize to higher orders, defining a gluing of patches carrying $k$ punctures each. The string vertices, read as products with several outputs, satisfy an extended form of the genus-zero $\mathrm{IBL}_\infty$ relations, including operations with no input or no output, exactly when the string products form a cyclic $L_\infty$ algebra. Finally, we compare our action with the bar--cobar construction of Chiaffrino, Mamade and Zwiebach.

</details>

Abstract: We revisit the regularized on-shell actions of rotating electrically charged AdS black holes of gauged supergravity via holographic renormalization and background subtraction, with a particular emphasis on the frame in which the radial cutoff is placed. For holographic renormalization, the asymptotically global AdS frame, recently employed in seven dimensions, works equally well in five and six dimensions: the renormalized on-shell action of the AdS$_d$ black holes with $S^1\times S^{d-2}$ conformal boundaries satisfies the quantum statistical relation, up to a parameter-independent Casimir term in odd dimensions, and thereby places the holographic comparisons that so far relied on this relation on a first-principles footing. Background subtraction, in contrast, fails to reproduce this result in the same frame; the mismatch, which we identify exactly in terms of the Casimir term and the counterterms, demands a criterion for the cutoff surface yet to be established. For precision holography, our results settle the choice of frame for holographic renormalization with conformally flat boundaries and delineate the ambiguities that remain in background subtraction and in holographic renormalization for boundaries that are not conformally flat.

In the physical setting, the Yang-Baxter equation (YBE), which ensures integrability, follows from the flatness of the Berry connection, which in turn follows from supersymmetry. However, the YBE imposes subtle constraints on the tunneling amplitudes, which we examine here. We construct recursion relations that allow higher collective amplitudes to be obtained from more elementary ones. We observe that different tunneling channels are in fact intertwined, leading to finer cancellations among amplitudes and to wall-crossing phenomena: depending on the parameters, the same amplitude is represented as a sum over different channels. We focus on $\mathsf{Y}(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ with higher-spin and non-evaluation representations, as well as on the fundamental representation of $\mathsf{Y}(\mathfrak{sl}_{n+1})$.

Abstract: We study the one-loop Casimir interaction between two $δ$--$δ'$ plates in the fixed one-soliton Pöschl--Teller background of the sine-Gordon kink. The transverse fluctuation problem is the Gaussian truncation of an $S^2$ sigma model, equivalently an easy-axis antiferromagnet with a domain wall, in which the plates exert no classical force on the wall. For $v\ge2|w|$ the fluctuation operators are non-negative, and strictly stable couplings, $v>2|w|$, also exclude zero modes, so the quadratic field theory is consistent at the fixed sine-Gordon mass. The background-dressed relative determinant factorises exactly into effective, gradient-dependent strengths of the two plates, and the sign of each frequency channel of the interaction energy is fixed by their orientation. Two identical, equally oriented, strictly stable plates switch from attraction near the kink to repulsion away from it under a rigid translation at fixed couplings and separation. The interaction energy and the force conjugate to the separation neither share their sign everywhere nor change sign at the same positions: there are regions of negative energy and repulsive force, so attraction and repulsion must be read from the force. For such plates with $w\neq0$ the force is repulsive at short and attractive at large separation, and the interaction energy has a minimum at a separation that depends on the position of the pair. For $v=1.05$, $w=0.5$ and $d=1.5$ the energy changes sign at $c\simeq-0.44$ and $2.63$, and the force at $c\simeq-0.30$ and $2.00$.

The key ingredient is a pencil of rational maps from the worldsheet to the boundary which exists for generic insertion points, unwinds all insertions at once, and collapses to the covering map when the latter exists.

Abstract: We revisit the bilinear formulation of the intermediate long-wave (ILW) equation by interpreting its principal-value integrals in the framework of Sato hyperfunctions. When the tau-functions have no zeros inside the relevant contours, the conventional source-free ILW equation is recovered. When zeros enter the contours, their residue contributions must instead be retained, leading naturally to a residue-extended ILW system with self-consistent sources determined by the tau-functions themselves. We introduce a residue index that counts the zeros enclosed in each analytic strip and use it to classify the resulting soliton sectors. The conventional elevation-soliton belongs to the zero-residue sector, whereas depression-solitons and higher elevation branches generally carry non-zero residue indices. Exact one- and multisoliton tau-functions are constructed, including mixed elevation--depression configurations with factorised two-body interactions. Their residue indices are additive and remain invariant during collisions, provided that no zero crosses a contour boundary. A Fourier-space analysis shows that the soliton spectra decay exponentially at large wavenumbers, with a decay rate determined by the distance of the nearest complex singularity from the real axis. This rate vanishes as an odd-multiple boundary between adjacent residue sectors is approached, providing a direct spectral signature of the transition between them.

Abstract: We study the analytic structure of scalar one-loop cosmological $n$-gon correlators using an integrand-level reduction and a Baikov representation adapted to linear tube-energy denominators. For the complete box at $d=n=4$, we evaluate an external-energy discontinuity in a collinear kinematic family and find a nonvanishing complete elliptic integral of the second kind after summing all time orderings. We also compare successive tube-energy discontinuities in $d=3$ and $d=4$, and relate selected tube cuts to the cosmological dressing representation. These results illustrate the importance of integration domains in determining the analytic structure beyond local leading singularities.

Abstract: We study the canonical structure of a string-inspired model in which the kinetic term couples fields at times separated by a finite shift $σ$, with a coupling $λ$ to a fixed background. From the boundary term in the variation of the action, we construct a conserved symplectic form and derive the positive-time Poisson bracket. Using the Schwinger--Dyson relation, we show that at positive time separation $s$ the commutator equals $i\hbar$ times this bracket. The canonical normalization $\hbar$ appears as a jump of the commutator expectation value at $s=σ$, which is equal time for shifted variables with a local kinetic term; for $0<s<σ$ the commutator vanishes for every $λ>0$. Hence $λ\to0$ at fixed $σ$ does not reproduce the free commutator on this interval, whereas $σ\to0$ at fixed $λ$ recovers the local commutator at every fixed $s>0$.

Abstract: In this work, we extend the equilibrium-evolution duality between stochastic quantization and Wilsonian renormalization to non-abelian gauge theories in anti-de Sitter space, for which the radial coordinate of AdS plays the role of the stochastic time. We show, to all orders in the fields for bulk theories without explicit radial dependence, that the Langevin distribution at time $t$ is the product of the ultraviolet and infrared radial wavefunctions at the cutoff $ε=t$. We therefore identify this Langevin process as the ground-state (Doob) transform of the radial evolution. By explicit calculation, we also extend the existing dictionary in the stochastic-renormalization duality: the radial component of the bulk field is Zwanziger's stochastic gauge-fixing function; radial gauge is Parisi-Wu quantization; and the bulk gauge $A_z=α^{-1}\partial_iA_i$ is Zwanziger's gauge. We also obtain the expressions for the triple-trace and quadruple-trace couplings of the holographic Wilsonian action at finite cutoff in closed form; in the infrared, they reduce to the known gluon correlators and, at the total-energy pole, to flat-space Yang-Mills amplitudes as expected. Abstract: We extend the Kaluza-Klein reduction procedure to the framework of harmonic superspace and apply it to the construction of off-shell superfield formulations of supersymmetric gauge multiplets. Furthermore, the resulting theories can be reformulated in $4D,\mathcal{N}=1$ superspace, opening the way to gauge-invariant off-shell descriptions of $4D,\mathcal{N}=1$ massive higher-spin theories.

Abstract: An infinite tower of higher-curvature corrections is expected to lead to regular black-hole solutions. We translate this assumption into a monotonicity requirement for deviations of the metric functions from a Schwarzschild black hole. Under these assumptions, conditions can be formulated which trace the increase of ``less singular'' black-hole behavior on and outside of the black-hole horizon within the regime of validity of the effective field theory. These conditions constrain the effective field theory coefficients. To exemplify this construction, we motivate five specific conditions and find a corresponding island of black hole regularity conditions at order eight in the effective field theory. Abstract: Motivated by the conjectural relationship between the IKKT matrix model and type IIB string theory, we classify supersymmetric twists of the matrix model, allowing for both dynamical and kinematical supercharges. This difference is related to a higher central extension of the IIB superalgebra that appears in our treatment of the IKKT model in the BV formalism. First, we show that the swing surface length computes the mutual information between suitably chosen nearly null boundary intervals in a special class of pure CFT states. The gravity interpretation of this result involves scattering processes which take place deep inside of the AdS$_3$ bulk. Second, we reduce the problem of finding an information-theoretic interpretation for swing surfaces to the more general problem of finding an information-theoretic interpretation of black hole inner horizons in holography.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 选取带三次相互作用的共形耦合标量检验场绕复鞍点研究；将波函数系数表示为离散谱和，求和项由爱因斯坦静态宇宙中的对应系数给出（类比平直时空的多胞形程序）；用Srivastava-Daoust多变元超几何函数刻画依赖末超曲面尺寸相位的系数，建立任意格点数的递推关系使求和坍缩为有理函数；对单点、两点Feynman-Witten树图导出差分方程，在有限求和集合上闭合以定义离散运动学流，并构造上三角有理变换矩阵及其路径无关相容条件。

Result: 波函数系数可写成离散谱和且求和项含爱因斯坦静态宇宙对应系数；与平坦FLRW的积分不同，Hartle-Hawking鞍点三球面上离散谱产生求和；系数可用Srivastava-Daoust超几何函数表示并满足递推关系，求和坍缩为有理函数；单点和两点树图满足在有限集合上闭合的差分方程，给出离散运动学流，变换矩阵为上三角有理阵且满足路径无关相容条件；得到二、三、四点宇宙学关联函数，并在晚时共形边界导出四点函数的显式差分方程。

Conclusion: 在Hartle-Hawking复鞍点主导的闭合宇宙背景下，宇宙学关联函数可通过离散谱和与离散运动学流结构来理解，无须显式追踪体时间演化；所得的递推与差分方程表明求和可坍缩为有理函数，变换矩阵上三角且路径无关，为在晚时共形边界计算关联函数（如四点函数）提供了显式工具。

Abstract: The Lorentzian gravitational path integral over homogeneous and isotropic metrics in a closed universe with positive cosmological constant is dominated by complex saddle points, such as the Hartle-Hawking saddles. We ask how to compute cosmological correlators on such complex backgrounds, and how they respond to the shifts of the kinematic variables---the kinematic flow---without tracking the bulk time evolution explicitly. We, specifically, study a conformally coupled scalar test field with a cubic interaction around these saddles. We find that the wave function coefficients can be written as discrete spectral sums with the summands containing the corresponding coefficients in the Einstein static universe, which are obtainable in analogy with the polytopes program for flat spacetime. Whereas the wave function coefficients of the flat FLRW case are given by integrals over shifted kinematics, the discrete spectrum on the three-sphere for Hartle-Hawking saddles produces sums. We identify the coefficients, which depend on the final hypersurface through a phase fixed by its size, with Srivastava-Daoust multi-variable hypergeometric functions. They satisfy recursion relations for any number of sites, under which the sums collapse into rational functions. For the one- and two-site Feynman-Witten tree diagrams, we derive difference equations in the kinematic variables which close on a finite set of sums, giving a discrete kinematic flow. The transformation matrices are upper triangular with rational elements, and satisfy a consistency condition which makes the flow path independent. We then obtain the two-, three- and four-point cosmological correlators. At the late-time conformal boundary, we derive an explicit difference equation for the four-point function.

</details>

Conclusion: 谐波分析与 dressed flat space 关联函数是等价谱表示；由此导出的 Euler 积分型 dressing 规则为 de Sitter 关联函数计算提供了可直接利用平直时空图论工具的途径。

Abstract: The Schwinger-Keldysh formulation of de Sitter correlation functions comes in different guises including harmonic analysis and dressed flat space correlators. Starting from the latter we derive dressing rules for scalar fields of arbitrary mass with auxiliary propagators in the form of Euler integrals that are accessible to flat space diagrammatic tools.

TL;DR: 本文通过子代数包含（subsystem inclusion）的模块化动力学（modular dynamics）框架，研究了量子纠错中子系统能否在补集被擦除时受到保护的问题。作者定义了子系统流（subsystem flow）来度量偏离纠错区间的程度，并引入历史代数、持续核、遍历性等概念刻画模块化轨道的生成与保留性质；解析延拓得到 Petz 恢复映射与正包含父哈密顿量，并证明在 L²-核性条件下父哈密顿量谱离散、耗散半群谱有隙，最后猜想此类谱可定义量子混沌的局域谱测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错的目标是构造编码以保护逻辑信息免受物理子系统擦除；而在局域多体系统（及 QFT 子区域）中，自然地存在处于固定参考态 |Ω⟩ 的子系统包含 N⊂M。作者希望回答：该包含是否容许一个"保参考态的编码"，使 N 在补集擦除下受到保护——这等价于 N 在该态下是否在 M 的模块化动力学下保持不变（模块自治性）。进一步，作者想定量刻画偏离这一纠错区间的程度，并将其与信息论量（相对熵损失）、算子代数结构（历史代数、持续核）以及物理量（父哈密顿量及其谱）联系起来。

Method: 本文采用算子代数与模块化理论（Tomita–Takesaki 理论）方法：(1) 定义子系统流（subsystem flow），其无穷小生成元度量相对熵损失，历史代数与持续核描述模块化轨道生成与保留的可观测量；(2) 将子系统流解析延拓得到 Petz 恢复映射及一族正的"包含父哈密顿量"；(3) 研究这些父哈密顿量演化使 N 离开 M 的性质，并证明在某些情形可生成整个 M∨M′；(4) 引入遍历性、L²-核性等假设，证明父哈密顿量谱离散、由重复 Petz 恢复映射生成的耗散半群以模块化持续核为不动点代数且谱有隙；(5) 提出用父哈密顿量本征值与本征矢定义量子混沌局域谱测度的猜想。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 模块自治性等价于存在参考态保持的纠错码保护 N 免受补集擦除；(2) 子系统流定量刻画偏离自治性的程度并度量相对熵损失；(3) 一般地，父哈密顿量演化可使 N 离开 M，某些情形下可生成整个 M∨M′；(4) 具有公共循环且分离向量的包含可生成一切，因子（如 QFT 嵌套子区域）的标准包含是遍历的；(5) L²-核性蕴含父哈密顿量谱离散，且由 Petz 恢复映射迭代生成的耗散半群谱有隙，其不动点代数为模块化持续核。

Conclusion: 作者建立了一个将量子纠错、模块化动力学与算子代数谱性质相统一的框架：纠错保护能力由模块自治性刻画，偏离程度由子系统流量化，并可用 Petz 恢复映射与父哈密顿量展开动力学分析。遍历性（模块轨道生成一切且不保留任何东西）在常见的因子标准包含（如 QFT 子区域）中普遍成立。L²-核性保证了相关哈密顿量与半群具有离散或有隙的谱结构。作者猜想这些谱本征数据可用于定义量子混沌的局域谱测度，从而将纠错、相对熵与混沌联系起来。

Abstract: In quantum error correction (QEC), the goal is to engineer an encoding that protects logical information against the erasure of a physical subsystem. In local many-body quantum systems, by contrast, nature provides a subsystem inclusion $\mN\subset \mM$ in a fixed reference state $\ketΩ$. Asking whether the inclusion admits a reference-preserving code that protects subsystem $\mN$ against erasure of the complement is equivalent to asking whether $\mN$ is preserved under the modular dynamics of $\mM$ in this state (modular autonomy). We define the subsystem flow, which measures the departure from this error correction regime. Away from autonomy, the infinitesimal flow quantifies relative-entropy loss, while the history algebra and persistent core describe which observables the modular orbit generates and retains. We call an inclusion ergodic when modular orbits generate everything and retain nothing. Analytic continuation of subsystem flow yields Petz recovery maps and a family of positive inclusion parent Hamiltonians. We show that, in general, evolving with these parent Hamiltonians allows $\mN$ to leave $\mM$, and in some cases, it can generate the entire $\mM\vee\mM'$. Inclusions with a common cyclic and separating vector generate everything, and standard inclusions of factors, such as those of nested subregions in QFT, are ergodic. Moreover, the $L^2$-nuclearity property implies that the spectrum of parent Hamiltonians is discrete. We define a dissipative semigroup by repeating the Petz recovery map, whose fixed-point algebra is the modular persistent core and whose spectrum is gapped under the assumption of $L^2$-nuclearity. We conjecture that the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of inclusion parent Hamiltonians can be used to define local spectral measures of quantum chaos.

</details>
