Abstract: Einstein gravity coupled to massless matter exhibits universality in the infrared. In the presence of long-range interactions we show that a novel superrotation charge conservation is equivalent to the massless limit of the classical logarithmic soft graviton theorem, and we propose a formula for the corresponding tail to the displacement memory. These results, which extend earlier work on gravity coupled to massive matter in a qualitatively different way, are exact in the gravitational coupling and reveal that long-range gravitational dressings resum. We also compute higher-order corrections to the known superrotation charge which are exact in the gravitational coupling and would correspond to a 1-loop exact correction to the Cachazo-Strominger subleading soft graviton theorem.

</details>


### [97] [Magnetic-slope crossovers in the QCD bulk-to-shear viscosity ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15929)
*Aalok Misra,Shivam S. Kushwah,Keshav Dasgupta,Charles Gale,Sangyong Jeon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用 M 理论/弦论启发的 QCD 类热等离子体模型与动力学理论恒等式，预测纵向体黏度-剪切黏度比 ζ∥/η∥ 对磁场的斜率在温度轴上改变符号两次（T1<T0<T2），并在多种自下而上的模型中验证了这一模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场如何改变夸克-胶子物质中的耗散（输运性质）几乎无法从第一性原理约束，因为格点模拟无法直接获取实时输运信息。文献中存在关于磁场对体黏度/剪切黏度比斜率符号的相互矛盾的预测，亟需一种不依赖归一化的稳健符号判据。

Method: 采用自上而下的 MQGP 启发的 M 理论/弦论构造来描述 QCD 类温热等离子体；在指定的单模准粒子截断下使用动力学理论恒等式；结合现有格点状态方程数据进行相容性构造，推导出对归一化不敏感的符号预测。进而用具有相适应载流子的动力学理论恒等式（高于 T0 为夸克-胶子等离子体、低于 T0 为手征 π 介子气体）验证，并与格点声速数据、自下而上的 VQCD 模型交叉比对。

Result: 发现纵向体黏度-剪切黏度比 ζ∥/η∥ 的磁场斜率在温度上两次变号：分别位于解禁闭赝临界温度 T0 以下（T1）和以上（T2），即 T1<T0<T2，其中较低温的反转点 T1 依赖于所述 π 介子气体速率不等式是否成立。动力学理论恒等式与格点声速数据均重现了相同模式；VQCD 在解禁闭相支持大温度下的负斜率，但从现有 ζ∥/s、η∥/s 图中无法分辨出斜率交叉点。

Conclusion: 本文给出一个对归一化不敏感的符号预测：ζ∥/η∥ 的磁场斜率在 T0 两侧各发生一次符号反转，并得到动力学理论、格点声速及 VQCD 模型的多重支持，从而为厘清该输运量的温度-磁场依赖行为提供了统一的判据。

Abstract: How a magnetic field alters dissipation in quark--gluon matter is almost unconstrained from first principles, since real-time transport is not directly accessible to lattice simulation. Using a top-down MQGP-inspired M/string-theory construction of a QCD-like thermal plasma, together with a kinetic-theory identity within a specified single-mode quasiparticle truncation and a compatibility construction using available lattice equation-of-state data, we propose a normalization-insensitive sign prediction that the field slope of the longitudinal bulk-to-shear viscosity ratio ζ_\parallel/η_\parallel reverses sign twice with temperature---below and above the deconfinement pseudo-critical temperature T_0, at T_1<T_0<T_2 (the lower reversal T_1 conditional on a stated pion-gas rate inequality). A kinetic-theory identity with phase-appropriate carriers, a quark--gluon plasma above T_0 and a chiral pion gas below, reproduces the same pattern, and lattice speed-of-sound data can be shown to be consistent as well. The bottom-up VQCD in the deconfined phase supports a negative slope for large temperatures; the slope crossover is not resolvable from the available ζ_\parallel/s, η_\parallel/s plots.

</details>


### [98] [Imaging the two-sided black hole interior with four-point correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15933)
*Joydeep Chakravarty*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用波包涂抹的边界四点函数，可在高频下探测双侧AdS黑洞视界内的局部物理，将关联函数因子化为类平直空间的散射振幅，并可用于“成像”黑洞内部结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AdS黑洞视界背后的局部物理一直以来都面临困难，需要构造能够透过视界探测内部时空的边界可观测量。

Method: 作者利用波包涂抹的边界四点函数，采用非平凡的时间编序，并引入一个沿热圆移动半程的态依赖算子插入。在高频极限下，瞄准内部体点的边界波包沿测地线传播并在体点附近局域散射，使关联函数因子化为类平直空间的散射振幅。

Result: 该可观测量能探测内部非平凡态的特征：视界后的零冲击波和物质壳层表现为当体点穿越它们时观测信号的跳变，从而实现对黑洞内部的成像。

Conclusion: 该方法为研究AdS黑洞内部的局部物理提供了新工具，能通过边界观测信号的变化揭示黑洞内部结构与动力学。

Abstract: We propose a boundary four-point function smeared with wavepackets, which probes local physics behind the horizon of a two-sided AdS black hole. At high frequencies, boundary wavepackets aimed at an interior bulk point travel along geodesics and scatter locally about the bulk point, factorizing the correlator into a flat-space like scattering amplitude. To obtain this configuration, we utilize a non-trivial time ordering of operators, and also use one state-dependent operator insertion moved halfway across the thermal circle. An interesting property of this observable is that it also probes features of nontrivial states: null shocks and matter shells behind the horizon show up as jumps in the observed signal as the bulk point crosses them, thereby imaging the black hole interior.

</details>


### [99] [Self-Force and Bremsstrahlung in Finite Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15993)
*Kyungsun Lee,Piljin Yi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究非线性电动力学（以Born–Infeld理论为原型）中运动点电荷的自身力与伴随的轫致辐射，独立计算局部自身力与全局辐射耗散并证明二者一致，并讨论文献中自身力的两种不同表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Maxwell理论中点电荷自身力已被广泛研究，但非线性电动力学（具有有限自场与有限自能）中的自身力问题尚未充分探讨。作者希望在Born–Infeld这一非线性框架下重新审视该问题，以揭示非线性对辐射及自相互作用的影响。

Method: 以Born–Infeld理论为原型，回顾Coleman对Maxwell理论的论述；分别独立计算运动的点电荷所受的局部自身力与全局辐射耗散（轫致辐射）；证明两者相互一致；利用运动电荷的有效多极矩刻画非线性残留在渐进辐射场中的效应；附录进一步推广到Born–Infeld ModMax理论。

Result: 尽管非线性在渐进辐射场区域趋于消失，其残余效应仍通过运动电荷的有效多极矩得以保留，从而导致与Maxwell理论的偏差；局部自身力与全局辐射耗散的计算结果相互一致。

Conclusion: 在Born–Infeld非线性电动力学中，运动点电荷的自身力与辐射耗散可以自洽地统一描述，非线性的残余影响通过有效多极矩体现；文中还系统讨论了文献中自身力两种不同表现及其在计算中的体现，并将结论推广至Born–Infeld ModMax。

Abstract: We study the self-force and the accompanying Bremsstrahlung for a moving point charge in nonlinear electrodynamics with finite self-field and self-energy. The self-force in the Maxwell theory has been the subject of many studies, often by renowned physicists. After reviewing Coleman's lucid treatise for Maxwell among them, we wish to explore the same question in the Born--Infeld theory, our prototype. We independently compute the local self-force and the global radiative dissipation, and show how the two are mutually consistent. Despite the nonlinearity turning off for the asymptotic radiative field, its residual effect survives via the effective multipole moments of a moving charge, allowing deviation from Maxwell. We close with a detailed discussion about two different manifestations of the self-force in the literature and how these enter our computation. One of the appendices explores Born--Infeld ModMax as a generalization.

</details>


### [100] [Silent Heavy Fields: An Exclusion Rule for the Cosmological Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16029)
*Shuntaro Aoki,Zhehan Qin,Masahide Yamaguchi,Yuhang Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一条“排除规则”，指出当de Sitter不变的heavy sector只通过单顶点与单个外场共变耦合时，宇宙对撞机信号（振荡特征）会消失；该规则先经谱分解加场重定义对两点函数交换图证明到任意圈阶，再推广至单圈多边形图，并要求重新审视此前归入此类的近似信号结论，同时以化学势启发的玩具模型首次给出三角形圈图的主导非零信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙对撞机依赖原初关联函数中的振荡特征探测暴涨期间的heavy field，但该方法存在盲点——heavy field可以在时间上演化却不在关联函数中留下信号。作者旨在厘清并系统刻画这些信号消失的机制，为准确判定哪些过程能产生可观测的非零信号提供判据。

Method: 作者首先考虑de Sitter不变的heavy sector以共变方式在单个顶点耦合到单个外场的两类图。第一类：由heavy sector两点函数介导的交换图，作者利用谱分解将heavy sector两点函数展开，再通过场的重定义证明其振荡信号相消，该证明对任意圈阶成立；第二类：单圈多边形图，作者将其振荡贡献与第一类进行匹配，从而把排除规则推广过去。作为反例验证，作者在由化学势启发的玩具模型中首次计算三角形圈图的主导振荡信号。

Result: 证明了一条明确的排除规则：当heavy sector为de Sitter不变且与单个外场仅通过单顶点共变耦合时，上述两类图的宇宙对撞机振荡信号为零。由此，此前被归入这些类别并给出的“近似信号”结论需要重新审视。作为规则之外的非零例子，化学势动机玩具模型中的三角形圈图给出了主导非零振荡信号。

Conclusion: 宇宙对撞机存在系统性盲点，且可由一条清晰的排除规则刻画：de Sitter不变的heavy sector经单顶点共变耦合到单外场时，特定传播子交换图与单圈多边形图的振荡信号都会消失。该结果对既有近似信号结论提出修正要求，并通过对三角形圈图非零信号的首次推导展示了排除边界，深化了对宇宙对撞机探测能力与局限的理解。

Abstract: The cosmological collider uses oscillatory signatures in primordial correlators to probe heavy fields during inflation. This probe has blind spots, as heavy fields can keep ticking in time while remaining silent in the correlators. In this letter, we derive an exclusion rule under which cosmological collider signals vanish. The rule covers two classes of diagrams where a de Sitter invariant heavy sector couples covariantly to a single external field at one vertex. We first prove it for exchanges mediated by the two-point function of the heavy sector to all loop orders, using a spectral decomposition followed by a field redefinition. The result then extends to one-loop polygons by matching their oscillatory contributions onto those of the first class. The approximate signals of previously studied processes that fall into these classes must therefore be revisited. As a nonvanishing example, we derive the leading signal of a triangle loop for the first time in a toy model motivated by chemical potentials.

</details>


### [101] [Anti-periodic Gravitino and Dirac Effective Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16047)
*Karim Benakli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在N=1超空间中用有效算符描述引力微子（gravitino）的狄拉克质量，并通过五维最小超引力在S¹/Z₂上紧致化、配合Scherk-Schwarz扭转，验证该算符与Kaluza-Klein物理狄拉克质量谱的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引入保持的U(1)_R对称性会禁止引力微子的Majorana质量，却允许狄拉克质量。作者希望为引力微子的狄拉克质量找到N=1超空间中的有效算符描述，类比于已知的狄拉克规范微子（gaugino）质量，从而在超对称框架下系统处理这类质量项。

Method: 作者构造自旋3/2双线性算符，将其表达为规范不变的引力微子曲率超场（curvature superfields）通过一个含逆d'Alembertian的非局域核耦合的形式。在最小五维超引力紧致化于S¹/Z₂的背景下，引力微子满足由Scherk-Schwarz扭转诱导的反周期边界条件。物理Kaluza-Klein狄拉克质量表现为与两端点局域源相关联的曲率超场两点函数中的极点。通过分离物理模式与规范模式，将这些传播子联系到一个由16×16矩阵表示的二次费米子核，其矩阵元与N=1超空间中两个不变量的费米子分量（包括旋量收缩与归一化）完全匹配，且该匹配在具有非零radion辅助场的固定背景上完成。

Result: 自旋3/2双线性算符可用规范不变的引力微子曲率超场通过含逆d'Alembertian的非局域核表达；在S¹/Z₂紧致化的最小五维超引力中，KK狄拉克质量作为曲率超场两点函数的极点出现；16×16矩阵的元素与两个N=1超空间不变量的费米子分量（含旋量收缩和归一化）在非零radion辅助场背景下精确匹配。

Conclusion: 引力微子的狄拉克质量可以类比狄拉克规范微子质量，在N=1超空间中用有效算符描述，其核心是曲率超场经非局域核的耦合。五维最小超引力紧致化模型验证了这一构造：物理KK质量谱、传播子极点、16×16费米子核与超空间不变量的匹配相互自洽，表明该有效算符描述是可靠且普适的。

Abstract: Introducing a preserved $U(1)_R$ symmetry forbids gravitini Majorana masses while allowing a Dirac mass. We investigate how a Dirac mass for gravitini can be described by an effective operator in $N=1$ superspace, in analogy with the well-known Dirac gaugino mass. We find that the corresponding spin-$3/2$ bilinear operator can be expressed in terms of gauge-invariant gravitino curvature superfields coupled through a nonlocal kernel involving an inverse d'Alembertian. We study this construction in minimal five-dimensional supergravity compactified on $S^1/\mathbb Z_2$. The gravitino satisfies anti-periodic boundary conditions induced by a Scherk-Schwarz twist. The physical Kaluza-Klein Dirac masses then appear as poles in the two-point functions of the curvature superfields associated with sources localized at the endpoints. Separation of physical and gauge modes relates these propagators to a quadratic fermionic kernel represented by a $16\times16$ matrix. Its elements match the fermionic components of two invariants written in $N=1$ superspace, including their spinor contractions and normalization. This matching is carried out on a fixed background with a nonzero radion auxiliary field.

</details>


### [102] [Holographic Counterterms from Restricted Weyl Symmetry in Even-Dimensional Einstein-AdS Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16187)
*Giorgos Anastasiou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于无穷小体Weyl变换的对称性方法，在任意偶数维建立了Einstein-AdS引力的体协变重整化方案：通过要求Einstein-Hilbert密度的受限Weyl变分（参数为Σ(X)）在移除径向截断后积分有限，诱导出固定耦合的欧拉密度与协变全导数层级，并得到以平移拉普拉斯算子表示的因子化解，从而给出任意偶数维的闭合形式补全，证明了Euclidean AlAdS Einstein几何的壳上有限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为偶数维Einstein-AdS引力构造一种基于渐近保持场方程的无穷小体Weyl变换的对称性重整化方法，以避免依赖特定维度手工构造，并统一给出任意偶数维的共形重整化作用量。

Method: 将Einstein-Hilbert密度在受限Weyl变换（参数Σ(X)）下的变分要求为零（模去径向截断移除后积分仍有限的项），由此导出两类补偿项：欧拉密度与一串协变全导数的层级。所得递推关系允许以平移拉普拉斯算子因子化，得到任意偶数维的闭合形式解。在Einstein背景上与ambient构造的共形不变量（规范化匹配后）一致；配套的重整化体积恒等式保证了壳上有限性。

Result: 该处方在四、六、八维重现已知的共形重整化作用量，并给出十维的显式补全（涉及拉普拉斯算子直到□³的幂）。应用于八维与十维、具有非共形平坦Einstein视界的AdS黑洞，得到与全息重整化一致的有限壳上作用量。

Conclusion: 基于渐近Weyl对称性的方法为任意偶数维Einstein-AdS引力提供了系统、体协变且闭合形式的重整化框架，消除了逐维手工作图，并与ambient构造及全息重整化结果相互印证。

Abstract: We develop a symmetry-based approach to renormalization for even-dimensional Einstein-AdS gravity based on infinitesimal bulk Weyl transformations that preserve its field equations asymptotically. We determine a bulk-covariant completion of the Einstein-Hilbert density by requiring its variation under restricted Weyl transformations with parameter $Σ(X)$ to vanish modulo terms whose integrals remain finite as the radial cutoff is removed. This requirement induces two types of compensating terms with fixed couplings: the Euler density and a hierarchy of covariant total derivatives. The resulting recursive relations admit a factorized solution in terms of shifted Laplacians, yielding a closed-form completion in arbitrary even bulk dimensions. On Einstein backgrounds, these expressions agree, after matching normalizations, with those obtained through the ambient construction of conformal invariants. The associated renormalized-volume identity establishes on-shell finiteness for Euclidean AlAdS Einstein geometries in arbitrary even dimensions. The prescription reproduces the known conformally renormalized actions in four, six and eight dimensions and provides an explicit ten-dimensional completion involving powers of the Laplacian up to $\Box^3$. Applications to eight- and ten-dimensional AdS black holes with non-conformally-flat Einstein horizons yield finite on-shell actions in agreement with Holographic Renormalization.

</details>


### [103] [Discrete anomaly inflow from SymTFT of $U(1)$ global symmetry: the modified Villain resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16696)
*Napat Poovuttikul,K. Sapmee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在改进 Villain 格点规范理论框架下，为带反常系数 k 的 U(1) 全局对称性构造了格点 SymTFT（对称性拓扑场论），并利用格点 Majorana 费米子显式实现奇数 k 所需的自旋结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从连续对称性的反常流入出发，推导超越群上同调的全局反常的线-结（junction）数据，为理解带反常的连续对称性的拓扑性质提供格点实现。

Method: 采用改进 Villain 表述构造 U(1) 全局对称性的格点 SymTFT；用格点 Majorana 费米子显式实现奇数 k 的自旋结构；从线的格点代数推导体（bulk）与有隙边界（gapped boundary）上的 R 符号与 F 符号；通过限制到 Z2 ⊂ U(1) 子群，利用映射环面（mapping torus）复现模 4 反常相位与费米子堆叠规则。

Result: 证明 Majorana 费米子恰好位于对称线的交汇处，即两个 U(1) 元素的融合绕圆周一圈的位置；推导出体内与边界上的 R 符号和 F 符号；证明奇数 k 时边界 F 符号满足 U(1) 对称缺陷的 Gu-Wen 关系，无需借助玻色子影子（bosonic shadow）；限制到 Z2 子群时复现模 4 反常相位与费米子堆叠规则；确定可被规范化的子群 Zd ⊂ U(1) 及所得理论之对称性。

Conclusion: 该工作为从连续对称性的反常流入推导超群上同调的全局反常线-结数据迈出第一步，并展示该 SymTFT 可用于判定哪些子群可被规范化及其对应理论对称性。

Abstract: We construct a lattice SymTFT for a $U(1)$ global symmetry with anomaly coefficient $k$ using the modified Villain formulation, in which the spin structure required for odd $k$ is realised explicitly by lattice Majorana fermions. We demonstrate that the same Majorana fermions sit at the junctions of the symmetry lines, exactly where the fusion of two $U(1)$ elements wraps around the circle. From the lattice algebra of lines and junctions we derive the $R$- and $F$-symbols in the bulk and on the gapped boundary, and show that for odd $k$ the boundary $F$-symbol obeys the Gu-Wen relation for the $U(1)$ symmetry defect, without passing through a bosonic shadow. Restricting to $\mathbb{Z}_2\subset U(1)$, we reproduce the mod-4 anomalous phase from a mapping torus and the fermionic stacking rule. As a simple application of this SymTFT, we also determine which subgroups $\mathbb{Z}_d\subset U(1)$ can be gauged and the symmetry of the resulting theories. This is a first step towards deriving, from the anomaly inflow of a continuous symmetry, the junction data of global anomalies beyond group cohomology.

</details>


### [104] [Multiparticle residues of open-string tachyon amplitudes from DDF sewing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16280)
*Dripto Biswas,Qu Cao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过缝合三次 DDF 顶点并求和所有中间态，计算临界开玻色弦彩色排序 tachyon 圆盘振幅的最大同时留数；每个内线能级给出有限模截断，留数由行列式前因子乘以源二次型的指数经系数提取得到，并在四点、五点、六点情形进行了检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法处理多点弦振幅的极点留数时，需要对物理中间态求和且涉及无限级，缺乏一个统一、显式且有限化的构造。作者希望借助 DDF 顶点与缝合操作，将留数计算约化为纯 Gaussian 收缩，从而系统化地得到任意平面三次树的留数公式。

Method: 采用 Del Giudice–Di Vecchia–Fubini（DDF）顶点的缝合（sewing）构造。将任意平面三次树分解为三次顶点并在内线上求和全部物理中间态；每个内线能级确定精确的有限模截断；在正确缝合约定下，局部坐标标度在顶点与缝合度规间相消。留数由行列式前因子乘以源中二次型的指数，并对缝合变量做系数提取。四点情形用三次局部映射与单玻色子应力张量 Ward 恒等式做全阶重求和恢复 Veneziano 极点系数；五点双留数、六点 comb 与雪花图给出显式矩阵，并用独立 Koba–Nielsen 展开在低阶检验。最后建立算符、坐标映射与测度恒等式，联系到 Grunsky 算符与高阶 Koba–Nielsen 顶形式。

Result: 留数公式为行列式（即横模玻色子的真空缝合因子）乘以源二次型指数，再经缝合变量系数提取；四点结果恢复到 Veneziano 极点系数；五点和六点结果与独立 Koba–Nielsen 展开低阶一致。

Conclusion: 该构造为树级开玻色弦 tachyon 圆盘振幅的留数提供了一套通用的 Gaussian/行列式表示，并将之与 Grunsky 算符及高阶 Koba–Nielsen 顶形式联系起来，具有推广到更高点的潜力。

Abstract: We compute the maximal simultaneous residues of color-ordered tachyon disk amplitudes in the critical open bosonic string by summing all physical intermediate states. Sewing cubic Del Giudice--Di Vecchia--Fubini (DDF) vertices reduces this sum to Gaussian contractions for any planar cubic tree, with the internal levels specified independently. Each internal level fixes an exact finite mode cutoff. The residue then follows by coefficient extraction in the sewing variables from a determinant prefactor times the exponential of a quadratic form in the sources. With consistent sewing conventions, the local-coordinate scales cancel between the vertices and the sewing metric. At four points, cubic local maps and a one-boson stress-tensor Ward identity give an all-order resummation that recovers the Veneziano pole coefficients. The same construction gives explicit matrices for the five-point double residue and the six-point comb and snowflake, with low-level checks against independent Koba--Nielsen expansions. The determinant is the vacuum sewing factor of the transverse bosons. We formulate the operator, coordinate-map and measure identities needed to relate this construction to Grunsky operators and the full higher-point Koba--Nielsen top forms.

</details>


### [105] [De Sitter Gravity and Emergent Time from Fusion Matrix Product States and SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17045)
*Shinsei Ryu,Yu-ki Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一个与二维德西特(dS)引力对偶的量子力学模型，即一个由矩阵乘积态定义的量子信道，通过逐步融合自旋实现，并与无弦交叉的Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev模型极限相关。其最简部分是一个具有涌现SL(2,R)对称性的随机游走，该对称性对应dS未来边界的对称性，并选定一个时间变量使游走服从Schwarzian作用量，给出dS Jackiw-Teitelboim引力的圆盘振幅。作为检验，作者计算了固定dS₂背景中自由无质量标量场在Bunch-Davies真空下的两点函数，得到一致结果，从而为二维dS引力的dS/CFT对应提供了一个具体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dS/CFT对应的一个主要难点在于缺乏一个明确、可控的微观（量子力学/全息）模型来描述德西特引力，尤其是二维情形。作者旨在提供一个具体的量子力学对偶模型，通过量子信道与矩阵乘积态的结构，连接dS₂引力及其边界对称性，从而为dS/CFT对应建立可计算的微观基础。

Method: 作者构造一个由矩阵乘积态定义的量子信道，每一步融合一个自旋，并将其与无弦交叉的Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）模型的极限相联系。模型最简部分表现为一个随机游走，具有涌现的SL(2,R)对称性，该对称性被认定为dS未来边界的对称性；该结构选定一个时间变量，使随机游走服从Schwarzian作用量，进而得到dS Jackiw-Teitelboim（JT）引力的圆盘振幅。为验证模型，作者在固定dS₂背景下考虑自由无质量标量场，计算其在Bunch-Davies真空中的两点函数，并与模型预言进行比较。

Result: 该模型成功给出dS JT引力的圆盘振幅，并识别出与dS未来边界对应的SL(2,R)对称性。在dS₂固定背景中，自由无质量标量场在Bunch-Davies真空下的两点函数与模型计算结果一致，对称性匹配也得到确认。

Conclusion: 该构造为二维德西特引力的dS/CFT对应提供了一个具体的量子力学模型：量子信道/矩阵乘积态框架自然地产生dS JT引力的圆盘振幅及其边界SL(2,R)对称性，并通过自由标量场的两点函数验证了对应关系的正确性。

Abstract: We propose a quantum-mechanical model dual to two-dimensional de Sitter (dS) gravity. It is a quantum channel, defined by a matrix product state that fuses one spin at each step, and is related to a limit of the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model without chord crossings. Its simplest part is a random walk with an emergent $SL(2,\mathbb R)$ symmetry, which we identify with the symmetry of the future boundary of dS. It selects a time variable in which the walk obeys the Schwarzian action and yields the disk amplitude of dS Jackiw-Teitelboim gravity. As the simplest case, we consider free massless scalar fields in the fixed background of dS$_2$. We find the agreement of the two-point function in the Bunch-Davies vacuum and the matching of symmetry. Our construction provides a concrete model for the dS/CFT correspondence for two-dimensional de Sitter gravity.

</details>


### [106] [Thermodynamics within and across AdS Gauss--Bonnet holographic CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16364)
*Limin Zeng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究五维AdS高斯-博内引力中，单一全息共形场论内部及不同反常系数理论之间的热竞争。作者推导了与无量纲耦合共轭的功项，并用共同边界时钟比较固定外源下的带电球形鞍点，发现改变外源会驱动端点CFT之间的一级切换。全局比较可约化为耦合区间的两个端点，并得到推广的克拉佩龙关系。进一步确定了中性霍金-佩奇线和带电小-大黑洞共存面，临界电荷在有限耦合处趋于零，熵隙以1/2指数关闭，热容在临界电荷的温度扫描中以2/3指数发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单个全息CFT内部以及具有不同反常系数（对应不同高斯-博内耦合）的CFT之间的热力学竞争，确定在何种外部控制参数下哪一支理论/相在热力学上占优，并刻画跨理论的相变与临界行为。

Method: 在五维AdS高斯-博内引力框架下，(1) 由边界第一定律推导出与无量纲耦合共轭的功项；(2) 采用共同边界时钟（单位流逝圆柱钟与零型真空能）比较固定外源下的带电球形鞍点；(3) 利用固定半径处的严格凹性将全局比较约化为耦合区间端点；(4) 通过推广的克拉佩龙关系联系共存线斜率与熵差；(5) 确定中性霍金-佩奇线和带电小-大黑洞共存面并分析临界行为。

Result: 改变外源会驱动端点CFT之间的一级切换；优选耦合沿共存线跳跃，共存线斜率与熵差由推广的克拉佩龙关系联系。当爱因斯坦端点自身经历小-大共存时，出现第三鞍点并在跨理论边界形成拐折；外控制参数的补偿性变化在有限能量移动下保持鞍点排序不变。在单个CFT内确定了中性霍金-佩奇线与带电小-大黑洞共存面；临界电荷在有限耦合处趋于零；熵隙以1/2指数关闭，热容沿临界电荷的温度扫描以2/3指数发散。

Conclusion: 在五维AdS高斯-博内系统中，跨CFT的热竞争可化约为耦合区间端点的比较，并由推广的克拉佩龙关系刻画；临界电荷的消失、熵隙关闭指数（1/2）与热容发散指数（2/3）揭示了该高阶曲率引力下的普适临界行为，同时外控制参数的补偿变化保持鞍点排序不变，深化了对全息CFT相结构及其跨理论竞争的理解。

Abstract: We study thermal competition within individual holographic conformal field theories (CFTs) and across theories with different anomaly coefficients in five-dimensional AdS Gauss--Bonnet gravity. We derive the work term conjugate to the dimensionless coupling from the boundary first law and use a common boundary clock to compare charged spherical saddles at fixed external source. Varying this source drives a first-order switch between the CFTs at the endpoints of the chosen coupling interval. In the unit-lapse cylinder clock with the zero-type-D vacuum energy, strict concavity at fixed radius reduces the global comparison to these two endpoints. The preferred coupling jumps across the coexistence line, whose slope is related to the entropy difference by a generalized Clapeyron relation. Where the Einstein endpoint itself undergoes small--large coexistence, a third saddle joins the competition and produces a kink in the cross-theory boundary. A compensating change of the external control preserves saddle ordering under finite energy shifts. Within fixed CFTs, we determine the neutral Hawking--Page line and the charged small--large black hole coexistence surface. The critical charge tends to zero at a finite coupling. The entropy gap closes with exponent one half, and the heat capacity diverges with exponent two thirds along a temperature sweep at the critical charge.

</details>


### [107] [Matching lattice and continuum chiral determinants at finite volume](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16544)
*Zhengwen Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于环面四维上的一个SU(5)代格点手征规范理论，基于Craig规范不变overlap测度的复数费米子权重与基于Hsu-Witten重构联络上连续Weyl行列式（相位由五维η不变量固定）的权重，在光滑带隙路径的连续极限下一致；并给出对数变差的显式一致收敛速率，以及相位和模在不同背景下的匹配/修正行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点手征规范理论中复数费米子权重的定义存在两种主要方案（Craig的规范不变overlap测度与Hsu-Witten的重构联络Weyl行列式），需要证明两者在连续极限下彼此等价，以解决规范手征性在格点上的实现与权重相位定义的歧义问题。

Method: 在平坦四环面上取一个SU(5)代，沿光滑带隙路径比较两种复数权重的相对权重；通过固定归一化并减去局域Yang-Mills抵消项（确定其系数），在光滑有界族上利用均匀Dirac带隙（无需真空展开收敛）证明匹配；对对数变差导出显式一致收敛速率，并对Craig插值证明重构界、将Hsu的小场比较推广到有限振幅；对Hsu带平直套环的反射正重构分析相位与二次抵消项对数系数修正，并在超荷背景下进行数值比较。

Result: 两种相对权重在连续极限下沿光滑带隙路径一致（固定归一化并减除局域YM抵消项后）。光滑有界族下仅需均匀Dirac带隙即可匹配，无需真空展开收敛。得到对数变差的显式一致收敛速率；Craig插值的重构界成立，小场比较推广至有限振幅。Hsu反射正重构下相位变差仍匹配，二次抵消项的对数系数被一个可计算、依赖剖面的因子增强。超荷背景下真空展开发散时：输入场的数值比较支持相位二次收敛；模量在二次标度上出现对数修正，其系数可由自由overlap真空极化解析给出。

Conclusion: 在连续极限下，Craig方案与Hsu-Witten方案的复数费米子权重在适当的归一化和局域抵消项处理后等价，从而为格点手征规范理论的权重定义提供了自洽的统一描述；同时厘清了不同重构（尤其反射正重构）下相位与模量的修正结构及收敛速率。

Abstract: Recent formulations of lattice chiral gauge theories define a complex fermion weight either through Craig's gauge-invariant overlap measure or, following Hsu and Witten, through a continuum Weyl determinant on a reconstructed gauge connection, with its phase fixed by the five-dimensional $η$-invariant. For one $SU(5)$ generation on a flat four-torus, we prove that their relative weights agree in the continuum limit along smooth gapped paths, after fixing a normalization and subtracting a local Yang-Mills counterterm, whose coefficient we determine. On bounded smooth families a uniform Dirac gap suffices: the vacuum expansion need not converge. We obtain explicit uniform convergence rates for the logarithmic variations and, for Craig's interpolation, prove the reconstruction bounds that the matching requires, extending Hsu's small-field comparison to finite amplitude. For Hsu's reflection-positive reconstruction with flat collars, the phase variations still match, while the logarithmic coefficient of the quadratic counterterm is enhanced by a calculable, profile-dependent factor. In a hypercharge background on which the vacuum expansion diverges, numerical comparisons at the input gauge field support quadratic convergence of the phase, whereas the modulus acquires a logarithmic correction to quadratic scaling, whose coefficient follows analytically from the free overlap vacuum polarization.

</details>


### [108] [Accelerating solitons in Rindler space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16568)
*Huiquan Li,Jianyong Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 二维闵可夫斯基时空中可积的相对论非线性场论在Rindler空间中仍保持可积。本文研究经典sine-Gordon孤子在Rindler空间中的性质，发现加速观察者只能看到完整孤子的一部分，该部分本身构成携带部分拓扑荷的孤子，并可解释为从视界入射、减速停止后回落并加速到光速的运动过程，能量可流入或流出Rindler视界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨相对论非线性可积场论在非惯性参考系（Rindler空间）中的行为，研究加速观察者视角下sine-Gordon孤子的性质及其与Rindler视界的能量交换机制。

Method: 利用二维闵可夫斯基时空中可积相对论非线性场论在Rindler空间中仍可积的性质，对经典sine-Gordon方程进行变换与求解，分析每个Rindler楔形中加速观察者所能观测到的孤子部分及其拓扑荷。

Result: 加速观察者只能看到完整孤子的一部分，该部分本身构成携带部分拓扑荷的孤子。孤子解可解释为：从视界入射的孤子减速并在某点停止，随后回落并朝视界加速至光速。能量可在Rindler视界流入和流出，高能孤子从视界获得能量，而低能孤子则损失能量。

Conclusion: 可积相对论非线性场论在Rindler空间中保持可积性，sine-Gordon孤子展现出与Rindler视界相关的部分拓扑荷行为和能量交换现象，揭示了加速参考系中孤子动力学的独特性质。

Abstract: Relativistic nonlinear field theories that are integrable in two-dimensional Minkowski spacetime remain integrable in Rindler space. We investigate the properties of classical sine-Gordon (SG) solitons in Rindler space. Accelerating observers in each of the two Rindler wedges can see only a portion of the full soliton in Minkowski spacetime; this portion itself forms a soliton carrying a partial topological charge. The soliton solution admits the following interpretation: a soliton incoming from the horizon decelerates to stop at some point, then falls back and accelerates to the speed of light toward the horizon. Energy can flow into and out of the Rindler horizon. The higher energy soliton gains energy from the horizon while the lower one loses energy.

</details>


### [109] [Is Spin Slow in Hot QCD?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16684)
*Zi-Hao Liu,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“热QCD中自旋是慢弛豫流体力学变量”的观点，证明轻夸克自旋其实以输运速率 g^4T ln(1/g) 快速阻尼，与矢量流几乎一样快，因此在夸克-胶子等离子体中不存在慢自旋模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自旋提升为流体力学变量通常需要其弛豫足够慢，热QCD中手征性翻转过程被质量压低这一事实曾让人预期自旋是慢变量。作者希望检验这一预期，并厘清自旋在夸克-胶子等离子体中的真实弛豫行为。

Method: 作者使用完整的leading-order碰撞算符分析轻夸克自旋的阻尼。关键在于空间自旋与动量方向锁定，因此即使不翻转手征性、保持螺旋度的软小角度散射也能轻易转动自旋方向。分析表明自旋阻尼涉及多个动理学模式，而非单一弛豫时间；全局转动只贡献可逆进动（避免混淆）。

Result: 轻夸克自旋以输运速率 g^4T ln(1/g) 阻尼，几乎与矢量流一样快，远快于质量压低的手征性翻转所暗示的慢弛豫。自旋阻尼由多个动理学模式共同决定，不存在单一弛豫时间描述。

Conclusion: 自旋在夸克-胶子等离子体中是快阻尼的自由度，耦合越强越不利于慢自旋模式的存在，因此不应被当作慢流体力学变量来处理。

Abstract: Promoting spin to a hydrodynamic variable requires slow relaxation, as mass-suppressed helicity flips in hot QCD suggest. Using the full leading-order collision operator, we show instead that light-quark spin damps at the transport rate $g^4T\ln(1/g)$, nearly as fast as vector currents. Since spatial spin locks to momentum, helicity-conserving soft, small-angle scatterings readily rotate it. Damping involves multiple kinetic modes beyond a single relaxation time, global rotation adds only reversible precession, and stronger coupling further disfavors a slow spin mode in quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [110] [Gauge Theory Bootstrap with varying $N_c$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16824)
*Rafael Cordoba,Yifei He,Martin Kruczenski,Derek Ping,Jignesh Mohanty,Emil Alegria*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用规范理论 Bootstrap 方法研究具有不同颜色数 $N_c$ 的渐近自由规范理论的低能强耦合动力学,通过将 $N_c$ 从求和规则与渐近形状因子中直接调参,提取散射长度、有效力程、低能常数 $\bar{l}_i$ 及共振态($σ$、$ρ$、$f_2$)的 $N_c$ 依赖关系,检验规范理论 Bootstrap 的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究渐近自由规范理论中微观参数(颜色数 $N_c$)如何影响低能强耦合动力学,以进一步检验 Gauge Theory Bootstrap 方法的可靠性。由于 $N_c$ 通过求和规则和渐近形状因子进入计算,该方法允许直接调节该微观参数,从而系统考察其在 $3\le N_c\le 12$ 范围内的低能物理行为。

Method: 采用 Gauge Theory Bootstrap(GTB)方法;将颜色数 $N_c$ 作为可调参数嵌入求和规则与渐近形状因子;在 $3\le N_c\le 12$ 范围内提取散射长度、有效力程和低能常数 $\bar{l}_i$,并确定 $σ$、$ρ$、$f_2$ 三个共振态的质量与宽度。

Result: 在 $3\le N_c\le 12$ 范围内得到:散射长度、有效力程和低能常数 $\bar{l}_i$ 的 $N_c$ 依赖关系;$ρ$ 和 $f_2$ 质量对 $N_c$ 依赖较弱,且其宽度随 $N_c$ 增大而减小,与常规 $q\bar{q}$ 介子的 $1/N_c$ 标度一致;$σ$ 介子的质量与宽度均随 $N_c$ 增大而增大,与先前的幺正化手征微扰论($χ$PT)分析相符。

Conclusion: 这些结果进一步证明了 Gauge Theory Bootstrap 是研究渐近自由规范理论的有效工具。$ρ$、$f_2$ 与 $σ$ 共振态呈现的不同 $N_c$ 行为分别符合 $q\bar{q}$ 介子图像与幺正化 $χ$PT 的预期,为该方法在调节微观参数、探索低能强耦合动力学方面的能力提供了新的检验。

Abstract: We use the Gauge Theory Bootstrap to study asymptotically free gauge theories with a varying number of colors $N_c$. The parameter $N_c$ enters the computation through the sum rules and asymptotic form factors, allowing us to tune this microscopic parameter directly and examine the corresponding low-energy strongly coupled dynamics. For $3\le N_c\le 12$, we extract the scattering lengths, effective ranges, and low-energy constants $\bar{l}_i$, and study their $N_c$ dependence. We also obtain the $σ$, $ρ$, and $f_2$ resonances for the same range of $N_c$. The $ρ$ and $f_2$ masses depend only weakly on $N_c$, and their widths decrease as $N_c$ increases, consistent with the $1/N_c$ scaling expected for conventional $q\bar{q}$ mesons. In contrast, the $σ$ meson mass and width both increase with $N_c$, in agreement with a previous unitarized $χ$PT analysis. These results provide a further test of the Gauge Theory Bootstrap for studying asymptotically free gauge theories.

</details>


### [111] [Holographic viscous transport in scalar-coupled Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16936)
*Tao Ma,Song He,Li Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在五维 Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet 全息模型中，直接对比了视界公式与从推迟应力张量关联函数提取的体粘滞系数和剪切粘滞系数，验证了视界公式在含 Gauss-Bonnet 耦合情形下的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验全息等离子体剪切和体粘滞系数的视界公式是否可靠，特别是在 Gauss-Bonnet 耦合为标量（dilaton）依赖、且不使用视界守恒律或熵产生公式的情况下，验证这些视界公式能否还原直接从边界 Kubo 公式得到的推迟应力张量关联函数结果。

Method: 在标定为零重子密度下格点 QCD 状态方程的五维 Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet 模型中展开研究。通过加入曲率和标量反项获得紫外有限的归一化关联函数，并在保留对 Gauss-Bonnet 耦合 α 的完整依赖下求解涨落方程。同时对比视界守恒流计算（常数耦合精确、标量依赖耦合一阶截断）与边界 Kubo 结果。

Result: 常数耦合时视界守恒流计算在剪切和体两个通道都与边界 Kubo 结果一致；标量依赖耦合下两种方法都发现 η/s 存在极小值。体通道中一阶 α 截断的守恒流计算得到的 ζ/s 峰值偏低，但峰值温度几乎不变。对两种耦合，未修改的 Eling-Oz 公式 ζ/s=(4π)⁻¹(∂φ_h/∂ln s)² 都能精确还原边界 ζ/s 结果，即使 Gauss-Bonnet 相互作用使 η/s 偏离 1/(4π)，该前置因子依然保留。

Conclusion: 这些结果为视界处方提供了直接的边界基准，并支持未修改的 Eling-Oz 公式在常数耦合之外的更一般情形下仍然成立。

Abstract: We test horizon formulas for the shear and bulk viscosities of a holographic plasma against viscosities extracted directly from retarded stress-tensor correlators. We work in a five-dimensional Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet model calibrated to the lattice QCD equation of state at zero baryon density. UV-finite renormalized correlators are obtained by including the required curvature and scalar counterterms, and the fluctuation equations are solved while retaining the full dependence on the Gauss-Bonnet coupling $α$, without using a horizon conservation law or an entropy-production formula. For constant coupling, the horizon conserved-current calculation reproduces the boundary Kubo results in both channels. For the scalar-dependent coupling studied here, both methods find a minimum in the shear-viscosity-to-entropy-density ratio $η/s$. In the bulk channel, the conserved-current calculation truncated at first order in $α$ yields a lower peak height in $ζ/s$, with the peak temperature nearly unchanged relative to the boundary result. For both couplings, the unmodified Eling-Oz (EO) formula, $ζ/s=(4π)^{-1}(\partialφ_h/\partial\ln s)^2$, reproduces the boundary result for $ζ/s$, where $φ_h$ is the horizon value of the scalar. The prefactor $1/(4π)$ is thus retained even when the Gauss-Bonnet interaction shifts $η/s$ from this value. These results provide a direct boundary benchmark for horizon prescriptions and support the unmodified EO formula beyond constant coupling.

</details>


### [112] [Covariant Wigner--Weyl calculus, non-dissipative transport and the chiral anomaly in inhomogeneous systems with nontrivial momentum-space topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17178)
*M. A. Zubkov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述、系统化并推广了非阿贝尔规范场中费米子的协变 Wigner–Weyl 微积分及其在坐标空间非均匀、动量空间具有非平凡拓扑系统中的应用，涵盖非阿贝尔手征分离效应与量子霍尔效应、相互作用下的稳定性、非均匀系统费米面不变量及非椭圆模型，并给出若干新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将此前分散的关于协变 Wigner–Weyl 微积分及其在非均匀拓扑系统中应用的结果统一到一致的约定下，明确假设与定义，填补该领域中关于算符协变符号规范场无关性、反常分解、重整化定理连续性条件等尚未解决的关键空白，并澄清非椭圆模型中手征磁效应与手征分离效应、反常之间的关系。

Method: 作者采用协变 Wigner–Weyl 微积分作为核心数学工具，对非阿贝尔规范场中的费米子体系进行符号运算与拓扑不变量分析；结合动量空间拓扑不变量（如 N_3）、指标配对、Moyal 乘积的纤维度、留数计算及 Luttinger–Ward 恒等式等方法，对非椭圆模型在连续与格点情形下的费米面不变量进行求解与表征。

Result: 新结果包括：表征协变符号与规范场无关的算符、积分反常的排序无关性；不依赖同伦假设且当无扭曲指标为零时反常精确分解为瞬子数与 N_3 之积；重整化速度流背后的 Luttinger–Ward 恒等式及替代布里渊区紧性的连续性条件；手征分离效应的 Moyal N_3 与主符号纤维度的等同；非椭圆模型费米面不变量的留数求值，及其蕴含的沿磁场守恒荷平衡流（一种非哈密顿形式所允许的手征磁效应），球面费米面模型将其换成手征分离效应与单 Weyl 反常，修改后可恢复该效应但以牺牲反常为代价；以及手征分离不变量的指标配对形式。

Conclusion: 本文完成了对协变 Wigner–Weyl 微积分及其拓扑应用的系统综述与推广，在统一约定下重新陈述旧结果并给出多项新结论，深化了对非均匀系统中动量空间拓扑、反常分解、非椭圆模型输运与不变量之间关系的理解，为后续研究提供了清晰的数学框架与可检验的判据。

Abstract: We review, systematize and extend the covariant Wigner--Weyl calculus for fermions in non-Abelian gauge fields and its applications to systems inhomogeneous in coordinate space with nontrivial momentum-space topology: the non-Abelian chiral separation and quantum Hall effects, their stability under interactions, the Fermi-surface invariant of inhomogeneous systems and the non-elliptic models that carry it, with their equilibrium chiral magnetic effect, the factorization of the chiral anomaly into the instanton number and the momentum-space invariant $N_3$, and the noncommutative-geometric form of the invariants. Previous results are brought to common conventions, with hypotheses stated and notions defined. New results: the characterization of the operators whose covariant symbol is gauge-field independent and the ordering independence of the integrated anomaly; the anomaly factorization on a closed four-manifold without the homotopy hypothesis, the factorization into instanton number and $N_3$ holding exactly when the untwisted index vanishes; the Luttinger--Ward identity behind the renormalized-velocity current and the continuum conditions replacing the compactness of the Brillouin zone in the non-renormalization theorems; the identification of the Moyal $N_3$ of the chiral separation effect with the fibre degree of the principal symbol; the residue evaluation of the Fermi-surface invariant of the non-elliptic models, in the continuum and on a lattice, and the equilibrium current of the conserved charge along the magnetic field that it implies -- a chiral magnetic effect permitted by the non-Hamiltonian form of these models -- which the model with spherical Fermi surfaces trades for a chiral separation effect and a single-Weyl anomaly, and a modification of it restores at the price of the anomaly; and the index-pairing form of the chiral separation invariant.

</details>


### [113] [Love numbers for the Myers-Perry geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17261)
*Rhucha Deshpande,Oleg Lunin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在任意维度的 Myers-Perry 几何中分析了标量场的 Love 数，将黑洞潮汐响应归结为两个常数：一个编码潜在的 logarithmic running（对数跑动），另一个是 Love 数本身，并论证跑动参数对视界结构不敏感，而 Love 数依赖于视界处的边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黑洞潮汐响应（Love 数）以探测黑洞内部结构与引力理论，尤其关注高维旋转黑洞（Myers-Perry）中标量场 Love 数的定义、跑动行为及对视界结构的敏感程度。

Method: 利用任意维度 Myers-Perry 几何中波动方程（标量场）的渐近展开，显式计算描述对数跑动的参数；Love 数则通过多种不同的近似方案，在指定视界边界条件后计算得到。

Result: 潮汐响应由两个常数刻画：跑动参数与 Love 数。作者论证跑动参数对视界结构不敏感，并在任意维度显式算出该参数；Love 数依赖视界边界条件的细节，并通过若干不同近似方法加以计算。

Conclusion: 在任意维度 Myers-Perry 黑洞中，标量场的对数跑动参数与视界结构无关，可解析确定；而 Love 数本身则敏感依赖于视界处的边界条件，需借助多种近似才能求得。

Abstract: We analyze Love numbers for scalar fields in the Myers-Perry geometry in arbitrary dimensions. The tidal responses of black holes are specified by two constants which encode a potential logarithmic running and the Love number. We argue that the parameter specifying the running is not sensitive to the structure of the horizon, and we explicitly compute this parameter for Myers-Perry black holes in arbitrary dimensions by utilizing the asymptotic expansion of the wave equation. The Love numbers rely on the details of the boundary conditions at the horizon, and we compute them using several distinct approximations.

</details>


### [114] [A precision bound for gravitational transseries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17274)
*Ji Hoon Lee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出全息可观测量在黑洞阈值以上的大 N 超序列 (transseries) 存在一个精度界：它由 D-膜瞬子数据决定，限制了在有限 N 下超序列能确定可观测量的精度。作者发展了基于 Écalle 理论的大 N 复兴 (resurgence) 方法，以捕捉超越 e^{-N} 瞬子的 e^{-N^2} 引力鞍点渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大 N 超序列在黑洞相中的可求和性 (resummation) 问题：当引力可观测量超过黑洞阈值时，单纯的 e^{-N} 瞬子不足以完整刻画渐近结构，需要理解由 D-膜瞬子 (e^{-N^2} 量级) 编码的更深层瞬子数据，以及它们如何限制超序列在有限 N 下的精度。

Method: 基于 Écalle 的工作发展大 N 复兴方法，将 D-膜瞬子求和纳入分析，捕捉 e^{-N^2} 渐近的引力鞍点；在假定黑洞相中 D-膜瞬子求和的增长行为下，证明大 N 超序列重新求和存在解析障碍 (analytic obstruction)。以 1/16-BPS 指数作为主要例子和一致性检验，推导热 AdS 与黑洞上 D-膜瞬子数据之间的对偶关系（交换两种背景上的 D-膜瞬子数目），并借此复现 D3-膜缠绕黑洞的作用量。

Result: 发现在黑洞相中对大 N 超序列的重新求和存在解析障碍，该障碍设定了精度界。在 N=4 超杨-米尔斯理论的 1/16-BPS 指数中，推导出热 AdS 与黑洞两种背景上 D-膜瞬子数据间的对偶关系，并成功复现了 D3-膜缠绕黑洞的作用量，验证了框架的一致性。

Conclusion: 大 N 超序列的精度由 D-膜瞬子数据设定的精度界所限制；该框架可推广到一般情形，并与双锥虫洞 (double-cone wormhole) 相联系。作者对未来在更多一般场景中的应用给出预期。

Abstract: We propose that the large $N$ transseries of a holographic observable above the black hole threshold admits a precision bound, a limit on how precisely the transseries can determine the observable at finite $N$, set by the data of its D-brane instanton sectors. In doing so, we develop methods in large $N$ resurgence, based on the work of Écalle, that capture the $e^{-N^2}$ asymptotics of gravitational saddles beyond the $e^{-N}$ instantons. Under an assumption on the growth of the D-brane instanton sums that we expect in the black hole phase, we find an analytic obstruction to the resummation of the large $N$ transseries. This obstruction sets the precision bound. As our main example and consistency check, we study the $\frac{1}{16}$-BPS index of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills and its black hole. We derive relations between the D-brane instanton data on thermal AdS and on the black hole, in the form of a duality that exchanges the numbers of D-brane instantons on the two backgrounds. In the index, these relations reproduce the actions of D3-branes wrapped on the black hole. We conclude with our expectations for generic situations and their relation to the double-cone wormhole.

</details>


### [115] [The $(5,5)$ String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17275)
*Justin Kaidi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究一种变形的 N=(2,1) 杂化弦，其右移子在 R^{2,2} 上、左移子具 (5,5) 号差（由 II_{3,3} 格点连接而成）。该弦无需零流，目标空间仍为四维，全部态（具有十六个超荷的无穷塔）均为无质量态。作者提出其经典目标理论是左移子物理态所成李超代数的自对偶规范理论，从而弦的每个态都是规范场，引力被纳入规范代数，得到一个纯三次、涵盖闭弦所有模式的场论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种能够将闭弦全部模式（含引力）统一为单一规范代数的场论表述。标准 N=(2,1) 弦需要放入零流（null current）才能保持无质量与一致性，本文希望构造一个无需零流、且目标空间仍保持四维的替代方案，为“引力即规范场”的自对偶规范理论提供弦论实现。

Method: 通过将左移子号差取为 (5,5) 并引入格点 II_{3,3} 构造 N=(2,1) 杂化弦。分析其谱：所有态无质量并承载十六个超荷。对中性玻色子，利用 Kutasov-Martinec 行列式作用量，证明其与“自对偶度量耦合自对偶麦克斯韦场”的三次作用量对偶，并用单个超场补全费米子部分。对带电态，给出证据表明其耦合常数等于左移子物理态所构成李超代数的结构常数。

Result: 该弦的所有态（无穷塔、含十六个超荷）均无质量。中性玻色子部分可由自对偶度量与自对偶麦克斯韦场的三次作用量描述，并可延拓到费米子。带电态耦合表现为李超代数的结构常数。由此得到经典目标理论是上述李超代数的自对偶规范理论：弦的每个态都是规范场，引力是规范代数的一部分，整体构成覆盖闭弦所有模式的纯三次场论。

Conclusion: 作者提出该杂化弦的经典目标理论是一个自对偶规范理论，其规范代数为左移子物理态构成的李超代数；每个弦态对应一个规范场，引力被纳入其中。这为“闭弦全部模式可由纯三次场论描述、且引力为规范场”的构想提供了具体模型与证据。

Abstract: We study an $N=(2,1)$ heterotic string with right-movers on $\mathbb R^{2,2}$ and left-movers of signature $(5,5)$, obtained by adjoining the lattice $\mathrm{II}_{3,3}$. Unlike the standard $N=(2,1)$ strings it requires no null current, and its target space remains four-dimensional. All of its states, an infinite tower with sixteen supercharges, are massless. For the neutral bosons, the Kutasov-Martinec determinant action is dual to a cubic action for a self-dual metric coupled to self-dual Maxwell fields, and we complete it with fermions using a single superfield. For the charged states we give evidence that the couplings are the structure constants of a Lie superalgebra formed by the physical states of the left-movers. We are thus led to propose that the classical target theory is a self-dual gauge theory for this superalgebra: every state of the string is a gauge field, and gravity is part of the gauge algebra. This would be a purely cubic field theory of all the modes of a closed string.

</details>


### [116] [Bound States of Two D0-Branes and the Three-Graviton Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17325)
*Sergio Sanjurjo,Themistocles Zikopoulos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文数值求解了 M 理论 SU(2) BFSS 矩阵模型的基态（对应两个 D0 膜或沿 M 理论圈的十一维引力子的束缚态），再现了三引力子耦合系数，并论证了该耦合的超对称保护性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证 BFSS 猜想中零能束缚态（两 D0 膜／十一维引力子）的性质，并通过数值方法精确检验三引力子耦合，进而探究其超对称保护机制及模型激发束缚态。

Method: 对 SU(2) BFSS 矩阵模型进行数值求解基态波函数；利用波函数渐近幂律尾巴系数提取三引力子耦合，并与 Herderschee 和 Maldacena 的解析结果对比；用矩阵模型中非重整化的一圈 D0 膜相互作用推导该耦合值；最后用表示论论证讨论激发束缚态。

Result: 数值解得到的波函数呈现出强耦合核心与 D0 膜分离时的幂律渐近尾巴；幂律衰减系数与 Herderschee-Maldacena 的三引力子耦合高精度吻合；证明该耦合受超对称保护，其值可由非重整化一圈相互作用导出。

Conclusion: 数值结果高精度验证了 BFSS 矩阵模型中三引力子耦合，并确认其超对称保护性质，为理解该模型的束缚态结构与 M 理论引力相互作用提供了有力支持。

Abstract: We numerically solve for the ground state of the $SU(2)$ BFSS matrix model for M-theory. This state corresponds to the zero-energy bound state of two D0-branes or, by the BFSS conjecture, to the eleven-dimensional graviton with two units of momentum along the M-theory circle. The wavefunction exhibits a strongly coupled core, as well as an asymptotic power-law tail where the D0-branes are well separated. The coefficient of this power-law decay is the three-graviton coupling, previously computed by Herderschee and Maldacena, which our numerical solution reproduces with high accuracy. We also show that this coupling is protected by supersymmetry in the matrix model, and derive its value from the non-renormalized one-loop interaction of the two D0-branes. Lastly, we provide a simple representation-theoretic argument regarding the recently proposed excited bound states of the model.

</details>


### [117] [Decoupling Dynamics: A Tale of Two Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17327)
*Andrea Antinucci,Oscar Lewis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明d维QFT在动力学规范连续阿贝尔对称性前后的不同SymTFT，实际上是同一个(d+1)维动力学全息规范理论的不同拓扑极限，其边界条件由规范操作决定；作者给出了这些SymTFT在希尔伯特空间和算符层面上的完全量子定义，并分析了非反常与反常例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知d维QFT的对称性由高一维的拓扑理论（SymTFT）刻画，SymTFT在规范有限对称性下不变，但在动力学规范连续对称性下会改变。作者旨在解释为什么规范连续对称性会改变SymTFT，并给出其完全量子的定义。

Method: 作者将规范前后不同的SymTFT视为同一个(d+1)维动力学全息规范理论的不同拓扑极限，规范操作改变该动力学理论的边界条件从而选取不同的拓扑极限。结合体全息理论的适当极限与光子截断，在希尔伯特空间和算符层面给出SymTFT的完全量子定义。

Result: 该构造阐明拓扑对称性缺陷、荷与反常如何保留，而流携带的动力学信息如何被丢弃。作者研究了非反常与反常情形，对(2+1)维体给出完整量子分析；对于具有混合轴矢反常的四维边界理论，恢复了规范矢量对称性后剩余的非可逆手征对称性的SymTFT。

Conclusion: d维QFT在动力学规范连续阿贝尔对称性前后的SymTFT变化源于同一(d+1)维动力学全息规范理论边界条件的改变，这一构造给出了SymTFT的完全量子定义，并成功复现了包括非可逆手征对称性在内的反常情形。

Abstract: The symmetries of a $d$-dimensional QFT are known to be captured by a topological theory in one dimension higher, the Symmetry Topological Field Theory (SymTFT), which is invariant under gauging finite symmetries of the QFT, but not under dynamical gauging of continuous symmetries. We show that the distinct SymTFTs of a $d$-dimensional QFT before and after dynamical gauging of a continuous Abelian symmetry arise as different topological limits of the same dynamical holographic gauge theory in $(d+1)$-dimensions. Gauging changes the boundary condition of this dynamical theory and thereby selects a different topological limit, explaining why the associated SymTFT changes. We give a fully quantum definition of these SymTFTs at the level of Hilbert spaces and operators by combining suitable limits of the bulk holographic theory with photon truncation. This construction clarifies how topological symmetry defects, charges, and anomalies survive while the dynamical information carried by currents is discarded. We study non-anomalous and anomalous examples, providing a complete quantum analysis for $(2+1)$-dimensional bulks. For a four-dimensional boundary theory with a mixed axial-vector anomaly, we recover the SymTFT of the non-invertible chiral symmetry that remains after gauging the vector symmetry.

</details>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [High-Fidelity In Silico Dataset of Ordered Binary and Ternary MXenes with Predictive Synthesis Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14687)
*Ahmed H. Biby,Charles B. Musgrave*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用DFT(r²SCAN泛函)构建了1040种有序二元/三元剥离MXene的热力学数据集，发现所有MXene在平衡条件下均不可合成，但在高温(115种)、高压(23种)及特定碳化学势(3种Mn-Fe-C)下可稳定，为MXene数据驱动发现提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新型MXene的定向合成因结构和组成空间庞大、缺乏可靠且考虑合成条件的稳定性描述符而受限，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论(DFT)结合r²SCAN泛函，对1040种有序的二元与三元剥离态MXene(含单过渡金属、面内有序双过渡金属、面内有序金属双空位、面外有序双过渡金属四类)计算其形成能、分解吉布斯能、分解产物、层对称群及能量高于凸包(energies above the hull)的值；并评估高温(500–2000 K)、高压(10 GPa)及碳化学势窗口下的热力学稳定性。

Result: 所有MXene在平衡条件下热力学不可及；其中115种(以碳化物为主)在高温下热力学稳定，23种在高压下稳定，3种处于Mn–Fe–C化学空间的MXene在实验可行的碳化学势范围内得以稳定。研究揭示了MXene空间的稳定性趋势，并明确了可行的合成策略。

Conclusion: 该数据集为开发机器学习稳定性描述符和指导新型MXene实验发现提供了坚实基础，通过识别可合成候选材料及其合成条件，弥合了数据驱动MXene发现中的关键缺口，并奠定了未来自主实验工作流的基础。

Abstract: Despite their promise, the targeted synthesis of novel MXenes remains challenging due to their vast structural and compositional space and the lack of reliable stability descriptors that account for synthesis conditions. To address this gap, we utilized density functional theory (DFT) with the r$^2$SCAN functional to develop a high-fidelity dataset of 1,040 ordered binary and ternary bare delaminated MXenes, including mono-transition metal MXenes, in-plane ordered double-transition metal MXenes, in-plane ordered metal-divacancy MXenes, and out-of-plane ordered double-transition-metal MXenes. For each structure, we calculated formation and decomposition Gibbs energies, decomposition products, layer symmetry groups, and energies above the hull. We find that all MXenes are thermodynamically inaccessible under equilibrium conditions. However, 115 predominantly carbide MXenes become thermodynamically stabilized at elevated temperatures (500--2000 K), and 23 are stabilized under high pressure (10 GPa). Additionally, three MXenes in the Mn--Fe--C chemical space are stabilized within an experimentally feasible range of carbon chemical potentials. These findings reveal stability trends across the MXene space and identify synthesis strategies that can guide experimental efforts. Our dataset provides a robust foundation for developing machine-learned stability descriptors and for guiding experimental efforts toward novel MXene discovery. By identifying synthesizable candidates along with their synthesis conditions, this work bridges a critical gap in data-driven approaches to MXene discovery and lays the foundation for future autonomous experimental workflows.

</details>


### [119] [Improving Nonequilibrium Thermal Conduction in CaF2 by Breaking the Phonon Bottleneck Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14957)
*Mingde Qu,Minghuan Song,Yuxing Wang,Wudi Wang,Chenbo Zhang,Jiamin Quan,Jun Xu,Zhongwei Zhang,Jie Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文针对CaF2激光光学材料中由纵向光学(LO)声子瓶颈效应导致的热积累问题，提出通过选择性激发横向声学(TA)声子来加速LO声子弛豫、促进能量向热载声子转移，从而实现主动热管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光光学材料中热电子将能量快速传递给传导能力弱的LO声子，形成非平衡声子瓶颈效应，限制了高效散热。传统上散热性能主要依赖热导率，但能量在不同声子模式间的重新分配同样关键，因此需要寻找打破声子瓶颈的调控策略。

Method: 结合电子-声子散射计算、声子玻尔兹曼输运分析和非平衡分子动力学模拟，识别出LO声子为优先接收热载流子能量的主要模式；并借助三声子散射相空间分析，提出选择性激发TA声子以加速LO弛豫并促进能量向声学模式转移的调控方案。

Result: LO声子能量积累源于其输运和弛豫效率低下；持续激发LO声子使热导率下降约12%；在LO声子瓶颈限制的态基础上，额外激发TA模式可使热导率提升约21%，甚至超过未激发时的数值。

Conclusion: 通过控制声子支间的能量重新分配，可以抵消声子瓶颈引起的热积累，为激光光学材料提供了一条通过选择性调控声子布居实现主动热管理的新途径。

Abstract: Heat dissipation in laser optical materials depends not only on their thermal conductivity but also on how absorbed energy is redistributed among various phonon modes. In calcium fluoride (CaF2), rapid energy transfer from hot electrons to weakly propagating longitudinal optical (LO) phonons can create a nonequilibrium phonon bottleneck effect that limits efficient heat removal. Here, we propose selective phonon excitation as an effective strategy to break this bottleneck. Combining electron-phonon scattering calculations, phonon Boltzmann transport analysis, and nonequilibrium molecular dynamics simulations, we identify LO phonons as the primary modes that preferentially receive energy from hot carriers, leading to energy accumulation due to their inefficient transport and relaxation. Guided by three-phonon scattering phase-space analysis, selective excitation of transverse acoustic (TA) modes can accelerate LO relaxation and promote energy transfer toward heat-carrying acoustic modes. While sustained LO-phonon excitation reduces the thermal conductivity by approximately 12%, additional TA-mode excitation improves heat conduction by approximately 21% compared to the state limited by the LO-phonon bottleneck, surpassing the unexcited value. These findings demonstrate that controlling interbranch energy redistribution can counteract phonon-bottleneck-induced heat accumulation and reveal a working route for active thermal management in laser optical materials through selective control of phonon populations.

</details>


### [120] [Reversal of the Anomalous Hall Effect by Directional In-Plane Electric Field in Strained Bilayer MoO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14982)
*Wenpeng Wang,hang Shi,Xiangru Kong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究应变双层MoO中层状磁性材料的谷/自旋自由度调控，证明面内电场方向可选择性切换单一谷的自旋劈裂并反转反常霍尔电导率，揭示应变与电场联合调控谷选择性自旋劈裂和反常霍尔响应的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状磁性材料若能同时调控谷和自旋自由度，可望用于多功能自旋电子器件。作者旨在探索如何利用外部场（应变与电场）对这类材料中的谷选择性自旋劈裂和反常霍尔响应进行可逆、定向的控制。

Method: 以双层MoO为研究对象，在能量与动力学稳定的AC2堆叠构型中施加双轴应变，诱导狄拉克型能带交叉以创造电场调控的条件；随后施加沿a、b方向的面内电场，分别选择性地解除X谷和Y谷的自旋简并，并分析Berry曲率的重新分布。同时对同样稳定且能量、动力学稳定的AC1构型进行应变扫描，研究金属-拓扑绝缘体转变、量子化自旋霍尔电导、谷极化和压磁性等现象。

Result: 1) 沿a方向电场仅在X谷劈裂自旋、沿b方向电场仅在Y谷劈裂自旋，实现单谷自旋劈裂的选择性切换；2) 该切换伴随符号相反的Berry曲率重新分布，导致反常霍尔电导率可逆反转；3) AC2中双轴应变诱导狄拉克型能带交叉，为后续电场控制提供基础；4) AC1构型在应变下展现金属-拓扑绝缘体转变、量子化自旋霍尔电导、谷极化及压磁性等丰富现象。

Conclusion: 应变与面内电场的联合作用能有效控制谷选择性自旋劈裂和反常霍尔响应，双层MoO有望成为多功能自旋电子与谷电子器件的理想平台。

Abstract: Layered magnetic materials with tunable valley and spin degrees of freedom hold promise for multifunctional spintronic devices. Here, we demonstrate that the orientation of an in-plane electric field enables selective switching of single-valley spin splitting and reversal of the anomalous Hall conductivity in strained bilayer MoO. In particular, electric fields along the a and b directions selectively lift the spin degeneracy at the X and Y valleys, respectively, while preserving it at the other valley. This valley-selective switching is accompanied by a redistribution of Berry curvature with opposite signs, giving rise to a reversible anomalous Hall response. These effects are realized in the energetically and dynamically stable AC2 stacking configuration, where biaxial strain induces Dirac-like band crossings that enable subsequent electric-field control. Meanwhile, the AC1 stacking configuration, which is also energetically and dynamically stable, exhibits rich strain-driven phenomena, including a transition from metal to topological insulator with quantized spin Hall conductivity, as well as valley polarization and piezomagnetism. Our findings reveal a combined effect of strain and electric fields for controlling valley-selective spin splitting and anomalous Hall responses, highlighting bilayer MoO as a promising platform for multifunctional spintronic and valleytronic devices.

</details>


### [121] [Depth determination from core-loss 4D-STEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15037)
*Michael Deimetry*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出利用动量分辨的核损失四维扫描透射电子显微术（4D-STEM）数据，在Born近似下通过探针位置傅里叶变换产生的干涉条纹频率来确定掺杂原子的深度位置，并用贝叶斯框架在极低剂量下实现纳米级到亚纳米级的深度测量精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越样品投影显微图像、确定掺杂原子在三维空间中的分布，对材料表征与开发至关重要。传统STEM显微图只能提供投影信息，无法直接获知掺杂原子沿深度方向的分布。

Method: 采用动量分辨的核损失4D-STEM数据；在Born近似下，对探针位置进行傅里叶变换，形成干涉条纹，其频率编码了掺杂原子相对焦平面的距离。针对两个实验挑战：（1）动力学散射降低条纹对比度，通过远离主要晶带轴采集来部分缓解；（2）核损失剂量极高，引入贝叶斯统计框架处理低计数数据。

Result: 在模拟中，该贝叶斯方法仅凭100到1000个核损失计数（取决于信背比）即可实现纳米到亚纳米级的深度测量精度。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在极低核损失剂量条件下实现掺杂原子的三维深度定位，为实际实验条件下的掺杂剂三维分布表征提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Moving beyond specimen-projected micrographs in scanning transmission electron microscopy to determine the three-dimensional distribution of dopants is of vital importance for materials characterization and development. We present an approach for dopant depth determination using momentum-resolved core-loss four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy data. Within the Born approximation, Fourier transformation over probe position reveals fringes whose frequency encodes the dopant's distance from the focal plane. Two principal challenges arise in extending this approach to realistic experimental conditions. First, dynamical scattering degrades fringe contrast which can be partially mitigated by acquiring off major zone axes. Second, because core-loss doses are punishing, we use a Bayesian framework which, in simulation, achieves nanometre to sub-nanometre depth precision from only 100 to 1000 core-loss counts, depending on the signal-to-background.

</details>


### [122] [Defective $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15060)
*Jorge Vega Bazantes,Timo Lebeda,Zhiqiang Mao,Jianwei Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用密度泛函理论（多体 meta-GGA 泛函）研究了块体 α-In₂Se₃ 的电子结构及其中性点缺陷（空位与反位缺陷）的形成能，发现中层 Se 空位在 Se 富与 In 富两种生长条件下均具有异常低的形成能，并通过局域结构弛豫揭示了其稳定性来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-In₂Se₃ 是一种具有铁电应用前景的二维范德华材料，但原生缺陷会显著影响其结构和电子性质。已有研究对缺陷（尤其是不同层位 Se 空位）的形成机制与稳定性缺乏基于高精度泛函的微观解释，因此需要系统研究其在 Se 富与 In 富条件下的缺陷形成行为。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT），并使用先进的 meta-GGA（meta-generalized gradient approximation，r²SCAN 类）交换关联泛函，对块体 α-In₂Se₃ 的电子结构进行计算；系统考察中性点缺陷（空位与反位缺陷）在 Se-rich 和 In-rich 两种化学环境下的形成能，并结合结构弛豫分析缺陷周围的局域原子重排与对称性。

Result: 计算成功复现了纯净 α-In₂Se₃ 的实验结构参数。发现中层 Se 空位在 Se-rich 与 In-rich 两种条件下均具有异常低的形成能。结构分析表明，该空位相邻的四面体配位 In 原子沿三重轴发生显著的局域弛豫，配位数由 4 降为 3，但仍保持其 C₃ᵥ 位点对称性，从而为这一缺陷的显著稳定性提供了微观解释。

Conclusion: 基于 meta-GGA 泛函的 DFT 计算表明，块体 α-In₂Se₃ 中层 Se 空位是最易形成的原生缺陷之一，其异常稳定性源于相邻 In 原子配位降低却保持 C₃ᵥ 对称性的局域弛豫机制。该结果为理解 α-In₂Se₃ 中的原生缺陷行为及其对铁电和电子性质的影响提供了重要理论依据。

Abstract: $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ is a two-dimensional van der Waals material with promising applications involving ferroelectricity. However, the presence of native defects can significantly influence its structural and electronic properties. In this work, we employ density functional theory with a state-of-the-art meta-generalized gradient approximation (meta-GGA) exchange-correlation functional to investigate the electronic structure of bulk $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ and the formation of neutral point defects, including vacancies and antisites, under Se-rich and In-rich growth conditions. Our calculations reproduce the experimental structural parameters of the pristine material and reveal that the Se vacancy in the middle layer exhibits an unexpectedly low formation energy under both chemical environments. Structural analysis shows that the neighboring tetrahedrally coordinated In atom undergoes a substantial local relaxation along the three-fold axis, which preserves its $C_{3v}$ site symmetry even as its coordination is reduced from four to three, providing a microscopic explanation for the remarkable stability of this defect.

</details>


### [123] [High-Throughput Surface Screening Reveals Energetically Competitive Low-Symmetry Facets in Wurtzite Semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15138)
*Gustavo Madrigal,Cibrán López,Jordi Llorca,Claudio Cazorla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合自动化表面生成、机器学习原子间势筛选与密度泛函理论，系统考察了纤锌矿 GaN、LaN 和 ZnO 的 226 个清洁化学计量表面（覆盖 |h|,|k|,|l|≤3 的全部 Miller 取向），发现低对称高指数棱柱面在能量上可与传统 m 面和 a 面竞争，但相近的形成能并不对应相似的电子能带对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤锌矿表面在诸多应用中起决定作用，但以往第一性原理研究多局限于高对称低指数面，因为低对称高指数表面建模计算成本高且复杂。而实际上表面形成能由面原子密度和断键强度决定，并不天然排斥高 Miller 指数面，因此有必要系统探索高指数面的稳定性与电子性质。

Method: 采用"自动化表面生成 + 机器学习原子间势筛选 + 密度泛函理论（DFT）+ 杂化泛函计算"的多级流程：对 GaN、LaN、ZnO 生成全部满足 |h|,|k|,|l|≤3 的 226 个清洁化学计量表面模型及其不等价终止面；先用机器学习势快速筛选，再用 DFT 计算表面形成能，并用杂化泛函评估真空参考的能带边缘位置（电离势与电子亲和势）。

Result: 十五个最低能量非极性面中有九个为高指数面，各化合物五个最佳候选面的表面能仅高出最小值 2.5–5.3%，表明低对称高指数棱柱面在能量上可与传统 m、a 面竞争。但相近的形成能不意味着相似的电子能级对齐：GaN 中两个最稳定表面形成能仅差 0.08 J/m²，真空参考带边位置却相差近 0.8 eV；LaN 中近简并的 m 面与 a 面相差 0.16 eV。

Conclusion: 纤锌矿中具有相近形成能的不同晶面可表现出显著不同的电离势与电子亲和势，因此表面稳定性与能带对齐需联合、系统地评估。该工作为实际可出现的纤锌矿晶面提供了稳定性和能带对齐图谱，可为相关纳米结构与异质界面的理性设计提供候选表面。

Abstract: Surfaces and interfaces govern the performance of wurtzite semiconductors in electronics, sensing, and solar-fuel conversion. To date, first-principles studies have focused mostly on high-symmetry low-index facets, owing to the computational cost and complexity of modelling low-symmetry high-index surfaces. In practice, however, surface formation energy is determined by the planar atomic density and the strength of the bonds severed upon cleavage, neither of which inherently excludes high-Miller-index surfaces from being energetically competitive. Here, we combine automated surface generation, machine-learning interatomic potential screening, and density functional theory to explore 226 clean, stoichiometric surface models of wurtzite GaN, LaN, and ZnO, spanning all Miller orientations with $|h|,|k|,|l| \le 3$ and their inequivalent terminations. We find that low-symmetry high-index prismatic facets are energetically competitive with respect to the conventional $m$- and $a$-planes: nine of the fifteen lowest-energy nonpolar surfaces are high-index, and the five best candidates of each compound lie within only 2.5-5.3% of the minimum surface energy. Hybrid-functional calculations reveal, however, that this energetic proximity does not translate into similar electronic alignment. In GaN, the two most stable surfaces differ by just 0.08 J m$^{-2}$ in formation energy but by almost 0.8 eV in vacuum-referenced band-edge positions, whereas in LaN the nearly degenerate $m$- and $a$-planes are separated by 0.16 eV. Wurtzite facets with similar formation energies may therefore exhibit very different ionization potentials and electron affinities. Our results enable the joint, systematic assessment of stability and band alignment across the facets that can actually occur in wurtzites, and provide a map of candidate surfaces for the rational design of related nanostructure and heterointerfaces.

</details>


### [124] [Probing Unknown Luttinger-Like Parameters and Band Structure in BAs: A 24-Band k.p and DFT Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15186)
*Mohsen Jemai,Nouha Mastour,Omar Zitouni,Tarek Barhoumi,Said Ridene*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用24能带k.p方法和密度泛函理论(DFT)计算并分析了砷化硼(BAs)的电子能带结构及与p型导带相关的未知Luttinger参数，验证了k.p方法的高精度与高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定BAs中p型导带尚未知的Luttinger-like参数，并考察24能带k.p模型能否在保证精度的同时，提供比DFT更高效的计算途径，以支撑复杂纳米结构和量子阱的建模。

Method: 采用24能带k.p理论与DFT两种方法计算BAs的电子能带结构；DFT计算使用CP2K代码中实现的高斯与平面波基组；将k.p结果与DFT及已有实验结果对比，并对24能带k.p哈密顿量参数进行调整。

Result: 24能带k.p模型在所研究的能量范围内准确再现了BAs的电子能带结构和关键能带参数，其有效质量和能带参数与先前发表的理论研究相吻合。

Conclusion: k.p方法在计算上比DFT更高效，同时保持高精度，适合用于基于BAs半导体的复杂纳米结构和量子阱的建模。

Abstract: In this work, the electronic band structure and the unknown Luttinger-like parameters associated with the p-type conduction bands (CBs) of boron arsenide (BAs) are calculated and analyzed using both 24-band $k.p$ and density functional (DFT) theories. For the DFT study, Gaussian and plane-wave basis sets implemented in the CP2K code were used. Comparison with DFT and available experimental results shows that the 24-band k.p model accurately reproduces the known electronic properties of BAs. These results demonstrate that the 24-band k.p model accurately reproduces the electronic band structure and key band parameters over the investigated energy range. In addition, the 24-band $k.p$ Hamiltonian parameters are adjusted and we show that the effective masses and the band parameters are validated against previously published theoretical studies. These findings suggest that the $k.p$ method is computationally more efficient than DFT, making it suitable for modeling complex nanostructures and quantum wells based on BAs semiconductor.

</details>


### [125] [Competing Octahedral Tilting and $B$-Site Off-Centering Govern the Phase Diagram of CsGe$_{1-x}$Sn$_x$Br$_3$ Perovskite Alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15200)
*Apinya Ngoipala,Erik Fransson,Paul Erhart,Julia Wiktor*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过机器学习势驱动的大规模分子动力学模拟，系统研究了CsGe₁₋ₓSnₓBr₃全组分范围内Ge/Sn两种B位阳离子所偏好的不同结构畸变（极性离位与八面体倾转）之间的竞争，揭示了低温下的同型相界及完整的成分-温度相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无铅卤化物钙钛矿中，Ge和Sn虽可替代Pb以避免毒性，但二者引发不同的结构畸变：Ge的立体化学活性4s²孤对电子驱动极性B位离位，而较大的Sn²⁺阳离子则倾向于八面体倾转。理解这两种不稳定性在全组分混合下的竞争机制，对调控无铅钙钛矿的性质至关重要，但此前缺乏系统研究。

Method: 作者利用基于杂化泛函数据训练的电荷感知（charge-aware）机器学习原子间势，对CsGe₁₋ₓSnₓBr₃全组分范围（x=0到1）进行大规模分子动力学模拟，绘制成分-温度相图，分析相变序列、结构序参量及电子带隙的变化。

Result: 低温下合金在x=0.60与0.65之间存在同型相界，分隔铁电单斜Cc相与具有混合反极性/极性离位的单斜P2₁相，倾转模式和离位有序均发生不连续变化。成分-温度相图再现了两个端点化合物的已知相变序列和温度，并在相界附近揭示了一个在任一母体化合物中都不存在的中间四方I4/mcm相。相变温度以上，B位阳离子保持局部离位但取向无序。相界处的结构相变导致低温下带隙跳变，而350 K时带隙随Sn含量平滑减小。

Conclusion: 该研究表明B位混合是调控无铅卤化物钙钛矿中极性与旋转有序的有效手段，为无铅钙钛矿的结构与电子性质设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Lead-free halide perovskites based on Ge and Sn avoid the toxicity of Pb, but the two cations favor different structural distortions. The stereochemically active Ge $4s^2$ lone pair drives polar $B$-site off-centering, whereas the larger Sn$^{2+}$ cation favors octahedral tilting. Here, we map how these two instabilities compete across the full composition range of CsGe$_{1-x}$Sn$_x$Br$_3$ using large-scale molecular dynamics simulations with a charge-aware machine-learned interatomic potential trained on hybrid-functional data. At low temperature the alloy exhibits a morphotropic phase boundary between $x=0.60$ and $0.65$ that separates a ferroelectric monoclinic $Cc$ phase from a monoclinic $P2_1$ phase with mixed antipolar and polar off-centering, with both the tilt pattern and the off-centering order changing discontinuously. The composition-temperature phase diagram reproduces the known transition sequences and temperatures of both end members and reveals an intermediate tetragonal $I4/mcm$ phase near the boundary that has no counterpart in either parent compound. Above the transition temperatures the $B$ cations remain locally off-centered but orientationally disordered. The structural transition at the boundary causes a jump in the band gap at low temperature, whereas at $350$ K the gap decreases smoothly with Sn content. These results establish $B$-site mixing as a means to tune polar and rotational order in lead-free halide perovskites.

</details>


### [126] [Brute-force molecular dynamics simulation of aging in amorphous Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15664)
*Elisa Petroni,Dario Baratella,Omar Abou El Kheir,Andrea Redaelli,Marco Bernasconi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用机器学习原子间势对非晶态 Ge2Sb2Te5 在略低于玻璃转变温度下进行了约 100 ns 的老化分子动力学模拟，发现老化过程中轴向键的 Peierls 畸变增强（与能隙增大相关）、原子迁移率显著降低、键寿命增加，但同极键并未在模拟时间尺度内减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相变电子存储器所用的硫系合金（如 Ge2Sb2Te5）在非晶态下会发生老化，导致电阻增大，对器件运行不利。作者需要揭示这种老化背后的结构弛豫机制。

Method: 采用机器学习原子间势，在略低于玻璃转变温度的温度下对非晶态 Ge2Sb2Te5 进行分子动力学模拟：先在 400 K 平衡 24 ns，再进行约 100 ns 的老化模拟；并辅以密度泛函理论（DFT）计算较小老化非晶模型中的电子能隙。

Result: 老化过程中观察到轴向键的 Peierls 畸变增强，这与 DFT 计算得到的电子能隙拓宽相关；原子迁移率明显下降、键寿命增加；但同极键数量未减少（理想老化玻璃中应不存在同极键，其消除需更长时间尺度）。

Conclusion: 老化主要与轴向键 Peierls 畸变增强和原子迁移率/键寿命变化相关，而消除同极键则需要远长于 100 ns 的时间尺度，说明非晶 Ge2Sb2Te5 的老化是一个多时段、多机制的过程。

Abstract: Chalcogenide alloys such as the prototypical Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$ compound are used in phase change electronic memories due to their ability to undergo a fast and reversible transition between the amorphous and crystalline phases upon heating. The amorphous phase is subject to aging that leads to an increase in the electrical resistance, which is detrimental to the operation of the devices. Using a machine learning interatomic potential, we have investigated the structural relaxations responsible for aging in amorphous Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$ in molecular dynamics simulations at temperatures slightly below the glass transition. The aging simulation, lasting about 100 ns, follows an equilibration of 24 ns at 400 K. During aging we observed an enhancement of the Peierls distortion in axial bonds which is a typical feature of these alloys that we relate to the widening of the electronic band gap computed within Density Functional Theory in smaller aged amorphous models. We also observed a marked reduction of the atomic mobility with an increase of the bond lifetimes during aging. However, we do not see a reduction in homopolar bonds, which are expected to be absent in the ideal aged glass and which should be removed on a much longer time scale.

</details>


### [127] [Periodic Trends in Oxygen Vacancy Formation Energies Across Binary Main-Group Metal Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15297)
*Manish Kumar,Bridget S. Ferris,Avital Isakov,Robert B. Wexler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文系统性地用 r²SCAN DFT 计算了 39 种主族金属二元氧化物多晶型的中性氧空位形成能，并用基于晶体本征性质的可解释特征模型（核心描述符为晶体还原势和带隙）以 0.31 eV 的平均绝对误差复现这些能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧空位决定了氧化物材料（如固体氧化物燃料电池、太阳能热化学燃料）的功能。三元钙钛矿的形成能已系统测绘，而二元氧化物作为其概念与热化学构建单元，其报告值分散在不同计算方法的各研究中，缺乏系统性、一致性的数据。

Method: 采用 r²SCAN 密度泛函理论，对涵盖第 1、2、13、14、15 族的 39 种主族金属二元氧化物多晶型进行系统性调查，并用 ShakeNBreak 方法定位重构后的缺陷基态；随后拟合一个可解释的晶体特征模型，其输入为原始晶体的本征性质（还原势可取自热化学表），并保留两个主导描述符进行建模。

Result: 形成能跨度达 8 eV，沿各族向下递减，且对多数多价阳离子随氧化态升高而降低，两者都遵循阳离子的可还原性。仅保留晶体还原势和带隙两个主导描述符的模型，以 0.31 eV 的留出集平均绝对误差复现形成能，低于其针对过渡金属钙钛矿的四描述符母模型。完整 Born-Haber 拟合中的断键项与稳定性项对二元氧化物在统计上不显著；最大残差识别出电子容纳偏离阳离子还原图像的主晶格。

Conclusion: 该数据集为机器学习原子间势提供了基准，所建模型可仅凭主族氧化物块体性质估算其空位形成能，打通了从本征性质到缺陷能量的预测路径。

Abstract: The function of oxide materials, from solid-oxide fuel cells to solar thermochemical fuel production, depends on their oxygen vacancies. Their formation energetics have been mapped systematically for ternary perovskites, but reported values for the binary oxides, their conceptual and thermochemical building blocks, remain scattered across studies with differing computational methods. Here we report a systematic r$^2$SCAN density functional theory survey of neutral oxygen vacancy formation energies across 39 binary main-group metal oxide polymorphs spanning groups 1, 2, 13, 14, and 15, using the ShakeNBreak method to locate reconstructed defect ground states. The formation energies span 8 eV and decrease down each group and, for most multivalent cations, with increasing oxidation state. Both trends follow the reducibility of the cation. An interpretable crystal feature model is fit to these energies; its inputs are properties of the pristine bulk, with the reduction potential alternatively taken from tabulated thermochemistry. Retaining only its two dominant descriptors, the crystal reduction potential and the band gap, the model reproduces the formation energies with a held-out mean absolute error of 0.31 eV, lower than that of its four-descriptor parent model for transition-metal perovskites. The bond-breaking and stability terms of the full Born-Haber fit prove statistically null for the binary oxides, and the largest residuals identify the hosts where electron accommodation departs from the cation-reduction picture that the descriptors encode. The dataset provides a benchmark for machine-learning interatomic potentials, and the model estimates vacancy formation energies of main-group oxides from bulk properties alone.

</details>


### [128] [Anisotropy driven critical phenomena in a van der Waals ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15380)
*Neesha Yadav,Harish Chandr Chauhan,Pintu Das*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了范德华铁磁体 Fe3GeTe2 的场依赖临界行为，利用 ANS 方法提取不同磁场窗口下的临界指数，发现易轴方向（B∥c）下体系由双重交换与巡游 RKKY 竞争主导的长程相互作用，随磁场增大逐渐趋近于长程三维 Ising 普适类；而难轴方向（B⊥c）下临界行为呈现亚普适（γ<1）特征，归因于外场塞曼能与强面外磁晶各向异性的竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明外加磁场、强磁晶各向异性以及涌现的其他能量尺度如何调控范德华铁磁体 Fe3GeTe2 的临界行为，并证明场依赖临界行为研究的必要性，超越传统单一框架的分析。

Method: 采用最近发展的归一化斜率平均法（ANS）分析临界行为，在高达 7 T 的多个磁场窗口下分别提取临界指数 β、γ 和 δ，并比较磁场沿易轴（B∥c）与面内难轴（B⊥c）两种构型下的结果。

Result: 当 B∥c 时，体系表现为由竞争性双重交换与巡游 RKKY 机制主导的长程相互作用，该行为随磁场增大被系统性抑制并收敛于长程三维 Ising 普适类（β∼0.330，γ∼1.00）；当 B⊥c 时，在整个测量磁场范围内观察到亚普适（γ<1）行为，该现象源于外场塞曼能与强面外磁晶各向异性之间的激烈竞争性相互影响。

Conclusion: 非平庸范德华磁体的临界行为从根本上受磁场取向与磁晶各向异性强度的调制，表明进行场依赖临界行为研究相对于传统框架是必要的。

Abstract: We report a detailed investigation into the field-dependent critical behavior of the van der Waals ferromagnet $\mathrm{Fe_3GeTe_2}$ to elucidate how applied magnetic fields ($B$), strong magnetocrystalline anisotropy, and emerging other energy scales modulate its critical behavior. Utilizing the recently developed method, viz., the average of normalized slope (ANS), for analysis of critical behavior, we evaluate the critical exponents ($β$, $γ$, and $δ$) across distinct field windows up to $\rm{7\,T}$. For $B$ applied along the crystal's easy axis ($B\parallel c$), the system exhibits long-range interactions dominated by competing double-exchange and itinerant Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) mechanisms, which is systematically suppressed with increasing $B$ to converge toward the long-range 3D Ising universality class ($β\sim0.330$, $γ\sim1.00$). Conversely, under an in-plane hard-axis configuration ($B\perp c$), the critical behavior study exhibits a sub-unity regime ($γ<1$) across the measured range of $B$. This behavior is attributed to the intense competitive interplay between the external Zeeman energy and strong out-of-plane magnetocrystalline anisotropy. Our findings demonstrate that critical behavior in nontrivial van der Waals magnets is fundamentally modulated by the orientation of $B$ and the strength of magnetocrystalline anisotropy, demonstrating the necessity of $B$-dependent critical behavior investigation over conventional frameworks.

</details>


### [129] [Metric random tilings: exact entropy and the diffraction of counted disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16730)
*Xinyan Wu,Qingqing Ji*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出"度量随机镶嵌"（metric random tilings）概念，研究拓扑锁定但边长波动的周期性网络，用精确计数引擎枚举开普勒的11种均匀网络，区分出7种零熵和4种正度量熵载体，并分析其熵与衍射特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机镶嵌（晶体、准晶体）中被计数的变量是"排列"而非"几何"，几何度量被视为由化学设计给定、由衍射测量的固定量。本文的目标是让几何本身成为可计数的对象，即在拓扑锁定、面精确闭合约束下允许边长涨落的周期性网络，从而将度量无序纳入精确计数范式，并使其能在真实材料中通过衍射（布拉格斑被"度量晕"包裹）被识别。

Method: 引入"度量随机镶嵌"框架：周期性网络保持锁定拓扑，边长在精确面闭合条件下涨落。构建专用的"计数引擎"（counting engines）来枚举开普勒的十一种均匀网络（uniform networks），按度量熵分类；对其中最密的载体（蜂窝网络）计算所有边长值域下的熵，并分析熵随值域平移的不变性以及衍射中相干分数随补丁尺寸的变化（德拜-沃勒定律）。

Result: 十一种均匀网络被分为7种零熵网络和4种正度量熵载体（每个均由显式构造证明）。在蜂窝网络（最密载体）上，所有边长值域均具有广延熵：算术类达到每瓦片 s = 0.544 kB（拟合带 0.53–0.58），其余两类约 0.2。衍射方面，边长值域的平移增大使熵严格不变，而固定补丁尺寸下布拉格强度的相干分数从 9×10⁻⁴ 变化到 0.58，遵循幅度流的德拜-沃勒定律。

Conclusion: 度量无序成功进入精确计数范式，并可通过真实材料衍射中"布拉格斑被度量晕包裹"的特征加以识别，为理解具有度量自由度的材料体系提供了新的理论与实验判据。

Abstract: Tiling mathematics has twice become the language of a state of matter: crystals, then quasicrystals, whose thermodynamics are described by the random tiling of fixed shapes. There the counted variable is the arrangement rather than the geometry, although the metric is what chemical design sets and diffraction measures. Here we introduce metric random tilings, periodic networks of locked topology whose edge lengths fluctuate under exact face closure, so that the geometry itself is countable. Purpose-built counting engines enumerate Kepler's eleven uniform networks and divide them into seven zero-entropy ones and four carriers with positive metric entropy, each certified by an explicit construction. On the honeycomb, the densest carrier, all edge-length value domains considered carry extensive entropy, with the arithmetic class reaching $s = 0.544\,k_B$ per tile (fit band 0.53--0.58), the other two $\approx 0.2$. In diffraction, translational increase of the value domain leaves the entropy exactly invariant while the coherent fraction of the Bragg intensity at fixed patch size varies from $9\times10^{-4}$ to 0.58, following the Debye--Waller law of the amplitude flow. Metric disorder thus enters the exact-counting paradigm, and is identifiable in real materials by Bragg spots wrapped in metric halos.

</details>


### [130] [Materials research with a general-purpose agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15451)
*Paulo R. Pires,Théo Cavignac,Tiago F. T. Cerqueira,Hai-Chen Wang,Silvana Botti,Miguel A. L. Marques*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍Alexandria材料数据库（约1500万条晶体密度泛函计算及其衍生数据、模型与反应图），并展示一个通用语言模型智能体如何借助这些资源自动完成从自然语言提问到可执行实验方案的完整材料发现流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料发现通常需要人工整合数据库、机器学习模型与化学知识，过程繁琐且依赖专家经验。作者希望将大规模DFT数据库、预训练模型（生成模型、通用原子间势、图神经网络）与反应图统一交给一个通用语言模型智能体，验证其能否独立完成"从提问到可做实验"的端到端闭环，从而大幅降低材料研发门槛。

Method: 作者构建了Alexandria数据库（含PBE、PBEsol、SCAN、TB-mBJ计算结果、最高150 GPa下的弛豫结构、介电张量、约14.5万条电子-声子计算，以及超4亿个机器学习势弛豫结构），并训练了晶体结构生成模型、通用原子间势和十多种性质的图神经网络。此外，基于凸包计算了193,416个稳定化合物之间174亿个反应构成的反应图。然后将这些数据、模型和反应图交给通用语言模型智能体，让其无人值守地执行筛选、推理与实验设计任务，并以太阳能吸收材料筛选、Ba-Zr-S体系探索及空气稳定替代物设计等多个案例进行验证。

Result: 智能体能够无人值守地从数据库筛选太阳能吸收材料，剔除合成放热过低的候选物，并给出含前驱体、坩埚和气氛的完整合成方案；在Ba-Zr-S体系中发现一个未报道的硫化物并设计出可证实或证伪该化合物的实验；还可结合生成模型新结构设计并测试空气稳定替代物。仅靠网络搜索时，该智能体只能提出文献中已有的方案，而结合数据、模型与反应图后，它能达成三者单独均无法完成的闭环任务。

Conclusion: 将大规模DFT数据、机器学习模型和反应图统一交给通用语言模型智能体，可使其单独完成从自然语言问题到实验室可执行、可验证实验的完整流程，证明"数据+模型+反应图"的协同能释放通用智能体在材料自动发现中的能力。

Abstract: Over the last decade we have built Alexandria, an open database of about 15 million density-functional calculations of crystals. It includes PBE, PBEsol, SCAN and TB-mBJ results, structures relaxed at pressures up to 150 GPa, dielectric tensors and about 145,000 electron-phonon calculations, together with more than 400 million structures relaxed with machine-learned potentials. On these data we trained a generative model for crystal structures, a universal interatomic potential and graph neural networks for more than ten properties, from band gaps to superconducting critical temperatures. From the convex hull we computed a reaction graph of the 17.4 billion reactions between its 193,416 stable compounds. Here we give all of this to a general-purpose language-model agent. Working unattended, the agent screens the database for a solar absorber, drops a candidate whose synthesis releases too little energy, and writes a synthesis plan with precursors, crucible and atmosphere. Asked about the well-studied Ba-Zr-S system, it finds an unreported sulfide and designs the experiment that would confirm or rule it out. Asked for an air-stable alternative, it designs one and tests it against new structures from the generative model. With web search alone, the same agent proposes what the literature already discusses. Put together, the data, the models and the reaction graph let a general agent do what none of them does alone: go from a question in plain words to an experiment that a laboratory can run and test.

</details>


### [131] [Defect Topology Governs the Optical Response of Doped Amorphous Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15478)
*Ridwan Hussein,Chinonso Ugwumadu,Louise A. M. Rosset,Roxanne M. Tutchton*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文采用杂化泛函密度泛函理论与线性响应计算，研究非晶硅(a-Si)中缺陷拓扑与化学掺杂对其光学响应的共同影响，揭示掺杂灵敏度不仅取决于缺陷浓度，还取决于缺陷的性质与空间组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶硅中缺陷拓扑与化学掺杂对光学响应的影响机制尚不明确，尤其缺乏原子尺度上缺陷结构如何调控掺杂效应的认识。

Method: 采用杂化泛函密度泛函理论(DFT)和线性响应计算，构建并考察四种结构不同的连续随机网络，并进行替代式硼(B)和磷(P)掺杂；结合Tauc分析和光学求和规则进行研究。

Result: 计算结果再现了关键实验趋势：具有完全伸展4配位的构型对掺杂的介电扰动最强；含预存缺陷态的网络掺杂效应更复杂。多数网络中硼比磷对介电响应扰动更强。含3配位和5配位缺陷、总缺陷分数为7.4%的网络逆转了硼主导的响应，变得对磷更敏感。Tauc与光学求和规则表明网络拓扑决定整体光学图景，而掺杂主要影响带隙附近态并重新分配光谱权重。

Conclusion: 缺陷拓扑是连接非晶结构、掺杂活性与光学响应的微观调控参数，为理解非晶硅光电性质提供了新的物理图景。

Abstract: The influence of defect topology and chemical doping on the optical response of amorphous silicon (a-Si) remains unresolved. In this study, hybrid-functional density functional theory and linear-response calculations are employed to examine four structurally distinct continuous random networks with substitutional boron and phosphorus doping. The calculated optical properties reproduce key experimental trends and demonstrate that dopant sensitivity is determined not only by defect concentration, but also by the character and spatial organization of defects. A configuration with fully extended 4-fold coordination shows the strongest dielectric perturbation to doping, while dopant effects in networks with pre-existing defect states are more varied. For most networks, the dielectric response is more strongly perturbed by boron than phosphorus. Notably, a network having both 3- and 5-fold defects with a total defect fraction of 7.4% reverses the typical boron-dominant response of other cases, becoming more sensitive to phosphorus. Tauc and optical sum-rule analyses further indicate that network topology defines the broader optical landscape, whereas doping primarily affects states near the gap and redistributes spectral weight. These findings establish defect topology as a microscopic control parameter that links amorphous structure, dopant activity, and optical response in amorphous silicon.

</details>


### [132] [Competing lattice instabilities in epitaxially strained BaSnO3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15550)
*Benyao Sun,Anna Grünebohm*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用密度泛函理论研究了双轴压缩应变下BaSnO3的晶格动力学，揭示了其结构不稳定性的层级，指出相稳定性由多种晶格失稳及其与非谐耦合的竞争所决定，而非仅由极性模软化控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清BaSnO3在双轴压缩应变下的相稳定性机制，纠正仅凭极性模软化推断相稳定性的不足，并探讨电场或电子掺杂调控相变临界应变的可能性。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）结合双模式能量面分析，系统地研究了不同应变下的晶格动力学模态（极性模、A点和M点八面体旋转）及其非谐耦合。

Result: 极性序与旋转序呈现竞争性耦合；A点和M点八面体旋转在中等应变下比区心极性模更早失稳，但对应连续相变且能量增益有限；大应变可诱导超四方极性态的一级相变，其极化强度和铁电能阱深度与压缩应变下BaTiO3相当；电场或电子掺杂可降低临界应变、促进超四方结构。

Conclusion: BaSnO3在压缩应变下的相稳定性由多种晶格失稳的竞争及其非谐耦合主导，超大应变可产生与BaTiO3相媲美的铁电极性态，且可通过电场或电子掺杂在更低应变下诱导超四方相。

Abstract: We use density functional theory to map the lattice-dynamical landscape of BaSnO3 under biaxial compressive strain and uncover the hierarchy of structural instabilities. Our results demonstrate that the phase stability of strained BaSnO3 cannot be inferred from the softening of the polar mode alone. Instead it is governed by the competition among lattice instabilities and their anharmonic coupling to structural relaxation. Two-mode energy-surface analyses show that the polar and rotational order parameters interact predominantly competitively. While A- and M-point octahedral rotations become unstable before the zone-center polar mode and dominate the harmonic response for moderate values of strain, they are related to a continuous transition without anharmonic lattice coupling and a moderate gain of energy with strain. Large strain may induce a first order transition to a supertetragonal polar state. Once this state is stabilized, the magnitude of polarization and ferroelectric energy well depth are comparable to BaTiO3 under compressive strain. Furthermore, we discuss how an electric field or electron doping may tip the balance between the different modes and favor of the supertetragonal structure over antipolar and rotational alternatives at a reduced critical strain.

</details>


### [133] [Dislocation Arrays Hosting Topological Bound States in Epitaxial Tetragonal FeSe Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16681)
*Huimin Zhang,Basu Oli,Zhengfei Wang,Michael Weinert,Lian Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了实空间中拓扑缺陷可承载稳健的局域量子态，用于量子信息处理与传感，但指出在晶体材料中确定性控制缺陷形成仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑缺陷能够承载稳健且局域的量子态，这对量子信息处理和量子传感具有重要价值；然而，目前还缺乏在晶体材料中确定性调控这些缺陷形成的方法，这一困难构成了本研究的主要推动力。

Method: 摘要未给出具体实验或理论方法，仅提出了问题背景，强调需解决晶体材料中拓扑缺陷形成的确定性控制难题。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验结果，仅陈述了拓扑缺陷可承载稳健局域量子态的已知结论，并指出确定性控制缺陷形成仍是尚未解决的挑战。

Conclusion: 实空间中拓扑缺陷为量子信息处理与传感提供了稳健的局域量子态平台，但在晶体材料中实现对缺陷形成的确定性控制仍是一个关键的未解难题。

Abstract: Topological defects in real space can host robust, localized quantum states for quantum information processing and sensing, yet deterministic control over their formation in crystalline materials remain challenging.

</details>
