### [134] [Cation-intermixing driven evolution from a narrow avoided crossing to an enlarged inverted gap in Ge$_2$Bi$_2$Te$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15631)
*Alexander S. Frolov,Alexander V. Fedorov,Nadezhda V. Vladimirova,Sergey M. Aksenov,Mark A. Naumov,Savely V. Domenko,Artem A. Minaev,Vasily S. Stolyarov,Denis V. Vyalikh,Lada V. Yashina*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过结合拓扑绝缘体Bi₂Te₃与铁电Rashba半导体GeTe的构筑单元，合成Ge₂Bi₂Te₅化合物，发现阳离子混排能驱动其电子结构从狭窄的避免交叉演变为显著增大的反转带隙，从而稳定拓扑电子结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否通过阳离子混排这一晶体生长过程中可控的自由度来调控和工程化拓扑电子结构，为功能量子材料的设计开辟新途径。

Method: 结合Bi₂Te₃的构筑单元与GeTe的结构基元合成Ge₂Bi₂Te₅化合物，并进行系统表征；采用动量分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）与从头计算（ab initio）相结合的方法研究其电子结构。

Result: Ge₂Bi₂Te₅表现出显著的阳离子混排；阳离子混排使Ge和Bi原子在阳离子亚晶格内重新分布，增加了价带顶附近Bi衍生态的贡献并增强自旋轨道耦合的有效贡献，从而驱动电子结构从狭窄的避免交叉演变为大幅增大的反转带隙，稳定了拓扑电子结构。

Conclusion: 阳离子无序（可在晶体生长过程中控制）为工程化拓扑电子结构提供了额外的自由度，为功能量子材料的开发开辟了新机遇。

Abstract: Combining the building blocks and structural motifs of the topological insulator Bi$_2$Te$_3$ and the ferroelectric Rashba semiconductor GeTe, we synthesized and comprehensively characterized the resulting Ge$_2$Bi$_2$Te$_5$ compound, which exhibits pronounced cation intermixing. Using momentum-resolved photoemission spectroscopy and ab initio calculations, we demonstrate how cation intermixing drives the evolution of its electronic structure from a narrow avoided crossing to a substantially enlarged inverted band gap. We establish the microscopic origin of this behavior in the redistribution of Ge and Bi atoms within the cation sublattice, which increases the contribution of Bi-derived states near the valence-band maximum and enhances the effective contribution of spin-orbit coupling, thereby stabilizing the topological electronic structure. Our findings demonstrate that cation disorder, which can be controlled during crystal growth, provides an additional degree of freedom for engineering topological electronic structures, opening new opportunities for the development of functional quantum materials.

</details>


### [135] [Zero-Field Optical Readout of Quintet States in Through-Space Coupled Tetracene Oligomers at Room Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15713)
*Jeannine Grüne,Maciej Majdecki,William K. Myers,Przemysław Gaweł,Hannah L. Stern*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过空间耦合的四并苯低聚物中五重态的自旋特性，实现了室温、零磁场下的光学读出，并证明构象柔性体系仍具有446 ns的室温相干时间，为分子量子比特（qudit）与量子传感器拓展了设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学可寻址的电子自旋是量子技术的有前景平台，分子体系可对其自旋与光学性质进行化学调控。然而，实现室温、无外加磁场条件下分子自旋态的光学读出仍具挑战。由单线态裂分产生的五重态是潜在的光学可寻址多能级qudit，但其在零场和弱磁场下的本征自旋跃迁鲜有研究，且显著的室温五重态相干性此前多在结构受限的分子构架中被证实。

Method: 作者将零场光探测磁共振（zero-field ODMR）与瞬态及脉冲电子顺磁共振（EPR）结合，考察构象柔性、通过空间耦合的四并苯低聚物中五重态的磁场依赖性与相干自旋性质。对四并苯二聚体与六聚体进行室温零场光学读出，并测绘光探测跃迁对弱外加磁场的响应；辅以EPR测量验证通过随机交换涨落形成五重态。

Result: 在四并苯二聚体和六聚体中均实现了室温、零场下的五重态光学读出，并获得了光探测跃迁对弱磁场的响应图谱。EPR测量支持五重态经随机交换涨落形成，并展示了446 ns的室温相位记忆时间，表明促成高自旋形成的构象动力学与显著自旋相干性可以兼容。

Conclusion: 该工作在一个化学模块化、通过空间耦合的分子构架中整合了无场光学读出、弱场响应和室温自旋相干性，扩展了光学可寻址分子qudit及量子传感器的设计空间。

Abstract: Optically addressable electron spins are promising platforms for quantum technologies, with molecular systems offering chemical control over their spin and optical properties. Achieving optical readout of molecular spin states at room temperature and without an applied magnetic field is an important step towards their practical implementation. Quintet states generated via singlet fission are promising optically addressable multilevel qudits, but their intrinsic spin transitions at zero and weak magnetic fields remain largely unexplored, while substantial room-temperature quintet coherence has predominantly been demonstrated in structurally constrained molecular architectures. Here, we combine zero-field optically detected magnetic resonance (zero-field ODMR) with transient and pulsed electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) to investigate the magnetic-field dependence and coherent spin properties of quintet states in conformationally flexible, through-space coupled tetracene oligomers. We demonstrate zero-field optical readout of quintet states at room temperature in both tetracene dimers and hexamers, and map the response of optically detected transitions to weak applied magnetic fields. Complementary EPR measurements support quintet formation through stochastic exchange fluctuations and demonstrate a room-temperature phase-memory time of 446 ns, showing that the conformational dynamics enabling high-spin formation remain compatible with substantial spin coherence. Together, these results integrate field-free optical readout, weak-field response and room-temperature spin coherence within a chemically modular, through-space coupled molecular architecture, expanding the design space for optically addressable molecular qudits and quantum sensors.

</details>


### [136] [Chiral-phonon-induced anomalous Hall effect in bilayer MoSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15761)
*Omer Yaniv,Swati Chaudhary,Carolina Paiva,Dominik M. Juraschek*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文证明，在非磁性、中心对称的双层MoSe₂中，光驱动的 chiral 声子可诱导反常霍尔响应；圆偏振激发E_u声子产生圆形动力学 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合，使 Floquet 能带在两谷处携带同号贝里曲率，霍尔响应符号由声子手性决定，σ_xy 可达 10⁻²–0.21 (e²/2πħ)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在非磁性、中心对称材料中通过超快光激发实现贝里曲率与电子输运调控的新途径，克服传统磁性或结构反演对称性破缺的限制。

Method: 基于第一性原理计算参数化双层 MoSe₂ 的有效 k·p 哈密顿量，研究圆偏振光激发 E_u 声子产生的圆形动力学 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合，并通过 Floquet 理论计算两谷的贝里曲率及霍尔电导。

Result: 圆激发 E_u 声子在两谷处产生同号贝里曲率，引发手性相关的反常霍尔响应；在实验可达光脉冲下 σ_xy 约为 10⁻²–0.21 (e²/2πħ)。

Conclusion: chiral 声子为在超快时间尺度上调控电子贝里曲率和输运提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: We demonstrate that optically driven chiral phonons induce an anomalous Hall response in a nonmagnetic, centrosymmetric material. Using first-principles calculations to parameterize an effective $\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}$ Hamiltonian for bilayer MoSe$_2$, we show that circular excitation of an $E_u$ phonon generates a circular dynamical Rashba spin-orbit coupling. The resulting Floquet bands carry same-sign Berry curvature at the two valleys, producing a Hall response whose sign follows the phonon handedness. We obtain a range of $σ_{xy}\sim10^{-2}-0.21$ $\frac{e^2}{2π\hbar}$ for experimentally accessible light pulses. Our results show that chiral phonons provide a route to controlling electronic Berry curvature and transport on ultrafast time scales.

</details>


### [137] [A Hierarchical Harmonic-to-Anharmonic Framework for ab initio Screening and Mechanistic Analysis of Negative Thermal Expansion in ZrV2O7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15767)
*J. Bustamante,A. Miliute,N. Jaykhedkar,A. A. Naik,J. Grandel,F. Emmerling,T. M. Stawski,J. George*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层计算方法研究ZrV2O7的负热膨胀（NTE），依次结合准谐近似（QHA）、温度依赖有效势（TDEP）和机器学习加速的从头算分子动力学（ML-MD），并用XRD实验验证。QHA先以负Grüneisen参数的低频声子作为廉价筛选指标，TDEP引入有限温度非谐性，ML-MD揭示驱动晶格收缩的温度依赖结构畸变；横向位移（uT）的增加为高温下准刚性单元模式（QRUM）机制提供了微观证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 框架材料的负热膨胀源于复杂的晶格动力学，传统理论方法难以捕捉；需要一种既能廉价筛选潜在NTE材料、又能在原子尺度解析其结构机制的分层计算策略。

Method: 构建分层计算策略：(1) 准谐近似（QHA）晶格动力学计算，识别具有负Grüneisen参数的低频声子模作为廉价筛选描述符；(2) 温度依赖有效势（TDEP）纳入有限温度非谐性；(3) 机器学习加速的从头算分子动力学（ML-MD）揭示温度依赖的结构畸变；并以X射线衍射（XRD）实验测量加以佐证。

Result: QHA识别出具有负Grüneisen参数的低频声子模；TDEP和ML-MD表明非谐性与温度依赖结构畸变驱动晶格收缩；横向位移（uT）随温度同时增大，为高温下准刚性单元模式（QRUM）机制提供了微观证据；结果与ZrW2O7等其他框架材料中集体多面体旋转和横向原子运动导致NTE的报道一致，并得到XRD实验验证。

Conclusion: 相对廉价的晶格动力学描述符（负Grüneisen参数的低频声子）可有效筛查潜在NTE材料，而更高层次的非谐模拟（TDEP、ML-MD）能够进一步在原子尺度解析NTE的结构机制；本文为框架材料NTE的研究提供了可行的分层计算范式。

Abstract: Negative thermal expansion (NTE) in framework materials arises from complex lattice dynamics that are challenging to capture using conventional theoretical approaches. Here, we establish a hierarchical computational strategy for investigating NTE in ZrV2O7, integrating the quasiharmonic approximation (QHA), temperature-dependent effective potentials (TDEP), and machine-learning-accelerated ab initio molecular dynamics (ML-MD). Quasi-harmonic lattice-dynamical calculations identify low-frequency phonon modes with negative Grüneisen parameters as inexpensive screening descriptors, while TDEP incorporates finite-temperature anharmonicity and ML-MD further reveals temperature-dependent structural distortions that drive the lattice contraction. The observations are further corroborated with X-ray diffraction (XRD) measurements. The concurrent increase in transverse displacement (uT) provides microscopic evidence for a quasi-rigid-unit-mode (QRUM) mechanism underlying the NTE behaviour at elevated temperatures. This is consistent with previous reports on other framework materials, including ZrW2O7, where collective polyhedral rotations and transverse atomic motions have been linked to NTE. Overall, our results demonstrate how relatively inexpensive lattice-dynamical descriptors can identify potential NTE materials, while higher-level anharmonic simulations can then resolve the underlying structural mechanisms at the atomic scale.

</details>


### [138] [Evidential Deep Regression for Universal Density Functional Theory Hamiltonian Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15786)
*Jiaxuan Liu,Yan Zhang,Caiyuan Ye,Tiannian Zhu,Zhong Fang,Hongming Weng,Quansheng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出 EviDTB，一种用于紧束缚 DFT 哈密顿量的证据深度回归方法，可在单次前向传播中估计预测不确定性，在计算成本与不确定性量化性能之间取得良好平衡，并通过不确定性引导筛选来加速材料电子性质搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用 DFT 哈密顿量模型可高效地从原子结构预测电子性质，但如何在快速预测哈密顿量的同时估计预测不确定性仍是一大挑战。现有方法（深度集成、MC Dropout、MVE）要么计算成本高，要么不确定性量化效果不佳。

Method: 提出 EviDTB，一种基于证据深度回归（evidential deep regression）的方法，用于紧束缚 DFT 哈密顿量建模，能在单次前向传播中同时输出预测值和不确定性估计。并与深度集成、Monte Carlo dropout 和均值-方差估计（MVE）三种基线方法进行对比。

Result: 在 5,478 个留出测试结构上，EviDTB 的完整自旋-轨道耦合哈密顿量矩阵元 MAE 为 0.622 meV。其每原子平均不确定性与平均局部平方预测误差相关性良好（Spearman ρ=0.812）；移除 15% 最不确定的结构后，该误差的第 99 百分位从 16.63 eV² 降至 3.72 eV²，下降 78%。

Conclusion: EviDTB 以较低计算成本实现了较好的不确定性量化，与预测误差高度相关，可作为优先筛选第一性原理验证候选结构的实用评分手段，有望加速具有目标电子性质材料的搜索。

Abstract: Universal density functional theory (DFT) Hamiltonian models trained across broad chemical and structural spaces offer an efficient route from atomic structure to electronic properties. However, estimating prediction uncertainty alongside fast Hamiltonian predictions remains a challenge. In this work, we present EviDTB, an evidential deep regression approach for tight-binding DFT Hamiltonians that effectively estimates prediction uncertainty in a single forward pass. Compared with deep ensembles, Monte Carlo dropout, and mean-variance estimation (MVE), EviDTB achieves a better balance between computational cost and uncertainty-quantification performance. On 5,478 held-out test structures, EviDTB achieves a full spin--orbit-coupled (SOC) Hamiltonian matrix-element mean absolute error of 0.622meV. Its mean uncertainty per atom correlates with the mean local squared prediction error (Spearman $ρ=0.812$); removing the 15% most uncertain structures reduces the 99th percentile of this error by 78%, from 16.63 to 3.72eV$^2$. This score offers a practical way to prioritize candidates for first-principles verification. We hope that combining fast Hamiltonian prediction with uncertainty-guided screening will accelerate the search for materials with desirable electronic properties.

</details>


### [139] [Lithium redistribution and electrode degradation in commercial NMC/Graphite and LCO/Graphite cells under high-rate cycling conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15818)
*Naisargi Kanabar,Nicholas Fusco,Seiichiro Higashiya,Devendra Sadana,Stephen Bedell,Haralabos Efstathiadis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究快速充电对NMC811/石墨和LCO/石墨两种锂离子电池衰减的影响，发现两者在1C与2C之间存在循环稳定性转折点，且锂在石墨负极的积累在2C时达到最大，强调负极侧退化与充电制度的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速充电会加速锂离子电池退化，但正极化学体系与充电制度对衰减的影响难以分离，需要系统地区分二者在高倍率循环性能中的各自作用。

Method: 对商用NMC811/石墨（200 mAh）和LCO/石墨（45 mAh）扣式电池分别在0.3C、1C、2C、3C下循环100次，随后采用扫描电子显微镜（SEM）、X射线衍射（XRD）和核反应分析（NRA）进行表征分析。

Result: 两类电池均在1C与2C之间出现循环稳定性转折。NMC811/石墨在0.3C和1C下保持约99.7%的初始放电容量，2C降至72.3%，3C降至63.5%；而LCO/石墨在2C下仅保持11.1%。NRA显示两种化学体系的石墨负极锂积累均在2C时达到最大，SEM观察到颗粒状表面沉积物。XRD显示正极(003)和石墨(002)衍射峰随倍率发生偏移，表明循环过程中存在结构变化。3C下较低的锂积累归因于电荷吞吐量减少，而LCO/石墨的3C工况受其充电制度限制。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了负极侧退化与充电制度在解读商用锂离子电池高倍率循环性能中的关键作用，为理解快充衰减机理提供了依据。

Abstract: Fast charging accelerates degradation in lithium-ion batteries, but the effects of cathode chemistry and charging protocol remain difficult to separate. Here, commercial NMC811/graphite (200 mAh) and LCO/graphite (45 mAh) coin cells were cycled for 100 cycles at 0.3C, 1C, 2C, and 3C, followed by scanning electron microscopy (SEM), X-ray diffraction (XRD), and nuclear reaction analysis (NRA). Both cell types exhibited a transition in cycling stability between 1C and 2C. NMC811/graphite retained approximately 99.7% of its initial discharge capacity at 0.3C and 1C, decreasing to 72.3% at 2C and 63.5% at 3C, while LCO/graphite retained only 11.1% at 2C. NRA revealed maximum lithium accumulation in graphite anodes at 2C for both chemistries, supported by SEM observations of granular surface deposits. XRD showed rate-dependent shifts in cathode (003) and graphite (002) reflections, indicating structural changes during cycling. The lower lithium accumulation at 3C was associated with reduced charge throughput, while the LCO/graphite 3C condition was limited by its charging protocol. These findings highlight the importance of anode-side degradation and charging protocols in interpreting high-rate cycling performance of commercial lithium-ion batteries.

</details>


### [140] [A New High $γ$-$γ'$ Fraction Polycrystalline Co/Ni Superalloy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15969)
*Cameron Crabb,Mark C. Hardy,Michelle S. Conroy,David Dye*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种可进行铸锭加工成形的Co/Ni基γ/γ'高温合金，通过均匀化、热轧及后续热处理工艺，获得了50% γ'相和142 μm晶粒组织。该合金在700–850 °C具有高强度，在750/800 °C蠕变条件下优于现有变形Ni基合金，并表现出优异的抗氧化性与长期组织稳定性，适合航空发动机高温静、转动部件应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变形Ni基高温合金在强度、蠕变性能与工艺性之间的平衡存在不足，亟需开发一种既具备优异高温力学性能与抗氧化性，又能通过常规铸锭冶金工艺（VIM熔炼、均匀化、热轧）加工成形的Co/Ni基γ/γ'高温合金，以满足航空发动机高温部件需求。

Method: 制备8.5 kg VIM铸锭，经均匀化处理并热轧至真应变1；开发热处理工艺以获得50% γ'相和142 μm晶粒。随后进行高温拉伸测试（700/800/850 °C）、蠕变测试（750 °C/600 MPa与800 °C/300 MPa）、800 °C/100 h氧化试验及800 °C/8000 h长时间时效，并对显微组织与相稳定性进行表征。

Result: 高温拉伸屈服强度分别为820 MPa（700 °C）、785 MPa（800 °C）和783 MPa（850 °C）。蠕变性能方面，最小蠕变速率降低、持久寿命延长，优于现有变形Ni基合金。800 °C/100 h氧化形成约1 μm的双层Cr₂O₃/Al₂O₃氧化膜，亚表层退化极小。800 °C/8000 h长期时效后γ'相粗化有限，且无有害金属间化合物或晶界相析出。

Conclusion: 该Co/Ni基γ/γ'合金在可加工性、强度、蠕变性能与抗氧化性之间实现了良好的综合平衡，适用于航空发动机高温静子和转子部件，是一种有前景的新型变形高温合金。

Abstract: An ingot-processable Co/Ni-based $γ/γ'$ superalloy has been developed for wrought parts in aircraft engines. An 8.5 kg VIM ingot was homogenised and hot rolled to a true strain of 1. A heat treatment was then developed to produce 50% $γ'$ and a grain size of 142 $μ$m. High-temperature tensile testing showed yield strengths of 820 MPa at 700 $^\circ$C, 785 MPa at 800 $^\circ$C and 783 MPa at 850 $^\circ$C. Creep tests at 750 $^\circ$C/600 MPa and 800 $^\circ$C/300 MPa demonstrated reduced minimum creep rates and extended rupture lives relative to current wrought Ni-base alloys. Oxidation at 800 $^\circ$C for 100 h formed a 1 $μ$m dual chromia/alumina scale with minimal subsurface degradation. Long-term exposure at 800 $^\circ$C for 8000 h showed limited $γ'$ coarsening and no deleterious intermetallic or grain boundary phases. The alloy therefore offers a promising balance of processability, strength and oxidation resistance for high-temperature static and rotating components.or grain boundary phases. The alloy therefore offers a promising balance of processability, strength and oxidation resistance for high-temperature static and rotating components.

</details>


### [141] [Quantifying sample heating by ablation-plasma x-rays in laser-driven shock experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16054)
*R. F. Smith,I. K. OCampo,S. Singh,T. M. Hutchinson,T. E. Lockard,J. H. Eggert,C. P. McGuire,A. L. Coleman,J. M. McNaney,S. Pandolfi,B. Nagler,D. McGonegle,H. J. Lee,D. Khaghani,N. Czapla,A. Krygier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过独特实验设计（样品先于压力波到达前被加热），利用原位X射线衍射量化了激光驱动动态压缩实验中消融等离子体X射线对样品的预热效应，比较了不同厚度聚酰亚胺烧蚀层及Al闪镀层的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光驱动动态压缩实验中，消融等离子体发射的X射线导致样品预热，这一未知因素长期困扰着主Hugoniot等确定热力学状态实验的解读，因为压力驱动与样品加热同时发生、难以分离。

Method: 采用一种独特的实验设计，使样品在压力波到达之前先被X射线加热，再通过原位X射线衍射将1微米厚Au样品的晶格膨胀转化为温度，系统考察了常用的25微米和75微米厚Kapton-B（聚酰亚胺）烧蚀层在不同激光辐照度下的加热行为，并测试了0.2微米Al闪镀层的增强效应。

Result: 对于25微米厚聚酰亚胺烧蚀层，观察到足以熔化Au的加热（温升约1050 K），且0.2微米Al闪镀层显著增强该加热；而75微米厚、无Al涂层的Kapton-B则强烈抑制加热，在高达1.2×10^13 W/cm^2的激光辐照度下，2.5微米厚Ti和1微米厚Fe样品中均未观测到可测量的加热。

Conclusion: X射线预热程度强烈依赖于烧蚀层的厚度与材料成分：更厚且无Al涂层的聚酰亚胺烧蚀层可有效抑制预热，而薄烧蚀层或Al闪镀层会显著增强加热，为激光驱动压缩实验选择烧蚀层以减少预热不确定性提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Sample preheat due to absorption of x-rays emitted by an ablation plasma has long been a source of uncertainty in laser-driven dynamic compression experiments. This unknown heating complicates interpretation of experiments designed to reach well-defined thermodynamic states, such as the principal Hugoniot, particularly because the pressure drive and sample heating occur simultaneously and are difficult to disentangle. Here, we quantify sample heating from x-rays using a unique experimental design in which the sample is heated prior to the arrival of the pressure wave. Using in situ x-ray diffraction, we relate lattice expansion to temperature within a 1-um-thick Au sample for commonly used 25-um and 75-um-thick Kapton-B (polyimide) ablators over a range of laser irradiances. Heating, sufficient to melt Au (Delta T ~ 1050 K), is observed for 25-um-thick polyimide ablators, which is significantly enhanced by the addition of a 0.2-um Al flash coating. In contrast, heating is strongly suppressed for 75-um-thick, non-Al-coated Kapton-B, with no measurable heating observed in 2.5-um-thick Ti or 1-um-thick Fe samples at laser irradiances up to 1.2 x 10^13 W/cm^2. .

</details>


### [142] [Tunable Long-Range Magnetic Exchange in Adatom-Decorated Transition Metal Dichalcogenide Monolayers: An ab initio Embedding Model Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16085)
*Xiaohang Jia,Ryan Weiss,Yusong Bai,Brenda Rubenstein*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究金属性单层过渡金属二硫化物(TMD)上的磁性吸附原子，以TaS₂上的Fe吸附原子为例，揭示RKKY间接交换耦合的原子尺度振荡及其对应变的可控响应，为可编程磁相与可重构自旋电子学提供平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维材料中工程化磁相互作用，有望实现可编程磁相和可重构自旋电子学功能，因此需要一种可通过吸附原子排布和应变来调控间接交换耦合的平台。

Method: 采用DFT+U计算金属性单层TaS₂上Fe吸附原子对在中间距离区间的交换耦合，揭示其原子尺度振荡及双轴应变效应；进一步建立第一性原理嵌入模型，复现DFT计算的原子对交换，并通过受控对比识别RKKY介导耦合为主要机制。

Result: DFT+U显示交换耦合在中间距离区间呈现原子尺度振荡，且双轴应变可调控耦合强度；嵌入模型复现了DFT结果并确认了显著的RKKY贡献，受控对比表明RKKY介导耦合是交换耦合应变可调性的主要起源。

Conclusion: 将吸附原子排布与应变相结合，可在金属性单层TMD中工程化可重构磁相互作用，为可编程磁性器件提供可行途径。

Abstract: Engineering magnetic interactions in two-dimensional materials offers a promising route toward programmable magnetic phases and reconfigurable spintronic functionalities. Here, we investigate magnetic adatoms on metallic monolayer transition-metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) as a platform for tunable indirect exchange, focusing on Fe adatoms on monolayer TaS$_2$. In the intermediate-distance regime, DFT+U calculations reveal atomic-scale oscillations in the exchange coupling between two Fe adatoms and show that biaxial strain tunes the coupling. We develop a first-principles embedding model that reproduces the DFT-calculated pair exchange and establishes a substantial RKKY contribution to the exchange; controlled comparisons further identify RKKY-mediated coupling as the primary origin of the exchange's strain tunability. These results point to combining adatom arrangement with strain as a means of engineering reconfigurable magnetic interactions in metallic monolayer TMDs.

</details>


### [143] [Uncertainty Aware AFM-IR Hyperspectral Cube Reconstruction via Convolutional-Attentive Conditional Neural Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16118)
*William F. Kent,Roberto does Reis,Gajendra Shekhawat,Emma Alexander,Vinayak P. Dravid*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种卷积-注意力条件神经过程（CACNP），用稀疏采样光谱和少量结构图像重建AFM-IR高光谱立方体，仅需5%光谱即可准确重建，推理时间小于0.1秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFM-IR能以纳米空间分辨率绘制化学组成，但逐谱采集耗时，限制了高光谱成像的吞吐量，亟需一种能加速数据采集与重建的方法。

Method: 设计卷积-注意力条件神经过程（CACNP）架构，从选定波数获取的稀疏采样光谱与少量结构图像中重建AFM-IR高光谱立方体。模型完全由实验红外光谱合成的立方体训练，同时预测光谱强度及其不确定性。

Result: 在嵌入环氧树脂的PMMA和PS颗粒实验数据上，仅用5%可用光谱即实现准确重建，与其他方法相比性能具有竞争力；128×128高光谱立方体推理时间小于0.1秒。

Conclusion: 预测不确定性为局部化学特征的自适应采样提供依据，推动自主、高通量AFM-IR工作流程的发展，并为加速其他高光谱成像方法提供了可借鉴的架构。

Abstract: Atomic force microscopy-infrared spectroscopy (AFM-IR) maps chemical composition at nanoscale spatial resolution, but the time required to acquire individual spectra limits hyperspectral imaging. Here, we introduce a convolutional-attentive conditional neural process (CACNP), a neural process architecture for reconstructing AFM-IR hyperspectral cubes from sparsely sampled spectra and a small set of structure images, each acquired at a selected wavenumber. The model is trained entirely on synthetic cubes generated from experimental infrared spectra and predicts both spectral intensities and their associated uncertainty. Validation on experimental datasets of poly(methyl methacrylate) and polystyrene particles embedded in epoxy demonstrates accurate reconstruction using only 5% of the available spectra, with competitive performance when compared to other methods. Inference takes less than 0.1 s on 128 X 128 hyperspectral cubes, supporting rapid reconstruction updates, while predictive uncertainty provides a basis for adaptive sampling of localized chemical features. This approach advances autonomous, high-throughput AFM-IR workflows and offers an architecture for accelerating other hyperspectral imaging methods.

</details>


### [144] [Direct imaging of plasmonic coupling between gold nanoparticles and one-dimensional van der Waals materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16132)
*Ibrahim Tanriover,Arindom Nag,Jong-Ho Lee,Huy Hoang,Haihua Liu,Thang Pham*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文将等离子体金属/范德华材料杂化体系从二维拓展到一维，首次在Au纳米颗粒与一维vdW材料NbS3的接触界面直接测量近场，并建立原子结构与纳米尺度等离子体响应相关联的多模态表征方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往等离子体金属与vdW材料的杂化研究局限于二维层状材料，且金属/vdW界面的近场通常只能通过远场测量间接推断，缺乏对一维vdW体系及其界面近场的直接表征。

Method: 采用盐辅助气-液-固生长法在石墨烯覆盖的TEM网格上制备NbS3纳米线与纳米带；利用原子分辨电子显微镜表征原子级锐利的Au/NbS3界面；通过1030 nm光子诱导近场电子显微镜（PINEM）直接测量Au-NbS3接触处的近场分布；结合时域有限差分（FDTD）模拟，量化近场对颗粒-NbS3间隙、NbS3厚度及结构尺寸比的依赖关系；并用特定尺寸的Au纳米颗粒进行罗丹明6G拉曼散射增强测试。

Result: 与NbS3接触的Au颗粒产生的近场强度更高、范围更广，且接触热点仅在入射偏振平行于颗粒-NbS3轴线时被激发；FDTD模拟复现了实验观察；尺寸特定的Au纳米颗粒使罗丹明6G的拉曼散射增强1.8-4.2倍（相对于单独Au颗粒），检测限达10^-7 M。

Conclusion: 该工作将等离子体金属/vdW杂化体系拓展至一维vdW晶体，并建立了将金属/vdW界面原子结构与其纳米尺度等离子体响应相关联的多模态方法。

Abstract: Hybrids of plasmonic metals and van der Waals (vdW) materials combine electromagnetic field enhancement with the electronic properties of the vdW component, but the studies reported so far concern two-dimensional layers, and the near field at the metal/vdW interface is generally inferred from far-field measurements. Here we report the plasmonic coupling between Au nanoparticles and a one-dimensional (1D) vdW material, NbS3, and measure the near field at the Au-NbS3 contact directly. NbS3 nanowires and nanoribbons were grown by salt-assisted vapor-liquid-solid growth on graphene-covered TEM grids, and atomic-resolution electron microscopy shows atomically sharp Au/NbS3 interfaces. Photon-induced near-field electron microscopy at 1030 nm shows that an Au particle in contact with NbS3 produces a near field of higher intensity and larger extent than an isolated particle of similar size, and that the hot spot at a contact is excited only when the incident polarization is parallel to the particle-NbS3 axis. Finite-difference time-domain simulations reproduce these observations and quantify the dependence of the coupling on the particle-NbS3 gap, the NbS3 thickness, and the dimension ratio of the NbS3 structure. Size-specific Au nanoparticles on NbS3 nanowires enhance the Raman scattering of rhodamine 6G by a factor of 1.8-4.2 relative to Au nanoparticles alone, with a detection limit of 10^-7 M. These results extend plasmonic metal/vdW hybrids to 1D vdW crystals and establish a multimodal approach that correlates the atomic structure of a metal/vdW interface with its nanoscale plasmonic response.

</details>


### [145] [Humidity-modulated Ostwald Step Rule in $\rm FAPbI_3$ Martensitic Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16147)
*Ping Tuo,Ju Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用基于从头算机器学习势的大规模原子模拟，作者再现了湿度升高导致 FAPbI₃ 中 δ→α 转变温度升高的实验现象，并发现沿 Burgers 转变路径存在一个对水有强亲和力的层状亚稳中间相；该中间相与 δ 相之间对水的竞争性吸收决定了转变动力学的湿度依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验观察到水分在 FAPbI₃ 相变中具有双重作用：制造时的高温高湿提升钙钛矿 α 相薄膜质量，而低温服役时湿度加速 α 相降解为光惰性的 δ 相。尽管实验证据丰富，但水分调控相稳定性与转变路径的微观机制尚不清楚，亟需从原子尺度加以阐明。

Method: 作者采用基于从头算机器学习势的大规模原子模拟，系统研究水分对 FAPbI₃ δ↔α 相变的影响，重点解析 Burgers 转变路径中的中间相结构及其与水的相互作用，并考察湿度对转变温度与转变动力学的作用。

Result: 模拟成功再现了湿度升高导致 δ→α 转变温度升高的实验现象；揭示沿 Burgers 转变路径存在一个层状亚稳中间相，该中间相具有强亲水性，它与 δ 相对水分的竞争性吸收决定了转变动力学的湿度依赖行为。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了水分在钙钛矿相变中的双重作用机制，并证明环境物种可通过稳定亚稳中间相来重塑相变路径，为奥斯特瓦尔德分步规则提供了依赖环境的新视角。

Abstract: Experimental observations reveal a dual role of moisture in $\rm FAPbI_3$ phase transitions. During fabrication at high temperatures, humidity improves the quality of the perovskite ($α$ phase) films, whereas at low service temperatures, humidity accelerates degradation of the photoactive $α$ phase into the photo-inactive hexagonal $δ$ phase. Despite extensive experimental evidence, the microscopic mechanisms by which moisture modulates phase stability and transition pathways remain unknown. Here, using large-scale atomistic simulations powered by an ab initio machine-learned potential, we reproduce the experimentally observed increase in the $δ$-to-$α$ transition temperature under increased humidity. Our simulations reveal a layered metastable intermediate along the Burgers transition pathway that exhibits a strong affinity for water. The competing water uptake of this intermediate and the $δ$ phase governs the humidity dependence of transition kinetics. These findings elucidate the dual role of moisture in perovskite phase transitions and demonstrate how environmental species can stabilize metastable intermediates and reshape transition pathways, providing an environment-dependent perspective on Ostwald's step rule.

</details>


### [146] [Studying temperature-induced decrease of spontaneous polarization in PbTiO$_3$ from vibrational amplitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16266)
*Zhikai Gao,Yuanji Xu,Fuyang Tian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理分子动力学模拟，提出一种投影振动振幅的方法，定量表征PbTiO$_3$中TiO$_6$八面体随温度的畸变，揭示了其铁电极化诱导的负热膨胀（NTE）源于八面体旋转的特定振动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PbTiO$_3$是典型的铁电极化诱导负热膨胀材料，其TiO$_6$八面体常用于研究极化与NTE，但此前缺乏对其畸变的定量表征手段。

Method: 基于第一性原理分子动力学（AIMD）模拟，提出一种投影振动振幅的方法来定量描述原子随温度的各向异性畸变；并结合势能面（随振幅变化）与单个原子受力进行分析。

Result: 发现巨大的负模Grüneisen系数主要来源于四方相沿c方向的两个边界对称点上TiO$_6$八面体旋转的不同振动模式；势能面分析表明c轴压缩有利于TiO$_6$八面体旋转；绕负膨胀轴旋转的TiO$_6$八面体振动模式能促进自发极化随温度升高而降低，从而诱发PbTiO$_3$由四方相向立方相转变。

Conclusion: TiO$_6$八面体绕负膨胀轴的旋转振动模式通过促进自发极化降低，驱动PbTiO$_3$发生四方—立方相变，并解释了其负热膨胀及大的负模Grüneisen系数的微观起源。

Abstract: PbTiO$_3$ is a typical ferroelectric polarization induced negative thermal expansion (NTE) material at a wide range of temperature. The elegant TiO$_6$ octahedron is used to study the polarization and NTE, whereas lacking its quantitative characterization. In this work, we propose a concise projection of vibrational amplitude to describe anisotropic distortion of atoms with temperature based on $ab$ $initio$ molecular dynamics simulations. We find that the huge negative mode Grüneisen coefficient is mainly from the different vibrational modes of the TiO$_6$ octahedral rotation at two boundary symmetry points in tetragonal phase along $c$ direction. The potential energy surface as a function of amplitude suggests the $c$-axis compression facilitates the TiO$_6$ octahedral rotation. We show that the vibrational mode of TiO$_6$ octahedral rotation around negative-expansion axis can promote spontaneous polarization reduction with increasing temperature, making transformation of PbTiO$_3$ from tetragonal to cubic phase occur, via the projected amplitude, potential energy surface, and forces of individual atoms.

</details>


### [147] [Optically-detected room-temperature spin-coherence from the GaV$^-$ in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16281)
*Adam D. Dodson,Tiamike Dudley,Luca Basso,Leopold Diaz,Jesse J. Lutz,Raj Patel,Matthew Markham,Jacob Henshaw,Andrew M. Mounce*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了新发现的带负电镓空位（GaV⁻）缺陷在金刚石中的物理性质，该缺陷兼具 NV⁻ 色心与 IV 族空位色心（G4V's）的诸多优点，其室温自旋相干、高 Debye-Waller 因子、短辐射寿命及电荷噪声防护等特性使其成为量子传感、网络与计算的重要资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态量子缺陷是量子信息科学中最有前景的平台之一，但金刚石中主流的 NV⁻ 色心易受电荷噪声影响且零声子线发射弱，而 IV 族空位（G4V's）则需要低温才能工作。因此亟需寻找一种兼具两者优势的新型量子缺陷。

Method: 作者通过聚焦离子束（FIB）注入和高压高温（HPHT）退火工艺在金刚石中制备出新发现的 GaV⁻ 缺陷，并系统表征其光学与自旋物理性质，包括自旋相干时间、Debye-Waller 因子、辐射寿命、零场分裂的温度依赖性以及对称性等。

Result: GaV⁻ 在室温下具有光学可探测的自旋相干性（T₂(293K)=1.06 μs），Debye-Waller 因子约 0.4，辐射寿命约 3.83 ns，零场分裂的温度依赖性是 NV⁻ 的 16 倍，且具有可抵御电荷噪声的 D₃d 对称性；同时该缺陷在低温下性能进一步提升。

Conclusion: GaV⁻ 同时集合了 NV⁻ 与 G4V's 的最佳特性，在室温自旋相干、光学稳定性和抗电荷噪声方面表现优异，有望成为量子传感、量子网络和量子计算领域的关键资源。

Abstract: Solid-state quantum defects (point crystal imperfections with optically addressable spins) are among the most promising platforms for quantum information science due to their long-lived electron and nuclear spin lifetimes. The negatively-charged nitrogen-vacancy (NV$^-$) and negatively-charged group IV vacancies (G4V's) in diamond are some the most promising quantum defects but, the NV$^-$ is highly susceptible to charge noise and has low emission from its zero-phonon line while the G4V's require low operating temperatures. Here we report the physical properties of the newly discovered negatively-charged gallium-vacancy in diamond, GaV$^-$. We find that the GaV$^-$, created through FIB implantation and high-pressure high-temperature annealing, exhibits a combination of the best properties of both the NV$^-$ and G4V's: optically-detectable spin coherence at room temperature ($T_2(293K) = 1.06 μ$s), a large Debye-Waller factor $\sim$0.4, a short radiative lifetime ($\sim$3.83 ns), strong temperature dependence of the zero-field splitting (16x that of the NV$^-$), and charge-noise-protected D$_{3d}$ symmetry. These features (along with improved performance at low temperature) position the newly discovered GaV$^-$ as a key resource for quantum sensing, networking, and computation.

</details>


### [148] [ORCHID-SAGE: Fully Reproducible CALPHAD Assessment from Evidence to Phase Diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16308)
*Raymundo Arróyave*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出 ORCHID-SAGE 标准，将 CALPHAD 热力学数据库评估过程中的人类专家判断记录为正式、可复现、可自动化的协议，使数据库不仅能提供参数，还能记录其构建过程，从而支持数据库的再生与自动更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料设计的首要需求是相稳定性知识，CALPHAD 方法构建的无机材料（尤其是合金）热力学数据库是预测新材料合成条件的关键能力。然而，构建数据库的许多步骤依赖专家判断，且这些判断很少被记录，导致他人只能使用已发布的数据库，却无法重新生成或更新它。现有机器虽已能采集数据、用机器学习原子间势建模型并对照实验精修，但将各部分整合为可靠数据库的“评估”环节仍由人类专家完成。

Method: 作者认为，只有将评估背后的专家知识编码为形式化协议，评估才能被可复现地自动化，并同时交付参数与构建记录。为此提出 ORCHID-SAGE 标准，将这一记录组织成可复现、可自动化的协议。作为首次尝试，该方法应用于 Cr-Ni 体系，验证了三种再生方式：(1) 在另一台机器上通过脚本根据记录重新生成数据库；(2) 由仅通过模型上下文协议（MCP）工作的 AI 智能体重新生成；(3) 仅从公开来源重新拟合，三者均得到相同预测。

Result: 在 Cr-Ni 体系上，数据库成功从其记录中由脚本、AI 智能体（经 MCP）以及仅用公开数据的重新拟合三种途径再生，并得到一致的预测结果，证明了 ORCHID-SAGE 标准能实现可复现、可自动化的数据库构建与再生。

Conclusion: 通过将专家判断以正式记录形式编码，数据库不再需要固定在发表时的状态，而可由 AI 智能体在自驱动实验室及其他来源提供新证据时持续更新；同时，判断权仍保留在人类手中并留存在记录中。

Abstract: Knowledge of phase stability is the first requirement for materials design, and predicting the conditions under which new materials can be synthesized has become a key capability of self-driving laboratories (SDLs). For inorganic materials, notably alloys, these predictions come from thermodynamic databases built with the CALPHAD method. Building a database, however, requires expert judgment at many steps, and because these decisions are rarely recorded, others can use a database but cannot regenerate or update it. Machines can already collect the data, build a first model with machine-learned interatomic potentials and refine it against experiments. The assessment itself, the sequence of judgments that turns these parts into a reliable database, is still carried out by human experts. We argue that it can be automated reproducibly only if the expert knowledge behind an assessment is encoded in formal protocols, so that an assessment delivers a record of its construction and not only its parameters. We introduce ORCHID-SAGE, a standard that organizes this record into reproducible and automatable protocols. In this first attempt, applied to Cr-Ni, the database was regenerated from its record by a script on a second machine, by an AI agent working only through the Model Context Protocol (MCP) and by a refit from public sources only, with the same predictions. Recorded in this way, a database need no longer be fixed at publication but can be updated by AI agents as SDLs and other sources provide new evidence, while the judgment remains with people and on the record.

</details>


### [149] [Dynamic Tunneling a Prior Phenomenon to Intermediate Resistive States in Defect Engineered Mn-SrTiO3 RRAM Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16380)
*Gowtham Velpandi,Ishaan Tushir,Durairaj Murugan,Thanumalayaperumal Thirunavukarasu,Ranjith Ramadurai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了2%Mn掺杂SrTiO3(PLD制备)的RRAM器件，通过缺陷工程引入带负电的Mn/Ti缺陷与带正电的氧空位，实现双向导电细丝的形成，获得免成型双极阻变特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RRAM阻变依赖导电细丝形成，往往需要成型(forming)过程且细丝行为不稳定。作者希望通过缺陷工程同时引入带负电(Mn3+相关)与带正电(氧空位)的缺陷，使两条细丝从两电极相向生长，从而增强阻变特性、实现免成型切换与多态存储。

Method: 采用脉冲激光沉积(PLD)制备2%Mn掺杂SrTiO3器件；通过缺陷工程构建带负电的Mn3+、Mn、Ti缺陷与单/双正电氧空位；利用XPS对比切换前后电极附近的缺陷型细丝；分析SET/RESET过程中导电细丝形成前后出现的动态隧穿中间态及其电流尖峰。

Result: 器件表现出免成型的双极阻变，ON/OFF比超过10^2，在0.1V低读电压下保持时间超过10^3秒；切换由氧空位与Mn变价负缺陷协同形成的导电细丝主导；在SET区细丝形成前和RESET区细丝断裂后观察到动态隧穿过程，产生微小电流尖峰，呈现可用于多态存储的中间阻态。

Conclusion: Mn-STO器件通过缺陷工程实现导电细丝的可控形成，兼具可靠的保持特性与单/多态存储潜力；动态隧穿中间态的存在为多级存储应用提供了新途径。

Abstract: Resistive random-access memory devices work under the principle of resistive switching from high resistive state to low resistive state under applied electric field. In this work, 2%Mn doped SrTiO3, devices were fabricated using PLD in which, the defects are engineered such that the conducting filament formation is expected to have two oppositely charged defects: a) presence of Mn3+ dopants, giving rise to a negatively charged Mn and Ti and b) single and double positive oxygen vacancies. This process of two filaments emanating from both the electrodes has shown enhancement in the resistive switching characteristics aiding in forming-free switching that exhibits over 102 as ON/OFF ratio with a memory retention beyond 103 seconds at a low read voltage of 0.1V. All devices demonstrated a stable bipolar resistive switching dominated by conduction filament formation resulting from a synergic effect of oxygen vacancies and the varying oxidation states of Mn based negative defects that propagates toward each other under bias. The formation of defect-based filaments around the bias electrode was evidently observed using XPS of the devices before and after switching. A dynamic tunnelling process is observed as an intermediate process before the conduction filament forms in the SET region and following the snapping in the RESET region. Conduction filaments being heavily doped localizes conduction pathways with a small finite tunnelling barrier gap that are instantaneously formed and ruptures during switching cycles. The dynamic tunnelling behavior gives rise to a tiny spike in current and is observed at the intermediate resistive states which are potential for multi-state usage of these devices. Overall, Mn-STO devices of varying thicknesses show promising retention features and defect engineering offers control over single and/or multi-states memory.

</details>


### [150] [Multi-reference variational states resolve stripe selection and local $d$-wave pairing in the Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16430)
*Honghui Shang,Jinlong Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用多参考神经变分辅助场量子蒙特卡罗(MR-nVAFQMC)方法，在二维Hubbard模型(U/t=8、晶格至32×32)中研究条纹与d波超导的竞争关系。结果表明，半填充反相畴壁能产生局域d波配对，但仅抑制静态条纹序不足以在条纹间建立可分辨的相干涉超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维Hubbard模型中条纹是竞争d波超导、还是通过涨落促进配对，这一问题长期以来悬而未决。作者旨在厘清条纹(尤其是反相畴壁)与d波配对之间的内在关系，并判断超导是否源于条纹涨落。

Method: 采用多参考神经变分辅助场量子蒙特卡罗(MR-nVAFQMC)方法，在U/t=8下将不同条纹周期与畴壁位置上未配对Slater行列式叠加，变分能量在32×32周期晶格上达到与领先基准相当的水平。针对铜氧化物次近邻跃迁(t'/t=-0.2)，研究对角跃迁如何保持电子掺杂反铁磁性并受挫空穴运动形成Yamada条纹；在1/8空穴掺杂下分析空穴跨反相自旋边界的退局域化效应，比较填充条纹与半填充畴壁，并检验相干移动畴壁对静态电荷调制与短程配对的影响，以及在不同条纹周期与反铁磁畴间的配对关联衰减。

Result: 在1/8空穴掺杂下，空穴跨反相自旋边界的退局域化压倒在位排斥，将纯模型的填充条纹劈裂为强局域配对的半填充畴壁；相干移动畴壁强烈抑制静态电荷调制但短程配对保持不变，表明局域配对是半填充畴壁的内禀属性而非条纹涨落驱动；然而跨反铁磁畴时，配对关联在一个条纹周期及更远处变得无法分辨。参数不经重优化迁移到更大晶格以及深度基准检查点均保留条纹自旋序，但在不确定范围内未解析出长程配对，仅允许弱超导。

Conclusion: 半填充反相畴壁产生局域d-wave库珀对，而在所研究的态中，仅抑制静态条纹序不足以在条纹间产生可分辨的相干(长程)超导。

Abstract: Whether stripes compete with $d$-wave superconductivity or promote pairing through fluctuations remains unsettled in the two-dimensional Hubbard model. Using multi-reference neural variational auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo (MR-nVAFQMC) at $U/t=8$, we superpose unpaired Slater determinants across stripe periods and domain-wall positions, reaching variational energies competitive with leading benchmarks on periodic lattices up to $32\times32$ sites. For cuprate next-nearest-neighbor hopping ($t'/t=-0.2$), diagonal hopping preserves electron-doped antiferromagnetism while kinetically frustrating hole motion into Yamada stripes with a dome of short-range connected $d$-wave pairing. At $1/8$ hole doping, hole delocalization across antiphase spin boundaries outweighs on-site repulsion, splitting the filled stripes of the pure model into half-filled walls with strong local pairing. Coherently delocalizing shifted walls strongly suppresses static charge modulation yet leaves short-range pairing unchanged, showing that local pairing is intrinsic to half-filled walls rather than driven by stripe fluctuations. Across intervening antiferromagnetic domains, however, pairing correlations become unresolved at one stripe period and beyond. Both parameter transfer to larger lattices without reoptimization and deep benchmark checkpoints retain stripe spin order without resolving long-range pairing, within uncertainties allowing weak superconductivity. Half-filled antiphase walls thus generate local $d$-wave pairs, whereas suppressing static stripe order alone yields no resolved coherence across stripes in the states studied.

</details>


### [151] [Edge-scaling softening of a universal machine-learned potential: point-defect formation energies in a coronene graphene quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16528)
*Andrii Tuzhykov,Vladimir Soloviev,Arnold Kiv,Andrii Bielinskyi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文以心形石墨烯量子点冠烯为基准，系统比较通用机器学习原子间势SevenNet与自旋极化PBE DFT在单空位、双空位及Stone-Wales缺陷上的能量表现，发现SevenNet系统性低估空位形成能，且低估幅度随欠配位程度增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势常因训练数据中高能、远离平衡构型不足而出现"系统性软化"，但其在石墨烯量子点欠配位边缘位点（空位具有显著分子自由基特征）上的具体表现尚不清楚，亟需定量基准评估以判断其在高通量筛选中的可靠性。

Method: 以具有D6h对称性和三类碳位点的冠烯（C24H12）为模型体系，将SevenNet（7net-omni-i12）与自旋极化PBE DFT（GPAW）对标；除了完整的位点与键分辨能量景观外，还进行了受控的"匹配重构"对比，在自洽化学势储库条件下用两种引擎对28个空位与双空位构型及Stone-Wales缺陷打分，并利用D6h等价位点的对称性离散度作为诊断指标。

Result: SevenNet低估了全部28个空位与双空位形成能：单空位平均低估0.30–0.74 eV，双空位为0.65–1.11 eV，且低估幅度随断裂边缘C-H键数目（欠配位程度）增加而增大；而Stone-Wales平均能量差异仅0.013 eV。SevenNet保持了三个依赖氢的重构盆地的排序，并将D6h等价位点复现到数meV以内。

Conclusion: 在冠烯基准中，MLIP可用于结构筛选与初步排序，而绝对空位能量及多盆地边缘构型仍需DFT验证；类内对称性离散度可作为盆地对弛豫敏感性的实用诊断指标。

Abstract: Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are increasingly used as computationally efficient surrogates for density functional theory (DFT) in high-throughput nanomaterials screening. Systematic softening has been reported for several universal MLIPs when high-energy and far-from-equilibrium configurations are underrepresented in training data. We examine how this behavior appears at under-coordinated edge sites in graphene quantum dots (GQDs), where a vacancy can have pronounced molecular-radical character. Using coronene (C$_{24}$H$_{12}$), a hexagonal GQD with $D_{6h}$ symmetry and three carbon classes, we benchmark SevenNet (7net-omni-i12) against spin-polarized PBE DFT (GPAW) for monovacancy, divacancy, and Stone-Wales defects. Beyond the full site- and bond-resolved landscapes, we perform a controlled matched-reconstruction comparison in which 28 vacancy and divacancy configurations are scored by both engines using self-consistent chemical-potential reservoirs. In this dataset, SevenNet underestimates all 28 vacancy and divacancy formation energies, and the mean deficit increases with under-coordination within each defect family: 0.30-0.74 eV for monovacancies and 0.65-1.11 eV for divacancies as the number of broken edge C-H bonds increases. By contrast, the mean Stone-Wales energies differ by only 0.013 eV. SevenNet also preserves the ordering of three hydrogen-dependent reconstruction basins and reproduces $D_{6h}$-equivalent sites to within a few meV. The within-class symmetry spread therefore provides a practical diagnostic of basin-sensitive relaxations. Within the present coronene benchmark, these results support MLIP-based structure screening and preliminary ranking, while absolute vacancy energies and multi-basin edge configurations still require DFT validation.

</details>


### [152] [Bulk CMSX-4 Single-Crystal Builds via Laser-Directed Energy Deposition: Epitaxial Stability and Microstructure Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16535)
*Divya Nalajala,Swapnil Bhure,R. Ajithkannan,Shubhang Galagali,Abhik Choudhury*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文针对激光定向能量沉积（L-DED）制备镍基高温合金单晶的难题，提出结合原位红外热反馈动态调激光功率与仿真引导外部冷却系统的策略，将连续外延生长高度提升至仅靠工艺控制时的近两倍，并优化了固溶热处理工艺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L-DED单晶制备涉及众多工艺参数的复杂交互，极难实现长圆柱棒状几何体的持续单晶生长，需要通过优化热量提取来维持凝固界面近稳态条件并延长外延生长。

Method: 结合原位红外热反馈动态调整激光功率，并辅以仿真引导的外部冷却系统，实现增材制造过程中的最优热量提取；通过EBSD验证单晶生长，利用一次枝晶臂间距评估凝固稳态性，并缩短固溶时间以优化热处理。

Result: 该策略实现了连续单晶生长，外延生长高度提升至仅靠工艺控制时的近两倍；一次枝晶臂间距近恒定表明凝固近似稳态；固溶时间缩短47%，加速共晶溶解，时效后析出相分布与传统Bridgman法和L-DED样品相似。

Conclusion: 通过热反馈动态调功率与外部冷却相结合，可有效维持L-DED长圆柱棒的单晶生长并提升可制备高度，同时优化热处理工艺，使L-DED样品析出相分布可与经典Bridgman法制备的样品相媲美。

Abstract: Single-crystal processing of Ni-based superalloys using laser-directed energy deposition (L-DED) is extremely challenging, given the interplay of a number of processing parameters. In this work, we develop strategies to maintain single-crystal growth in long cylindrical rod geometries by combining dynamic adjustment of laser power, informed by in-situ infrared thermal feedback, with a simulation-guided external cooling system. This combined approach ensures optimal heat extraction during additive manufacturing, maintaining near-steady-state conditions at the solidification interface and extending continuous epitaxial growth to nearly twice the build height achievable through process control alone. The developed strategy results in continuous single-crystal growth, as validated by electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD). The steady-state nature of solidification is revealed through the near-constant primary dendritic arm spacing. Further, the heat-treatment is optimized by reducing the solutionizing time (by 47\%), resulting in faster eutectic dissolution. Comparison of the aged microstructures reveals a similar distribution of precipitates between the classical Bridgman- and L-DED-processed samples.

</details>


### [153] [Tailored magnetic domain-wall ratchet effects through density gradients in triangular-hole arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16735)
*Julia Herrero-Albillos,María Ángeles Laguna-Marco,María Vélez,Xabier Zubizarreta-Iriarte,Daniel Arechavaleta,Aurelio Hierro-Rodriguez,Carlos Quirós,Michael Foerster,Cristina Bran,Arantxa Fraile-Rodriguez,José Ignacio Martín*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究非晶 Co₀.₈₆Zr₀.₁₄ 薄膜中密度梯度三角形孔阵列对磁畴壁动力学与形貌的调控，发现密度梯度可增强或逆转几何棘轮效应，实现畴壁反向传播并显著降低退钉扎场的随机涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性阵列中磁畴壁运动存在显著的随机涨落，难以实现确定性控制；作者旨在通过引入工程化密度梯度，探究其对畴壁传播方向、退钉扎场可重复性的影响，以实现确定性畴壁调控。

Method: 在非晶 Co₀.₈₆Zr₀.₁₄ 薄膜上制备密度梯度三角形孔阵列，采用克尔显微镜和高分辨 XMCD-PEEM 成像表征畴壁动力学与形貌；通过刚性畴壁模型和经验标度律（包含孔间距与阵列周期性）描述退钉扎场。

Result: 畴壁传播由孔取向、密度梯度与局域静磁相互作用共同决定：协同构型下密度梯度增强几何棘轮效应，竞争构型下两者相互抵消产生反向棘轮并实现畴壁反向传播。密度梯度阵列大幅降低退钉扎场的随机涨落，实现高度可重复的畴壁运动。

Conclusion: 工程化密度梯度是实现畴壁传播确定性控制的有效途径，在自旋电子学与神经形态器件中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: We investigate domain wall (DW) dynamics and morphology in amorphous Co$_{0.86}$Zr$_{0.14}$ thin films patterned with engineered, density-graded triangular-hole arrays. Using Kerr microscopy and high-resolution XMCD-PEEM imaging, we demonstrate that DW propagation is governed by the interplay between hole orientation, density gradients, and local magnetostatic interactions. In cooperative configurations, the density gradient reinforces the geometric ratchet effect, whereas in competing configurations, the two mechanisms oppose each other, leading to an inverse ratchet and enabling experimental access to backward DW propagation. Remarkably, density-graded arrays substantially reduce the stochastic variability of depinning fields observed in periodic arrays, resulting in highly reproducible DW motion. The depinning fields are described by a rigid DW model and an empirical scaling law incorporating both inter-hole spacing and array periodicity. These results demonstrate that engineered density gradients provide an effective route to deterministic control of DW propagation, with potential applications in spintronic and neuromorphic devices.

</details>


### [154] [Large Pockels Effect in Multiaxial Ferroelectrics Induced by Polarization Reorientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16773)
*Zhijun Jiang,Jinyang Ni,Hong Jian Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出，多轴铁电体中的极化重取向可通过创造结构软度来诱导大的线性电光（Pockels）效应，并从唯象理论与第一性原理计算两方面予以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有大线性电光（Pockels）效应的材料在低能耗光子器件中潜力巨大。以往策略多依赖与相界、畴壁或应变诱导相变相关的结构软度，本文旨在探索一条全新的途径——利用多轴铁电体的极化重取向来创造结构软度并增强Pockels效应。

Method: 作者建立唯象理论，发现在多轴铁电体中，极化方位角与电场之间存在滞回关系，且在合适的极化方位角处线性电光系数可能出现发散。进一步对单层SnSe和块体KNbO3等几种多轴铁电体开展第一性原理模拟，验证极化重取向对电光系数的增强效果。

Result: 唯象理论表明，极化方位角-电场关系呈滞回特性，线性电光系数在特定极化方位角处可能发散。第一性原理计算预测，极化重取向可显著增强单层SnSe和块体KNbO3的电光系数。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了所提策略的合理性，说明多轴铁电体是实现大Pockels效应的有前景平台，为大Pockels效应工程提供了全新思路。

Abstract: Materials with large linear electro-optic (Pockels) effect have significant potential for fabricating energy-efficient photonic devices. Previous strategies have mostly relied on structural softness---associated with phase boundary, domain wall, or strain induced phase transition---to create large Pockels effect in ferroelectrics. In this Letter, we show that polarization reorientation in multiaxial ferroelectrics may create structural softness and thus induce large Pockels effect. By developing a phenomenological theory, we find that multiaxial ferroelectrics exhibit a hysteretic relationship between the azimuth angles of the polarization and the electric field, and a possible divergence of the linear electro-optic coefficient may occur at an appropriate polarization azimuth angle. This suggests a brand-new strategy for engineering large Pockels effect in multiaxial ferroelectrics. We perform first-principles simulations on several multiaxial ferroelectrics and predict that polarization reorientation can significantly enhance the electro-optic coefficients in monolayer SnSe and bulk KNbO$_3$. This validates the soundness of our strategy and emphasizes multiaxial ferroelectrics as promising platforms toward large Pockels effect.

</details>


### [155] [Systematic Evaluation of Training Data Generation Strategies for Machine Learning-Based Molecular and Materials Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16792)
*Minh Nghia Nguyen,Beate Paulus,Akhil S. Nair*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了分子与材料性质预测中的多种训练数据获取策略（从随机采样到各类主动学习方法），发现不确定性采样总体表现最佳，且策略有效性高度依赖于模型、数据集与预测任务的相互作用，并据此给出在给定标注预算下选择数据获取策略的指导原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML模型通常在通过任意或通用采样策略构建的大型数据集上训练，这种策略可能导致与预测任务最相关的化学与材料空间区域被过度或不足表示。因此，核心挑战是如何高效地筛选训练数据，以在最小化标注样本数量的同时最大化预测性能。

Method: 系统比较了从随机采样到多种主动学习方法的一系列训练数据获取策略，并在不同模型类别、表示方式、数据集和目标性质上评估这些策略的性能表现。

Result: 数据获取策略的有效性强烈依赖于模型、数据集和预测任务之间的相互作用，其中基于不确定性的采样方法总体上优于其他替代策略。

Conclusion: 基于实验发现，论文提出了在给定标注预算下选择数据获取策略的指导原则，强调不确定性采样总体更优，但最优策略需结合具体的模型、数据集与任务来确定。

Abstract: Machine-learning (ML) models for molecular and materials property prediction are often trained on large datasets curated through arbitrary or generic sampling strategies. Such approaches can lead to the over- or under-representation of regions of chemical and materials space that are most relevant to the prediction task. An important challenge is therefore how to efficiently curate training data to maximize predictive performance while minimising the number of labelled examples required. Here, we systematically compare training data acquisition strategies for molecular and materials property prediction, ranging from random sampling to various active learning approaches. We evaluate their performance across different model classes, representations, datasets, and target properties. Our results show that the effectiveness of data acquisition strategies depend strongly on the interplay between the model, dataset, and prediction task, although uncertainty-based sampling generally outperforms alternative strategies. Based on these findings, we derive guidelines for selecting data acquisition strategies under a given labelling budget.

</details>


### [156] [Circular phonons generate negligible magnetization in insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17222)
*C-C. Wu,M. Buzzi,A. Iudica,R. Romero,G. De Vecchi,R. Merlin,A. Cavalleri*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文通过同时测量驱动SiC晶体内部的偏振旋转和外部杂散磁场，解决了"圆偏振声子是否伴随真实磁化"的争议，最终确定磁化可忽略不计（μ₀M < 0.001 T）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆偏振声子的光激发开辟了无平衡对应物的非平衡现象（如合成磁场、拓扑序调控、铁轴态操控），但学界对其是否伴随可观的"真实"磁化存在重大争议。此前基于探测驱动材料内偏振旋转的实验受到多种混淆因素干扰，包括非平衡条件下法拉第效应的解释、泵浦-探测实验中虚假的非线性光学贡献等，导致无法确定磁化的真实大小。

Method: 作者同时测量两个互补的物理量：一是驱动材料内部的偏振旋转（反映内部光学响应）；二是利用邻近的法拉第探测器测量驱动样品外部产生的杂散磁场（直接探测真实磁化）。实验采用5 MV/cm的中红外驱动场作用于SiC晶体，并将内部偏振旋转测量结果与外部磁杂散场测量结果进行对比、交叉验证。

Result: 在5 MV/cm中红外驱动下，观察到驱动SiC晶体内部出现巨大的偏振旋转，与先前报道一致；根据所作假设的不同，该旋转可对应高达μ₀M = 0.3 T或低至4 μT的磁化。然而，对驱动样品外部磁杂散场的测量确凿表明，SiC中的磁化可忽略不计（μ₀M < 0.001 T）。

Conclusion: 内部巨大的偏振旋转并非由真实磁化引起，而是源于非磁性的光学响应（如非线性光学效应或非平衡条件下的磁光效应解释不当）。通过外部杂散场这一无歧义的探测手段，作者证明圆偏振声子伴随的真实磁化极小，从而澄清了该领域的长期争议。

Abstract: The optical excitation of circular phonons has opened up non-equilibrium phenomena with no equilibrium counterparts, ranging from the generation of synthetic magnetic fields to the control of topological order or the manipulation of ferroaxial states. In this area, significant controversy has arisen on whether sizeable "true" magnetizations accompany circular phonons. Several confounding factors cloud the interpretation of the experiments, which are based on probing polarization rotation in the driven material. These range from the interpretation of the Faraday effect in non-equilibrium conditions to spurious nonlinear optical contributions in pump probe experiments. Here we resolve these ambiguities by measuring both polarization rotation inside the driven material and the stray magnetic field generated outside it with a proximate Faraday detector. For a 5 MV/cm mid-infrared drive, we observe a colossal polarization rotation inside a driven SiC crystal, similar to what was reported in previous work. Depending on the assumptions made, this could correspond to magnetizations as high as $μ_0 M = 0.3 T$ or as low as 4 $μT$. However, when measuring the magnetic stray field outside the driven sample we unambiguously determine that the magnetization in SiC is negligibly small ($μ_0 M \lt 0.001 T$).

</details>


### [157] [SiC-BN composites with extreme thermal and environmental stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17283)
*Abhijit Biswas,Eugenio Garcia-Granados,Saman Zare,Mozhdeh Mirakhory,JungHun Park,Shuvodeep De,Jessica Reyes,Hunter B. Schonfeld,Noah A. Burke,William Riffe,Michael S. Lafferty,Sudaice Kazibwe,Maryam Amiri,Shreyasi Chattopadhyay,Mingrui Xu,Gelu Costin,Tanguy Terlier,Jianhua Li,Ching-Wu Chu,Liangzi Deng,Bharat K. Jasthi,Manoj Tripathi,Venkataramana Gadhamshetty,Gi-Dong Sim,Kathy Lu,Adri C. T. van Duin,Patrick E. Hopkins,Sanjay Sampath,Pulickel M. Ajayan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 一种等质量比6H-SiC/h-BN放电等离子烧结复合材料在>1500°C极端氧化和热循环条件下保持优异稳定性，并具有温度无关的稳定热导率，是一种轻质、易加工的极端环境结构材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有陶瓷材料在极端高温氧化条件下难以同时维持结构完整性和抗热震性能——纯6H-SiC和h-BN在高温氧燃料火焰下会迅速断裂或侵蚀，亟需兼具热稳定性与加工性的轻质结构陶瓷。

Method: 采用放电等离子烧结制备等质量比的6H-SiC与h-BN复合材料；通过高速氧燃料（HVOF）火焰在空气中进行长时间加热和热循环测试；结合微观结构表征、有限元分析热流图以及ReaxFF分子动力学模拟，研究其抗氧化机制、孔隙对热应力的缓解作用及高温界面重构。

Result: 复合材料在表面温度>1500°C的空气氧化环境下可承受长时间加热与热循环而不失效，而纯6H-SiC与h-BN迅速断裂或侵蚀；表面形成多层二氧化硅保护层进一步抗氧；本征孔隙有效缓解热应力；高温界面重构形成富Si-N/C-B键网络，增强界面结合力；且在宽温域内呈现罕见的温度无关稳定热导率。

Conclusion: 这种轻质、易加工的6H-SiC/h-BN陶瓷复合材料在极端环境下具有优异的热稳定性、抗氧化性和稳定的热传导性能，是极具前景的极端环境结构材料。

Abstract: Ceramic materials that maintain structural integrity and resist thermal-shock under extreme conditions are critical for many applications. Here we show that spark plasma sintered composites containing equal weight fractions of hexagonal silicon carbide (6H-SiC) and boron nitride (h-BN) exhibit exceptional stability under extreme high-temperature oxidizing conditions and unusually stable temperature-independent thermal conductivity across a broad temperature range. Under high-velocity oxygen-fuel torch exposure in air, it withstands prolonged heating and thermal-cycling at surface temperatures reaching >1500 °C, whereas pure 6H-SiC and h-BN rapidly fractures or erodes. Microstructural evaluation shows the formation of a multi-phase surface silica layer that further protects the material from oxidation. Finite-element analysis heat-flux map shows that intrinsic porosity mitigates thermal stress, while ReaxFF molecular dynamics simulations reveal thermally driven interfacial reconstruction and the formation of a Si-N/C-B-rich bonding network, enhancing the interfacial cohesion at elevated temperatures. This lightweight, easily machinable ceramic composite will be a promising structural material in extreme environment applications.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Susceptibility Bottlenecks in Equilibrium Propagation Using Oscillator Ising Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14727)
*Malihe Farasat,Oshada Rathnayake,Nikhil Shukla*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究振荡器伊辛机（OIM）中物理学习信号的传输机制，揭示了隐藏层响应可分解为输出端微扰力与层间磁化率两个因子，并指出仅实现目标能量景观不足以保证有效物理学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡传播（EP）虽能在物理系统中实现学习，但对振荡器伊辛机而言，底层振荡器动力学如何传输学习信号尚不清楚；作者希望厘清哪些物理/动力学因素决定学习信号能否有效从输出层传递到隐藏层。

Method: 将隐藏层响应分解为输出端微扰（nudging force）与层间磁化率（susceptibility）两个因子；构建二分 OIM，并让耦合形式在相位差耦合与相位和耦合之间插值，同时保证所有耦合形式在二值锁相态上实现相同的伊辛哈密顿量；通过二次谐波注入、混合曲率分析及数值仿真验证各因素的作用。

Result: 二次谐波注入主要通过相位二值化影响微扰力；耦合形式通过能量景观的混合曲率控制层间磁化率；在等量混合时，二值流形上混合曲率消失，强烈抑制了隐藏层学习信号；数值仿真证实了这些机制及其对分类性能的影响。

Conclusion: 仅在物理系统中实现期望的能量景观不足以确保有效学习，系统还必须支持学习所需的微扰传输；这为设计可用于平衡传播的物理学习硬件提供了关键约束。

Abstract: Equilibrium propagation (EP) enables learning in physical systems by exploiting the displacement between free and weakly perturbed equilibrium states. Here, we investigate how the underlying oscillator dynamics govern the transmission of learning signals in oscillator Ising machines (OIMs). We show that the hidden-layer response can be decomposed into two distinct factors: the nudging force applied at the output and the susceptibility governing its transmission to the hidden layer. Using a bipartite OIM with interactions interpolating between phase-difference and phase-sum coupling, we investigate these factors while ensuring that all coupling forms realize the same Ising Hamiltonian at binary phase-locked states. We demonstrate that second-harmonic injection primarily influences the nudging force through phase binarization, whereas the coupling form controls the cross-layer susceptibility through the mixed curvature of the energy landscape. At equal mixing, the mixed curvature vanishes on the binary manifold, strongly suppressing hidden-layer learning signals. Numerical simulations validate these mechanisms and reveal their consequences for classification performance. Our findings establish that realizing a desired energy landscape alone is insufficient to ensure effective physical learning; the system must also support the transmission of perturbations required for learning.

</details>


### [159] [Beyond Ground States: Learning Many-Body Hamiltonians for p-Bit Invertible Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14744)
*Tian Feng,Xutang Fan,Yiyun Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出将p-bit可逆逻辑的哈密顿量设计视为逆统计力学问题：通过约束极大似然拟合四阶以内相互作用，使吉布斯分布在各钳制情形下逼近合法状态上的均匀分布，并以间隙下限保证所有合法态低于非法态。学习得到的能量用于乘法和加法，在多数尺寸下的更新次数与工作量均优于手写编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统p-bit可逆逻辑构造只追求基态成员资格和较大的最小能量间隙，但有限时间采样器会穿越激发态，因此需要一种直接以采样效能为目标、拟合吉布斯分布（而非仅基态）的能量设计方法。

Method: 将哈密顿量设计形式化为逆统计力学：在“间隙下限保证所有合法态能量低于所有非法态”的约束下，拟合一个最高四阶相互作用的能量，使其吉布斯定律在每种钳制情形的合法状态上逼近均匀分布。采用带剪枝与重新拟合的约束极大似然估计从固定项池中选取项；每个能量都经过全情形精确审计；评分标准为在重启动下、采用九种调优采样器中的最佳者达到已验证解所需的时间。并在FPGA色彩扫描SSA核上做位精确验证，对比学习能量与手写编码。

Result: 在欠定模式下，学习乘子所需的更新次数仅为手写编码的0.55、0.44、0.52和0.46倍（4x4到7x7，区间[0.38,0.56]），且7x7以下各家族更快且无已解析工作量惩罚；学习加法器在8位反向模式为0.71更新次数，24与32位约四分之一，32位工作量为0.55。在5x5等池、预算与间隙条件下，间隙最大化拟合对更新次数为0.79[0.73,0.88]、工作量为1.26，而似然拟合对间隙拟合为0.55[0.46,0.58]的更新和0.58[0.48,0.61]的工作量。系数舍入到最窄精确整数位宽后，学习乘子在FPGA上4x4时周期数0.41倍、7x7时0.057[0.045,0.068]倍，查找表至多2.4倍。

Conclusion: 该方法只需电路的合法赋值集合即可自动学习高效能量；在欠定模式下优于手写编码，但在确定模式下（如12位起的加法器）手写编码工作量更低且更新更少。

Abstract: Probabilistic-bit (p-bit) invertible logic encodes a Boolean relation as an energy whose ground states are its legal assignments, computing in any direction by sampling. Traditional constructions target ground-state membership and a wide minimum energy gap, yet finite-time samplers traverse excited states. We cast Hamiltonian design as inverse statistical mechanics: fit an energy with interactions up to order four whose Gibbs law approaches uniformity on each clamp case's legal states, under a gap floor keeping every legal state below every illegal one. Constrained maximum likelihood with pruning and refitting fits terms from a fixed pool, and every delivered energy is audited exactly on all cases. Each energy is scored by time to a verified solution under restarts with its best of nine tuned samplers. In underdetermined modes, learned multipliers factor in 0.55, 0.44, 0.52 and 0.46 [0.38, 0.56] of the hand-written encodings' updates from 4x4 to 7x7, faster within every family below 7x7 at no resolved work penalty; learned adders solve the reverse mode in 0.71 of the updates at 8 bits and about a quarter at 24 and 32 bits, at 0.55 of the work at 32 bits. At 5x5 under equal pool, budget and floor, a gap-maximizing fit reaches 0.79 [0.73, 0.88] in updates and 1.26 in work against the hand-written encoding, while likelihood fitting takes 0.55 [0.46, 0.58] and 0.58 [0.48, 0.61] of the gap fit's updates and work. Rounding coefficients to the narrowest exact integer words tested, we run both energies at each pair's common word on one FPGA color-sweep SSA core, bit-exact with software: learned multipliers take 0.41 of the hand-written encoding's cycles at 4x4 and 0.057 [0.045, 0.068] at 7x7, at up to 2.4 times its lookup tables. The method requires only a circuit's legal assignments; in determined modes, hand-written encodings take less work, and from 12 bits on the adder fewer updates.

</details>


### [160] [Zeroth persistent homology of ground-state correlations at clean and disordered quantum transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14797)
*Francisco Belchí,Nahuel Statuto*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文用持续同调（第零维）分析横向与纵向场下反铁磁伊辛链的基态关联，证明其条形码与持续景观速度能无阈值地定位临界点与混合序线，并在重整化区与无序实现中识别强无序重整化群团簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相较于常规响应量，拓扑数据分析（特别是第零维持续同调）能否从交错基态关联中提供额外、稳定且无阈值的信息来刻画量子临界与无序诱导的重整化现象，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 利用精确自由费米子解和密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）计算交错基态关联；构建单连接层次结构并提取零维持续同调的条形码与持续景观，以其速度最大值定义无阈值伪临界点；在无序情形下考察全域与受限过滤、标记合并树对强无序重整化群团簇的恢复能力。

Result: 持续景观速度最大值所定义的伪临界点能以与常规响应相当或更高的精度定位干净临界点与混合序线；在重整化进入区，全域速度会错位，需将过滤限制在序尺度才能恢复进入点，但所有测试估计量都无法定位出口；边界条件诱导的畴壁抑制了无序平均的重入瓣；对单个无序实现，标记合并树能恢复大部分强无序重整化群团簇（包括格点受限层次结构遗漏的非连通团簇），且无序越强一致度越高。

Conclusion: 相干信息蕴含在完整关联矩阵中，而第零维持续同调提供了一个稳定、无阈值的层次结构，为检测量子临界性、混合序线及无序诱导的重整化现象提供了有效且互补的工具。

Abstract: The antiferromagnetic Ising chain in transverse and longitudinal fields exhibits a continuous critical point, a mixed-order transition line and, with quenched disorder, an infinite-disorder fixed point and reentrant order. Using exact free-fermion solutions and the density-matrix renormalization group, we examine what the zeroth persistent homology of staggered ground-state correlations adds to conventional responses. Its barcode records the merge scales of the single-linkage hierarchy, and the maximum of its persistence-landscape velocity defines a threshold-free pseudocritical point. This point locates the clean critical point and the mixed-order line with an accuracy comparable to conventional responses. In the reentrant regime, the global velocity misplaces the entry, which is recovered only by restricting the filtration to the ordering scale, and none of the tested estimators locates the exit. A domain wall forced by the boundary conditions suppresses the disorder-averaged reentrant lobe. For individual disordered realizations, the labelled merge tree recovers most strong-disorder renormalization-group clusters, including noncontiguous ones missed by lattice-restricted hierarchies, with increasing agreement at stronger disorder. This information resides in the full correlation matrix, while zeroth homology provides a stable, threshold-free hierarchy.

</details>


### [161] [Direct imaging of Su-Schrieffer-Heeger edge modes in a disordered suspended mechanical oscillator chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14826)
*Defeng Lu,Rohan Modi,Nicolas Delnour,Michael Hilke*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文报道了Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）模型的一种可重构宏观力学实现：由相同压缩弹簧耦合的交替质量块悬挂链。通过视频追踪重建键分辨谱和模式轮廓，验证了拓扑边界态及无序（质量扰动）对边缘模频率简并和模式对称性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望构建一个可直接成像、可重构的经典宏观力学平台，用于直观研究SSH模型的拓扑简正模式，特别是边界局域模式和缺陷/无序效应，从而在经典系统中实证一维拓扑物理。

Method: 将交替质量块与相同压缩弹簧组成的悬挂链作为SSH模型的宏观实现。关键的SSH自由度取为相邻质量块间的纵向相对位移（键坐标基）。在该基下，对力学动力学矩阵施加常数平移与重标度，可将N个质量块的链映射为一个(N-1)格点的SSH链。实验上采用视频追踪提取键分辨的谱与模式轮廓，并与理想有限SSH链的理论预测对比。

Result: 拓扑终止支持局域在边界附近的中隙模式，并呈现有效偶长度SSH链所预期的交替子晶格结构。质量扰动会劈裂边缘模频率，并使模式轮廓相对于无扰动预测出现不对称性。

Conclusion: 该宏观力学平台可直接成像，为研究经典力学系统中的拓扑简正模式及无序效应提供了有力工具，实验结果与有限SSH链理论吻合良好。

Abstract: We report a reconfigurable macroscopic mechanical realization of the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model: a suspended chain of alternating masses coupled by identical compression springs. The relevant SSH degree of freedom is the relative longitudinal displacement between neighboring masses. In this bond-coordinate basis, applying a constant shift and rescaling of the mechanical dynamical matrix maps a chain of $N$ blocks onto an (N-1)-site SSH chain. Using video tracking, we reconstruct bond-resolved spectra and mode profiles and compare them with predictions for an ideal finite SSH chain. The topological termination supports mid-gap modes localized near the boundaries, with the alternating-sublattice structure expected for an effective even-length SSH chain. Mass variations split the edge-mode frequencies and produce asymmetric mode profiles relative to the non-disordered predictions. This directly imageable platform enables studies of topological normal modes and disorder effects in a classical mechanical system.

</details>


### [162] [Physical interpretation of spin-glass models with correlated disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16765)
*Hidetoshi Nishimori*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文综述了一类具有特定关联键无序的Ising自旋玻璃模型，该模型通过Oshima-Arai-Hukushima采样程序构造，与Mattis模型形成"角色互换"的类比：其铁磁相实质上是披着铁磁外衣的自旋玻璃相，磁化强度即与参考构型的重叠度，为研究Edwards-Anderson模型中的温度混沌和磁场混沌提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立关联键无序模型与标准Edwards-Anderson自旋玻璃之间的联系，利用其铁磁序来研究自旋玻璃相中的温度混沌与磁场混沌等难题，为自旋玻璃问题提供新视角。

Method: 采用Oshima-Arai-Hukushima采样程序：从具有对称±J分布的Edwards-Anderson模型中抽取一个平衡自旋构型作为参考构型，将模型改写在该参考构型变为全上态（all-up state）的规范下，从而得到关联键无序。该构造与Mattis模型类比但角色互换；挫败统计与对称±J模型完全相同；推广引入参量γ后，规范等价类的概率仅由γ决定，而参考构型抽取时的逆温度只决定各类内部的分布。模型的磁化强度即与参考构型的重叠度。

Result: 通过该对应关系，对关联模型铁磁相的研究得出了关于Edwards-Anderson模型自旋玻璃相中温度混沌和磁场混沌的意外结果。关联无序模型为自旋玻璃问题提供了高度非平凡的成果。

Conclusion: 具有关联无序的模型为自旋玻璃研究提供了新视角，并已被证明是自旋玻璃问题中高度非平凡结果的丰产领域。

Abstract: Ising spin-glass models with a specific type of correlated bond disorder have recently been used to relate properties of spin-glass phases, such as temperature chaos and magnetic-field chaos, to ferromagnetic order. This correlated disorder has a simple physical interpretation, which underlies the sampling procedure introduced by Oshima, Arai, and Hukushima for numerical simulations: the bond configuration is obtained from the Edwards-Anderson model with the symmetric $\pm J$ distribution by drawing one of its equilibrium spin configurations and writing the model in the gauge in which this reference configuration becomes the all-up state. The construction is analogous to the Mattis model, with the roles of the ferromagnet and the spin glass reversed: whereas the Mattis model is a ferromagnet in disguise, the ferromagnetic phase of the correlated model is essentially a spin-glass phase in disguise. The frustration statistics of the correlated-disorder model are exactly those of the symmetric $\pm J$ model, and, in a generalization with an additional parameter $γ$, the probabilities of the gauge equivalence classes are determined by $γ$ alone, whereas the inverse temperature at which the reference configuration is drawn determines only the distribution within each class. The magnetization of this model is the overlap with the reference configuration. Through this correspondence, studies of its ferromagnetic phase have led to unexpected results on temperature chaos and magnetic-field chaos in the spin-glass phase of the Edwards-Anderson model. Models with correlated disorder have thus provided a new perspective on spin glasses and have proved fertile ground for highly nontrivial results on the spin-glass problem.

</details>


### [163] [Low-temperature vibrational anomalies and thermal transport in polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16848)
*V. Sokolenko,A. I. Krivchikov,A. Jeżowski,V. Boiko,O. A. Korolyuk,D. Szewczyk*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究通过对比六种多环芳烃晶体（芘、并四苯、苝、并五苯、蒄、碗烯）的低温热容、低频拉曼光谱和热导率，揭示有序分子晶体中也能出现类似玻璃（非晶）的玻色峰与热输运行为，且这些特征与分子结构和晶体堆积方式有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色峰通常与结构无序（非晶材料）相关联，但有序分子晶体中也出现类似热容异常；目前其与分子结构、晶体堆积的关系及其与热输运的联系尚不清楚。作者希望通过系统比较一系列结构相关的多环芳烃晶体，厘清玻璃型振动与热学特征是否取决于分子结构和堆积方式。

Method: 选取六种多环芳烃晶体（芘、并四苯、苝、并五苯、蒄、碗烯），通过低温热容测量、低频拉曼光谱和热导率测量进行系统对比。对热容过剩上升段拟合升余弦标度函数（无自由形状参数），分析拉曼晶格模的数目与宽度，并检验热导率是否存在激活型贡献。

Result: 对具有清晰峰值的化合物，热容过剩的上升段符合升余弦标度函数，而衰减段因化合物而异；系列内分辨出的晶格模拉曼带的数目和宽度各不相同；热导率呈现从类晶体到类玻璃行为的连续变化；五种平面化合物中存在可分辨的激活型贡献，而碗烯数据在现有测量灵敏度内无需引入该贡献。

Conclusion: 玻璃型的热学和振动特征并非非晶材料独有，而是与分子结构及晶体堆积的差异相关；但仅凭这些结果尚不能将堆积方式确立为唯一的因果变量。

Abstract: The boson peak is an excess of low-frequency vibrational states over the Debye prediction and is commonly associated with structural disorder. Similar heat-capacity anomalies also occur in ordered molecular crystals, but their dependence on molecular structure and their relation to heat transport remain incompletely understood. Here we compare six polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon (PAH) crystals -- pyrene, tetracene, perylene, pentacene, coronene and corannulene -- using low-temperature heat capacity, low-frequency Raman spectroscopy and thermal conductivity measurements. For compounds with well-resolved maxima, the rising side of the heat-capacity excess is consistent with a raised-cosine scaling function with no adjustable shape parameter, whereas the decay is compound-dependent. The number and widths of resolved lattice-mode Raman bands vary across the series, although their assignment is constrained by spectral background and resolution. Thermal conductivity ranges from crystal-like to glass-like behaviour. An activated contribution is resolved in the five planar compounds but is not required to describe the corannulene data within the sensitivity of the present measurements. These results show that glass-like thermal and vibrational signatures are not unique to amorphous materials and are associated with differences in molecular structure and crystal packing; they do not, by themselves, isolate packing as the sole causal variable.

</details>


### [164] [Cup and cap synchronization: higher-order node dynamics from topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16979)
*Runyue Wang,Yuanzhao Zhang,Ginestra Bianconi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出统一高阶节点同步理论的Cup and Cap同步框架，揭示三角以上高阶交互（尤其是四体交互）对同步相变临界性质的根本影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高阶节点同步模型虽能产生不连续同步相变，但缺乏一个能统一既有模型、并揭示三角形之外高阶交互作用的统一理论框架。

Method: 结合代数拓扑与非线性动力学，提出Cup and Cap同步框架，自然纳入高阶网络的拓扑组织，并与两类既有高阶Kuramoto模型及其临界行为进行对比分析。

Result: Cup and Cap同步可复现两类既有高阶Kuramoto模型的普适类临界行为，同时展现出既有模型无法捕捉的新现象；特别地，纳入四体交互会从根本上改变同步相变的临界性质，产生仅含边和三角形交互模型所无法观察到的集体现象。

Conclusion: 研究表明必须超越通常研究的三体项，四体及更高阶交互对临界行为至关重要；Cup and Cap同步可作为理解高阶网络集体动力学的统一理论框架。

Abstract: Higher-order node-based synchronization provides distinct mechanisms for discontinuous synchronization transitions. However, a comprehensive theoretical framework capable of encompassing existing models and revealing the role of higher-order interactions beyond triangles is still missing. Here, combining algebraic topology and nonlinear dynamics, we introduce Cup and Cap Synchronization, a unifying theoretical framework for higher-order node-based dynamics that naturally incorporates the topological organization of the underlying higher-order network. We show that Cup and Cap Synchronization can display critical behavior belonging to the universality classes of two existing higher-order Kuramoto-like models, whose distinct critical behaviors were thought to originate from different mechanisms. Remarkably, we demonstrate that Cup and Cap Synchronization displays additional phenomenology not captured by the previously proposed models of higher-order node-based synchronization. Most relevantly, the inclusion of four-body interactions can fundamentally alter the critical properties of the synchronization transition, giving rise to collective phenomena that cannot be observed in models including only interactions mediated by edges and triangles. These findings reveal the importance of going beyond the commonly studied three-body terms and establish Cup and Cap Synchronization as a unifying theoretical framework for understanding collective dynamics in higher-order networks.

</details>


### [165] [Statistics of Fock-space correlation lengths in the many-body localised phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17306)
*Bibek Saha,David E. Logan,Sthitadhi Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种定量的微观理论，利用Fock空间两点关联函数解释多体局域相中由长程多体共振导致的波函数强不均匀性；数值与理论表明典型关联呈纯指数衰减，而平均关联因稀有共振节点呈亚指数衰减，且源于关联长度分布方差随系统尺寸反比缩小，并与超立方Fock空间的高维几何共同作用，进而导出奇异谱及量子多重分形反常对称性破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体局域相中存在长程、多体共振，表现为Fock空间上本征态振幅的强不均匀性。此前缺乏定量、微观的理论来刻画这种不均匀性，特别是对典型行为与平均行为差异、系统尺寸依赖以及相关的多重分形性质缺乏系统理解。

Method: 以无序倾斜场伊辛链为模型进行精确对角化，构建以Fock空间两点关联函数为核心的定量微观理论；分析典型与平均关联的衰减行为，提取Fock空间关联长度分布及其方差随系统尺寸的标度（方差与系统尺寸成反比），并结合高维超立方Fock空间图的几何性质解释典型与平均行为的定性差异。

Result: 典型关联表现为完全指数衰减，而平均关联呈亚指数衰减并具有由稀有共振节点驱动的系统性尺寸依赖；这种差异源于关联长度分布的方差随系统尺寸倒数减小，并与Fock空间高维几何相互作用。理论还导出了多体局域本征态的奇异谱，并从理论上确立了量子多重分形的反常对称性破缺是Fock空间几何的固有结果。

Conclusion: Fock空间两点关联函数理论定量解释了多体局域相中典型与平均行为的分歧，揭示了稀有共振与高维几何的协同作用是波函数强不均匀性和量子多重分形反常对称性破缺的根源，为多体局域态的微观描述提供了统一框架。

Abstract: The presence of long-ranged, many-body resonances in the many-body localised phase of disordered quantum systems is manifest in strong inhomogeneities of eigenstate amplitudes on the Fock space. We develop a quantitative, microscopic theory for this, centred on two-point Fock-space correlation functions. Exact diagonalisation of the disordered tilted-field Ising chain uncovers a stark contrast: typical correlations decay perfectly exponentially, whereas average correlations exhibit subexponential decay with systematic system-size dependence driven by rare resonant nodes. We show that this behaviour stems from an underlying distribution of Fock-space correlation lengths with a variance that scales inversely with system size. Crucially, the interplay of this distribution with the high-dimensional geometry of the hypercubic Fock-space graph, leads to the qualitative difference between the typical and average behaviours. The theory also allows us to derive the singularity spectrum of the many-body localised eigenstates, and to establish theoretically the anomalous symmetry violation of quantum multifractality as an inherent consequence of the Fock-space geometry.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Little-Parks Oscillations and Parity Effects in Mesoscopic Superconducting Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14597)
*Iogann Tolbatov,Luca Salasnich*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文从弱耦合 BCS 极限到中等耦合区，解析研究准一维介观环中的 Little-Parks 效应。利用带扭曲边界条件的 Ginzburg-Landau 展开，作者同时纳入离散有限尺寸拓扑奇偶干涉和超流诱导的连续质心动能偏移，推导出跨不同相互作用强度有效的 $T_c(Φ)$ 闭式表达式。结果表明 LP 响应由两部分组成：由 $ξ/R$ 支配的离散拓扑奇偶效应，以及按 $1/R^2$ 标度的连续动能项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Little-Parks 效应研究多局限于 BCS 弱耦合极限，未能系统刻画耦合强度增强时离散拓扑奇偶效应与连续质心动能两项物理机制的竞争与过渡，尤其缺乏跨可调相互作用强度的统一解析描述。

Method: 作者考虑介观准一维超导环，采用带扭曲边界条件的 Ginzburg-Landau 展开，将离散有限尺寸拓扑奇偶干涉和连续质心（CM）动能位移同时纳入理论框架，导出 $T_c(Φ)$ 的闭式表达式，并解析分析耦合强度变化下各项的标度行为与竞争关系。

Result: LP 响应由两项构成：一项是受关联长度比 $ξ/R$ 控制的离散拓扑奇偶效应，另一项是按 $1/R^2$ 标度的连续动能项。随耦合增强、对关联长度收缩（$ξ\ll R$），组分对在几何上"看不见"全局边界约束，离散奇偶调制被指数压低；但凝聚体仍需获得依赖磁通的宏观质心动量，因此 $T_c$ 保持主要由超流动能驱动的代数型 LP 调制。作者还讨论了在强耦合极限附近相位涨落占主导之前，平均场 GL 有效性的失效边界。

Conclusion: 在介观环中，Little-Parks 效应可统一分解为离散拓扑奇偶贡献与连续质心动能贡献；耦合增强使前者指数衰减而后者保持代数型主导。该结果将 LP 现象的理解从弱耦合 BCS 极限推广到中等耦合区，并明确了平均场 GL 理论的适用范围边界。

Abstract: We analytically investigate the Little-Parks (LP) effect, the periodic oscillation of the superconducting transition temperature $T_c$ driven by an applied magnetic flux $Φ$, from the weak-coupling Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) limit to the intermediate-coupling regime in a mesoscopic, quasi-one-dimensional ring. Utilizing a Ginzburg-Landau (GL) expansion with twisted boundary conditions, we correctly incorporate both the discrete finite-size topological parity interference and the continuous center-of-mass (CM) kinetic energy shift induced by the supercurrent. We derive a closed-form expression for $T_c(Φ)$ valid within the GL framework across variable interaction strengths. We demonstrate that the LP response comprises two distinct physical contributions: a discrete topological parity effect governed by the correlation length ratio $ξ/R$, and a continuous kinetic energy term scaling as $1/R^2$. As the coupling increases and the spatial correlation length of the pairs contracts ($ξ\ll R$), the constituent pairs become geometrically blind to the global boundary constraint, leading to an exponential suppression of the discrete parity modulation. However, the requirement that the condensate acquires a flux-dependent macroscopic CM momentum persists. This ensures that the critical temperature $T_c$ maintains an algebraic LP modulation driven predominantly by the macroscopic kinetic energy of the supercurrent. We bound our theoretical approach by discussing the breakdown of mean-field GL validity before phase fluctuations dominate near the strong-coupling limit.

</details>


### [167] [Symmetry-Enabled Rolling of Composite Bosons: A Route to Superconductivity from Strong Repulsion with $T_c$ Proportional to the Electron Bandwidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14615)
*Zhiyu Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 论文提出一种由强库仑排斥驱动的超导机制：电子-空穴结合形成3电子-1空穴(3e1h)复合玻色子，其凝聚可致超导。关键是引入"滚动"(rolling)格点运动机制，使紧束缚复合体能以单粒子跳跃移动而不经过高能中间态，从而在强束缚下保持有限质量，使Tc可达电子带宽量级。作者推导滚动判据，构造全部14种最小支持滚动的晶格模型，并用双层栅格数值验证Tc可达电子跳跃能的百分之几。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强库仑排斥系统中发现的超导体促使作者追问：排斥能否产生仅受电子带宽限制的Tc。排斥能将电子束缚到空穴上，形成3e1h复合玻色子，其凝聚可产生超导。但在受控（束缚能远大于动能）区间，紧束缚复合体在晶格上的移动通常需经过高阶过程，中间态能量约等于束缚能，导致强束缚使复合体变重，限制超流刚度进而压制Tc，使Tc远低于带宽。

Method: 作者利用对称性绕过"强束缚-复合体变重"的权衡。在满足特定对称性要求的晶格中，复合体能通过单粒子跳跃移动而不经过任何高能中间态，这种运动称为"滚动"。作者推导出滚动的判据，并据此构造全部14种支持滚动的最小晶格模型；随后证明可滚动的3e1h复合体可以在基态稳定存在；并提出在双栅极双层结构中实现该超导，用精确数值方法验证。

Result: 推导出复合体"可滚动"的判定准则；构造出全部14种最小的、支持滚动运动的晶格模型；证明3e1h复合体可在基态被稳定；数值计算表明在双栅极双层结构中Tc可达电子跳跃能的百分之几。滚动使复合体质量即使在无限强束缚下仍保持有限，从而在受控区间实现正比于电子带宽的Tc。

Conclusion: 滚动机制克服了强束缚下复合体变重、Tc受制的难题，将"可滚动性"确立为在强排斥电子系统中寻找和设计超导体的指导原则，文中构造的最小模型可作为原型。该结果为在强库仑排斥体系中实现高Tc超导提供了新途径与设计框架。

Abstract: Motivated by superconductors discovered in systems with a strong Coulomb repulsion, we ask whether such a repulsion can give rise to superconductivity with a transition temperature $T_c$ bounded only by the electron bandwidth. Coulomb repulsion can produce pairing as it binds electrons to holes. If each hole binds three electrons into a three-electron-one-hole (3e1h) composite, the condensation of these bosonic composites can give rise to superconductivity. However, the resulting $T_c$ falls far below the electron bandwidth in the controlled regime where the binding is much stronger than the carriers' kinetic energy. This is because, on a lattice, a tightly bound composite usually has to move through a higher-order process where the intermediate state costs an energy of order the binding energy, so that the strong binding makes the composite heavy, limiting the superfluid stiffness and hence $T_c$. Here, we show that symmetry offers a way around this tradeoff. In lattices satisfying certain symmetry requirements, a composite can move by single-particle hops without any costly intermediate state, a motion we call rolling. Such rolling keeps the mass of the composite finite even for an infinitely strong binding, so that a $T_c$ proportional to the electron bandwidth can be achieved in the controlled regime. We derive a criterion for rolling and apply it to construct all fourteen minimal lattice models that support such rolling. We show that rollable 3e1h composites can indeed be stabilized in the ground state. We then propose to realize such superconductivity in a dual-gate bilayer, and use exact numerics to show that $T_c$ can reach a few percent of the electron hopping. These results establish rollability as a guiding principle in the search for and design of superconductors in strongly repelling electron systems, with the minimal models constructed here as prototypes.

</details>


### [168] [Nitride crystalline barrier epitaxial SIS Josephson junctions by MBE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14968)
*Manas Verma,Anand Ithepalli,Amit Rohan Rajapurohita,Eegene Clara Chung,David A. Garcia-Wetten,Naomi Pieczulewski,Danqing Wang,John G. Wright,Archit Shah,Jimy Encomendero,Rishabh Singh,Chandrashekar Savant,Hangwang Yang,Anthony Hyatt,Jaehong Choi,Thomas Varghese,Kushagra Aggarwal,Paul B. Welander,Apurva Mehta,David Schuster,Valla Fatemi,Gregory D. Fuchs,Michael C. Hamilton,Hong X. Tang,David A. Muller,Huili Grace Xing,Debdeep Jena*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文首次通过分子束外延（MBE）工艺成功制备了具有晶态AlN隧道势垒的NbN/AlN/NbN超导-绝缘体-超导（SIS）约瑟夫森结，克服了长期以来晶态势垒仅能由MOCVD或MBE制备有源器件的材料难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，具备晶态隧道势垒的SIS约瑟夫森结无法通过MOCVD或MBE等外延技术实现，而这些外延技术恰恰是制造当今晶体管、激光器等电子/光电子系统的核心手段，因而需要突破材料瓶颈以实现晶态势垒约瑟夫森结。

Method: 采用分子束外延（MBE）技术生长NbN/AlN/NbN三层结构，其中AlN势垒厚度约4.5 nm，制备出首个功能性的氮化物晶态势垒约瑟夫森结。

Result: 器件表现出相干约瑟夫森隧穿：临界电流密度约17 A/cm²，IcRn乘积约2.5 mV，超导能隙电压约3.7 mV，且能隙随温度呈类BCS演化。迟滞的电流-电压特性以及磁通调制的Fraunhofer电流图案证实了欠阻尼SIS约瑟夫森行为。
