Conclusion: 通过MBE实现晶态势垒约瑟夫森结，为合金与超晶格电极及隧道势垒等更丰富的材料体系打开大门，并有望实现超导量子电路与HEMT、激光器等有源电子/光电子器件的单片集成。

Abstract: Superconductor-insulator-superconductor Josephson junctions with crystalline tunnel barriers have remained elusive by epitaxial techniques such as MOCVD or MBE that produce the junction materials and devices powering today's electronic and photonic systems like transistors and lasers. Here, we overcome the key materials hurdle to realize the first functional nitride crystalline barrier Josephson junctions by MBE. Despite an AlN barrier thickness of approximately 4.5 nm, the NbN/AlN/NbN trilayers exhibit coherent Josephson tunneling with critical current densities of approximately 17 A/cm2, an IcRn product of approximately 2.5 mV, and a superconducting gap voltage of approximately 3.7 mV with a BCS-like gap evolution with temperature. Hysteretic current-voltage characteristics and Fraunhofer current modulation with magnetic field establish underdamped SIS Josephson behavior. The realization of crystalline barrier Josephson junctions by MBE opens the doors to a richer materials palette with alloys and superlattice electrodes and tunnel barriers, as well as potential monolithic integration of superconducting quantum circuits with active electronic and photonic devices such as HEMTs and lasers.

</details>


### [169] [Interplay of Pair Density Wave and Uniform Superconductivity on the Kagome Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15354)
*Ruiqing Fu,Xianxin Wu,Ziqiang Wang,Sen Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文在kagome晶格单轨道t-W-J模型中研究配对密度波（PDW）与均匀超导的相互作用。发现均匀超导分量会重塑三个F1 PDW分量的振幅与相位结构，产生纯键配对极限下不存在的超导态，并导致手性PDW随在位吸引增大发生拓扑转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在p型van Hove填充附近，在位吸引W有利于零动量配对（常规BCS失稳），而最近邻键吸引J因kagome几何与子晶格干涉天然倾向于有限动量配对，从而产生PDW。二者共存与竞争的物理机制及其对序参量景观和准粒子拓扑的影响尚不清楚，需要统一的理论框架加以研究。

Method: 采用kagome晶格上的单轨道t-W-J模型，其中W和J分别表示格点上与最近邻键上的有效配对吸引。在2×2超胞内进行自洽平均场计算，研究均匀超导与PDW的竞争/共存；并结合Ginzburg-Landau（金兹堡-朗道）分析，识别能够促进序参量重组的对称性允许耦合项。

Result: 均匀超导分量重新组织了三个F1 PDW分量的振幅和相位结构，产生了纯键配对极限下不存在的超导态。所得态展现出独特的电荷调制与准粒子谱；随着在吸引强度增大，手性PDW发生拓扑转变。Ginzburg-Landau分析确认存在对称性允许的耦合可促成该重组。

Conclusion: 均匀超导能够重塑PDW态的序参量景观与准粒子拓扑，表明在kagome晶格中均匀配对与有限动量配对之间存在丰富的耦合机制，为理解相关超导材料中PDW与均匀超导的相互作用提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study the interplay between pair density wave (PDW) and uniform superconductivity on the kagome lattice within a single-orbital $t$-$W$-$J$ model near the $p$-type van Hove filling, where $W$ and $J$ denote effective pairing attractions on lattice sites and nearest-neighbor bonds, respectively. While onsite attraction favors zero-momentum pairing through the conventional BCS instability, nearest-neighbor attraction naturally promotes finite-momentum pairing due to the kagome geometry and sublattice interference. The model therefore provides a framework for exploring the competition and coexistence between uniform superconductivity and PDW order. Self-consistent mean-field calculations in a $2\times2$ supercell reveal that the uniform component reorganizes both the amplitudes and phase structure of the three $F_1$ PDW components, giving rise to superconducting states absent in the pure bond-pairing limit. A Ginzburg--Landau analysis identifies symmetry-allowed couplings that can favor this reorganization. The resulting states exhibit distinct charge modulations and quasiparticle spectra, while the chiral PDW undergoes topological transitions as the onsite attraction increases. Our results demonstrate how uniform superconductivity reshapes the order-parameter landscape and quasiparticle topology of a PDW state.

</details>


### [170] [Machine-accelerated extraction of experimental superconducting parameters for physical insight and prediction across thousands of materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15627)
*Mihir Manium,Benjamin Geisler,Peter J. Hirschfeld,Amalia I. Coldea*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: AutoSuperCon 是一个包含八个超导参数、覆盖6793种超导体共20354条记录的数据库，数据由大语言模型从超过47000篇arXiv预印本中提取，并经参考数据集验证（与NIMS SuperCon的R²=0.96）。基于该数据库，作者揭示了若干物理规律，并训练了可在超导族内筛选候选材料的机器学习预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新型超导体有望推动核聚变、无损耗电子学等下一代技术发展。机器学习虽能预测候选超导体，但除临界温度外缺乏经过验证的实验训练数据，这严重制约了数据驱动的超导材料发现。

Method: 作者构建了AutoSuperCon数据库，利用大语言模型从超过47000篇arXiv预印本中自动提取八个超导参数，并与NIMS SuperCon等参考数据集进行交叉验证（R²=0.96）。随后基于该数据库进行统计分析，并采用迁移学习方法训练基于成分（composition）的特征预测器，用于预测临界温度和上临界场。

Result: AutoSuperCon数据库包含八个超导参数、6793种超导体的20354条记录。统计发现：常规超导体的中位能隙比（gap ratio）为3.6，而铜氧化物超导体升至6.4；28%的上临界场超过Pauli极限；临界温度存在一个与超流密度成正比的上限。训练的基于成分的预测器对临界温度达到R²_log=0.83，对上临界场达到R²_log=0.69。在已知超导族内，仅凭成分即可同时筛选这两个参数的候选材料。

Conclusion: AutoSuperCon通过大语言模型自动提取和验证大规模超导参数数据，弥补了超导领域实验训练数据的不足，既提供了跨数千种超导体的物理洞察（如能隙比趋势、Pauli极限违反、临界温度上限），也证明了在已知超导族内基于成分的机器学习筛选是可行且有效的，从而支持数据驱动的超导材料发现。

Abstract: New superconductors could unlock next-generation technologies from nuclear fusion to lossless electronics. Machine learning can predict candidate superconductors but lacks validated experimental training data beyond the critical temperature. Here we present AutoSuperCon, a database of eight superconducting parameters, containing 20354 entries for 6793 superconductors. The data were extracted from over 47000 arXiv preprints using a large language model and validated against reference datasets ($R^2 = 0.96$ with NIMS SuperCon). Using AutoSuperCon, we show that median gap ratios rise from 3.6 in conventional superconductors to 6.4 in cuprates, 28% of upper critical fields exceed the Pauli limit, and critical temperatures lie below a ceiling proportional to the superfluid density. Further, we train composition-based predictors for the critical temperature ($R^2_{\log}=0.83$) and the upper critical field ($R^2_{\log}=0.69$), boosted by transfer learning. Within known superconducting families, composition alone is sufficient to screen candidates for both parameters. AutoSuperCon thus enables physical insight and data-driven discovery across thousands of superconductors.

</details>


### [171] [Band geometry, Anderson's theorem, and vortex nucleation in unconventional 2D superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15872)
*Vladislav Poliakov,Inti Sodemann Villadiego,T. Senthil*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究无序对具有非平庸能带几何的二维材料中超导的影响，重点分析无序引起的拆对效应，以及手性时间反演破缺超导体中无序能否诱导涡旋玻璃相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期在二维材料中发现手性超导体的启发，作者希望厘清无序在非平庸能带几何材料超导中的作用：一是检验Anderson定理和Abrikosov-Gorkov拆对描述在非平凡Bloch波函数结构下如何被修改，二是探究随机势无序能否在破坏时间反演的手性超导体中成核出超导涡旋。

Method: 作者首先从正常态Bloch波函数结构出发，推广Anderson定理与Abrikosov-Gorkov无序拆对理论；进而将结果应用于扭曲MoTe2，对比其BCS配对机制与实测正常态剩余电阻率；随后针对手性时间反演破缺超导体，研究随机势无序能否使超导涡旋成核，并区分BCS机制与任意子流体机制下的差异。

Result: 结果表明：(1) 非平庸能带几何会修正传统的无序拆对描述；(2) 对扭曲MoTe2，BCS配对机制与实测的正常态剩余电阻率之间存在张力；(3) 对BCS机制稳定的超导体，弱无序不会诱导涡旋玻璃相；而由任意子流体形成的超导则可表现出不同行为。

Conclusion: 非平庸能带几何显著改变无序对超导的影响，并使得扭曲MoTe2的BCS配对解释面临剩余电阻率的约束；同时，弱无序在手性超导体中不会普遍诱导涡旋玻璃相，除非超导来源于非BCS（如任意子流体）机制。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent discovery of chiral superconductors in 2d materials, we study the effects of disorder on superconductivity in materials with non-trivial band geometry. First, we show how Anderson's theorem and the conventional Abrikosov-Gorkov description of disorder-induced pair breaking are modified by the structure of the normal-state Bloch functions. We apply these results to twisted MoTe2 and show that a BCS pairing mechanism for the observed superconductor is in tension with the measured normal state residual resistivity. Second, we study whether random potential disorder can nucleate superconducting vortices in a chiral time- reversal-breaking superconductor. We show that, for a superconductor stabilized by the usual BCS mechanism, weak disorder does not induce a vortex glass phase, in contrast to superconductivity formed from an anyon fluid.

</details>


### [172] [Evidence of anisotropic surface state residual loss in UTe$_2$ amid isotropic low-energy excitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16196)
*Arthur Carlton-Jones,Jared Z. Dans,Gicela Saucedo Salas,Shanta R. Saha,Johnpierre Paglione,Nicholas P. Butch,Steven M. Anlage*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 借助谐振腔微扰技术，本文同时测量了非传统超导体UTe₂在微波频率下的穿透深度（电感通道）与表面电阻（电阻通道），发现二者均呈二次幂律且各向同性，而残余损耗则表现出系统性的各向异性；这一结果与弱散射下单对费米面节点图像不符，并讨论了拓扑超导作为解释的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UTe₂作为非传统超导体的配对机制与可能的拓扑性质仍不明确，单靠某一类测量（电感或电阻通道）难以全面刻画基态以上激发的方向性依赖，因此需要同时考察两个通道并系统研究其各向异性行为。

Method: 采用谐振腔微扰技术，在微波频率下测量多种UTe₂单晶的复合表面阻抗，使电感通道与电阻通道处于可比量级并被同等对待；通过对大量谐振模式进行研究，系统刻画复合电动力学，从而获得穿透深度与表面电阻随温度及方向的变化规律。

Result: 低温下穿透深度与表面电阻均呈现相似的幂律温度依赖，且在所有模式汇总后表现为二次（T²）并且各向同性；但残余损耗则表现出明显且系统性的各向异性。该幂律结果与弱散射条件下费米面上仅存在单对点节点的情形不相容。

Conclusion: 各项同性的二次幂律叠加各向异性的残余损耗，无法用弱散射下的单对点节点模型解释，作者提出拓扑超导可作为理解这一组合行为的可能诠释。

Abstract: We explore the pairing mechanism and possible topological properties of the unconventional superconductor UTe$_2$ through their influence on both the inductive and resistive channels using resonant cavity perturbation techniques. We measure a composite surface impedance for a variety of UTe$_2$ single crystals at microwave frequencies, where the inductive and resistive channels are comparable and can be treated on equal footing. By studying the composite electrodynamics for a large number of resonant modes, we are able to provide a comprehensive picture of the directional dependence of the excitations out of the ground state. We find similar power-law temperature dependencies at low-temperature for both the penetration depth and surface resistance, which in aggregate across modes, are quadratic and isotropic. On the other hand, we find a distinct and systematic anisotropy of the residual loss. The power-law results are inconsistent with a single pair of point nodes on the Fermi surface under weak scattering. We discuss the possibility of topological superconductivity as an interpretation of these isotropic power-laws in conjunction with the anisotropic residual loss.

</details>


### [173] [Quantum geometry drives orbital paramagnetism in superconducting fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16387)
*Zi-Ting Sun,Ying-Ming Xie,Chunli Huang,Naoto Nagaosa*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究多分量超导体在转变温度以上的磁响应，发现涨落可产生顺磁响应，并揭示其与库珀对量子几何的关系，最后以时间反演对称性破缺的kagome超导体模型加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为超导体通过迈斯纳效应排斥磁场，转变温度以上涨落产生的轨道抗磁磁化率在趋近转变时发散。作者想探究多分量超导体的涨落是否可能产生顺磁而非抗磁响应，并阐明其物理机制。

Method: 利用高斯涨落理论，对转变温度以上的一般多分量超导体进行分析，将磁响应分解为两项竞争贡献：库珀对质心运动的常规抗磁项，以及超导序参量不同分量间干涉产生的顺磁项；后者与库珀对量子几何相联系。并将理论应用于具有两个手性配对模式的kagome超导体模型。

Result: 当母态保持时间反演对称时，顺磁贡献可超过常规抗磁贡献，产生净的涨落顺磁性；而当轨道时间反演对称被破坏（如形成弱环形流序）时，两个手性模式简并解除，降温趋近转变时低能模式主导涨落，抗磁性重新占优，回归单分量物理。

Conclusion: 该工作为超导体中的涨落顺磁性提供了机制，并表明轨道磁响应可作为探测库珀对量子几何的手段。

Abstract: Superconductors expel magnetic fields through the Meissner effect. Even slightly above the superconducting transition temperature, electrons can briefly form Cooper pairs, leading to an orbital diamagnetic susceptibility that diverges as the transition is approached. In this work, we use Gaussian fluctuation theory to study a generic multicomponent superconductor above its transition temperature and show that fluctuations can instead produce a paramagnetic response. We identify two competing contributions: the usual diamagnetic contribution from the center-of-mass motion of Cooper pairs and a paramagnetic contribution from interference between different components of the superconducting order parameter. The latter is related to pair quantum geometry, which describes how the internal components of the Cooper pair change with momentum. We then apply our theory to a model of a kagome superconductor with two chiral pairing modes. When the parent state preserves time-reversal symmetry, the paramagnetic contribution can outweigh the conventional diamagnetic contribution, producing net fluctuation paramagnetism. However, when the orbital time-reversal symmetry is broken, e.g., by forming a weak loop-current order, the degeneracy between the two chiral modes is then lifted. Upon cooling toward the superconducting transition, the lower-energy mode dominates the fluctuations, and diamagnetism wins as single-component physics takes over. Our results provide a mechanism for fluctuation paramagnetism in superconductors and show that the orbital magnetic response can probe the quantum geometry of Cooper pairs.

</details>


### [174] [Noise-Protected Superconducting Qudits from Higher-Harmonic Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16563)
*Hamed Vakili*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文展示了通过利用电流-相位关系的二次谐波，超导三结环路可承载强抗弛豫的量子三态（qutrit），并指出二次谐波的符号是设计受保护超导量子多能级系统（qudit）的关键原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qudit（d 能级量子单元）能比 qubit 存储更多信息，但其对噪声的敏感性阻碍了容错量子计算的应用。作者旨在设计一种受保护的 qutrit，使其既能抵抗弛豫，又能控制退相干的限制。

Method: 利用超导三结环路的电流-相位关系中的二次谐波来保护 qutrit。当二次谐波为正时，采用结间具有小不对称性的简单环路即可实现可调谐的对称保护 qutrit；当二次谐波为负时，通过引入小的对称破缺项（如弱约瑟夫森二极管效应）来实现。作者还通过微观计算表明，在交替磁性约瑟夫森结中，栅电压可选择二次谐波的符号。

Result: 两种设计方案各有取舍：第一种具有精确的电荷选择定则；第二种则几乎不受电荷噪声影响，且其准粒子错误可被纠正。具体选择取决于所用材料。两者都需要可观的二次谐波，交替磁性约瑟夫森结被识别为候选平台。微观计算显示栅电压可控制二次谐波符号，但栅电压噪声会成为退相干的主要来源。

Conclusion: 这些发现确立了二次谐波的符号作为设计受保护超导 qudit 的设计原则。

Abstract: Qudits, which generalize qubits to $d$ levels, store more information per circuit element than qubits. As with qubits, however, their sensitivity to noise must be overcome before they can be used for fault-tolerant quantum computing. Here we show that, by exploiting the second harmonic of the current--phase relation, a superconducting loop of three Josephson junctions can host qutrits strongly protected against relaxation, while dephasing limits their coherence. When the second harmonic is positive, a simple loop with a small asymmetry between the junctions provides a tunable, symmetry-protected qutrit. When it is negative, a protected qutrit can be realized by adding a small symmetry-breaking term, such as a weak Josephson diode effect. The two designs involve different trade-offs: the first has an exact charge selection rule, whereas the second is nearly insensitive to charge noise and its quasiparticle errors can be corrected. The preferable choice therefore depends on the materials used. Both require a sizable second harmonic to begin with, and we identify altermagnetic Josephson junctions as a candidate platform. Finally, microscopic calculations show that a gate voltage selects the sign of the second harmonic in these junctions, although gate-voltage noise then becomes the leading source of dephasing. These findings establish the sign of the second harmonic as a design principle for protected superconducting qudits.

</details>


### [175] [Sign changing pairing in single layer FeSe/SrTiO3 revealed by nonmagnetic impurity bound states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16679)
*Huimin Zhang,Zhuozhi Ge,Michael Weinert,Lian Li*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文利用扫描隧道显微镜/光谱学研究了分子束外延生长的单层FeSe/SrTiO₃，发现非磁性Fe空位缺陷诱导的束缚态具有电子-空穴空间不对称性（空穴态二重对称、电子态四重对称），且在磁场下无能量移动或劈裂，支持符号改变的序参量（非常规配对）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层FeSe/SrTiO₃(001)高温超导性的发现引发了关于其配对对称性究竟是传统的、符号保持的s波，还是非常规的、符号改变配对的广泛争论。辨析其配对对称性是理解该体系高温超导机理的关键。

Method: 采用分子束外延（MBE）生长单层FeSe/SrTiO₃，并利用扫描隧道显微镜/光谱学（STM/STS）探测配对态。通过在Fe平面内引入非磁性Fe空位缺陷，研究其诱导的带内束缚态的空间分布、电子-空穴不对称性、对称性特征以及外磁场下的响应。

Result: 观察到非磁性Fe空位缺陷诱导的稳健带内束缚态，其表现出强烈的电子-空穴空间不对称性：空穴态呈二重对称，电子态呈四重对称。在施加外磁场时，束缚态不发生能量移动或劈裂，与符号改变的序参量相符。

Conclusion: 实验证据支持单层FeSe/SrTiO₃具有符号改变的（非常规）序参量，为澄清该体系配对对称性之争提供了直接的谱学与空间分辨证据。

Abstract: The discovery of high-temperature superconductivity in single layer FeSe epitaxially grown on SrTiO3(001) substrates has instigated extensive debate over whether its pairing symmetry is conventional sign-preserving s-wave or unconventional sign-changing. Here we probe the pairing state for single layer FeSe/SrTiO3 grown by molecular beam epitaxy using scanning tunneling microscopy/spectroscopy. We observe robust in-gap bound states induced by non-magnetic Fe-vacancy defects within the Fe-plane, which exhibit strong spatial electron-hole asymmetry with two-fold symmetry in hole states and four-fold in electron states. The bound states exhibit no energy shift or splitting under an applied magnetic field, consistent with a sign-changing order parameter.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

### [176] [Quantitative Gaussian limits of periodic O(N) models at and below the critical temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14570)
*Weiqi Jiang,Pengcheng Hou,Zhiyi Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在周期盒子（边长 M、维数 d≥3）上的零场最近邻 O(N) 模型中，于球形模型临界温度及其以下的温度范围内，证明了局部自旋坐标的体积一致高斯逼近，并给出了 1-Wasserstein 误差的精细阶估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 O(N) 模型在临界温度及以下的局部自旋坐标是否服从高斯逼近，并希望得到对 N（自旋分量数）、M（空间体积）以及温度、耦合参数一致的定量误差估计，从而刻画有限体积与有限分量数效应（如方向耦合的各向异性、球形模型谱移动 s_M 等）对逼近精度的影响。

Method: 考虑 d≥3 维周期盒子上零场最近邻 O(N) 自旋模型，基于有限体积平均球形模型的谱移动 s_M 构造目标高斯场（每个坐标场为缩放的各向异性高斯自由场，临界温度以下附加独立的常值高斯项）。在正向耦合与逆温度的紧致范围内，证明前 p≤N 个坐标的未归一化 1-Wasserstein 误差上界为 C√p(N^{-1/2}+s_M)。进一步分析含最近邻星（某点及其 d 个正向邻居）的窗口与周期无限体积积累态情形，并在 d≥5、逆温度超过临界值一个正量时改进为 C(p/N+√p M^{-d})。

Result: 在固定空间窗口上，对充分大的 M,N，前 p 个坐标的 1-Wasserstein 误差至多为 C√p(N^{-1/2}+s_M)；在含最近邻星的窗口上，误差当且仅当 p=o(N) 且 √p s_M→0 时趋于零；对任意周期无限体积积累态，任意非空固定窗口的误差当且仅当 p=o(N) 时趋于零；d≥5 且温度严格低于临界温度时上界改进为 C(p/N+√p M^{-d})；对固定 p，含星窗口的精确阶为 N^{-1}+M^{-d}，而周期积累态中每个非空固定窗口的精确阶为 N^{-1}。

Conclusion: 在球形模型临界温度及以下的零场最近邻 O(N) 模型中，局部自旋坐标具有体积一致的高斯逼近，且其误差阶由 N 和体积 M 共同决定，误差在不同窗口类型（含星窗口与非空固定窗口）及不同温度区间表现出精确的渐近阶，表明各向异性、谱移动 s_M 和分量数 N 是控制逼近精度的核心因素。

Abstract: We prove a volume-uniform Gaussian approximation for local spin coordinates in the zero-field nearest-neighbour $O(N)$ model on periodic boxes of side $M$ in $d\ge3$, at and below the critical temperature of the limiting spherical model. On a fixed spatial window, for sufficiently large $M,N$, the unnormalized $1$-Wasserstein error for the first $p\le N$ coordinates is at most $C\sqrt p (N^{-1/2}+s_M)$, where $s_M\to0$ is the spectral shift of the finite-volume mean spherical model. The estimate is uniform on compact ranges of positive directional couplings and inverse temperatures. The target has independent coordinate fields, each a scaled anisotropic Gaussian free field with an independent spatially constant Gaussian term below criticality.
  On a window containing a nearest-neighbour star (a site and its $d$ positive-direction neighbours), the error tends to zero as $M,N\to\infty$ exactly when $p=o(N)$ and $\sqrt p s_M\to0$. For any choice of periodic infinite-volume accumulation states, the error on any nonempty fixed window tends to zero as $N\to\infty$ exactly when $p=o(N)$. In $d\ge5$, when the inverse temperature exceeds its critical value by at least a fixed positive amount, the bound improves to $C(p/N+\sqrt p M^{-d})$. For fixed $p$, the sharp order is $N^{-1}+M^{-d}$ on star-containing windows and $N^{-1}$ on every nonempty fixed window in periodic accumulation states.

</details>


### [177] [Rational layers in the low temperature expansion of the three-dimensional Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14577)
*Ran Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究零场简单立方格点伊辛模型在低温展开下的液滴计数问题。作者不以壁面积排序，而是以"超过两个指定横向跨度所允许的最小壁面积的多余量"来分层组织液滴，证明每个固定多余层（包括对数覆盖修正与欧拉反演）都存在分母因子仅限 1-x、1-y、1-x-y 的有理二元生成函数，并显式给出零到三余层的完整层结构，经精确格点检验与低温比较验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统低温展开按壁面积排序伊辛液滴，但同一阶内可能出现不同横向跨度的液滴，无法精确刻画其结构。作者希望改用"多余量"（excess）这一更精细的层级来组织简单立方模型，从而获得每层的精确生成函数，并厘清已知几何成分与多联骨牌枚举的关系。

Method: 证明将重复的坐标切割压缩为有界装饰（bounded decorations），并把每个无界区间分配给一条单调链；再由有限控制器强制实现诱导接触与任意的覆盖颜色复用。由此得到每层仅有 1-x、1-y、1-x-y 分母因子的有理二元生成函数。随后显式组装零到三余层的完整层结构，并将已知几何成分归因于早前的多联骨牌枚举。

Result: 每个固定余层（含全部对数覆盖修正与欧拉反演）都有分母因子受限为 1-x、1-y 和 1-x-y 的有理二元生成函数；作者显式给出所有宽度上零到三余层的完整层结构。精确格点检查验证了公式的正确性。经认证的低温比较显示，在一个采样点上取得了适度的混合精度增益，但评估成本更高。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了精确的层结构，确认每个固定余层可由有理二元生成函数紧凑刻画；但对无界余层求和（从而得到完整的低温展开）仍是待解决的独立问题。

Abstract: A low-temperature expansion orders Ising droplets by wall area, but droplets with different transverse spans can occur at the same order. We instead organize the zero-field simple-cubic model by the excess above the minimum wall area compatible with two prescribed spans. We prove that every fixed excess layer, including all logarithmic cover corrections and Euler inversion, has a rational bivariate generating function with denominator factors restricted to 1 - x, 1 - y and 1 - x - y. The proof compresses repeated coordinate cuts into bounded decorations and assigns each unbounded interval to one monotone chain. A finite controller then enforces induced contacts and arbitrary cover-color reuse. We explicitly assemble the complete layers of excess zero through three over all widths, attributing their known geometric constituents to earlier polyomino enumeration. Exact lattice checks corroborate the formulas. A certified low-temperature comparison shows a modest hybrid accuracy gain at one sampling point and higher evaluation cost. The result establishes an exact layer structure; summation over unbounded excess remains a separate problem.

</details>


### [178] [Hidden universality of rare fluctuations and a central-limit structure at criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14621)
*Nikolaos G. Fytas,Víctor Martín-Mayor,Attilio L. Stella,Gianluca Teza,Alexandros Vasilopoulos,David Yllanes*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文首次为二维Ising普适类中的临界涨落大偏差提供了直接数值证据，证明经特征宽度归一化后，极远端尾部塌缩到同一普适类不同微观模型共享的渐近定律上，该定律由守恒场论导出的普适振幅支配，指向临界涨落的广义中心极限定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心极限定理适用于弱相关随机变量，但在临界点长程关联使其失效。已有研究认识了典型临界涨落的标度不变性，而稀有涨落（大偏差）因概率极小，超出传统数值方法能力，缺乏直接数值证据。作者旨在回答：临界点上是否存在一般性的极限定理来刻画稀有涨落。

Method: 采用大规模Tethered蒙特卡洛模拟，探测二维Ising普适类中序参量涨落，覆盖概率小至10^{-200}的极远端尾部；将涨落按其特征宽度归一化，验证不同微观模型尾部是否塌缩到单一渐近定律；并从共形场论推导出普适振幅的猜想精确结果，与数值提取值进行对比。

Result: 归一化后的远端尾部在不同微观模型间塌缩到同一条渐近定律；该塌缩由一个普适振幅支配，其数值提取结果与从共形场论推导的猜想精确值相容。

Conclusion: 临界涨落的大偏差遵循普适渐近定律，支持存在一个适用于临界系统的“广义中心极限定理”，首次在二维Ising普适类中获得了直接的数值证实。

Abstract: The central limit theorem is among the most fundamental principles in the natural sciences, governing the fluctuations of sums of weakly correlated random variables through Gaussian statistics. At critical points, however, long-range correlations invalidate this paradigm, raising a fundamental question: does any general limit theorem survive at criticality? Although scale invariance has long been recognised as the defining feature of typical critical fluctuations, the behaviour of rare fluctuations has remained largely unexplored, as their extraordinarily small probabilities place them beyond the reach of conventional numerical methods. Recent theoretical advances suggest that universal asymptotic laws {may govern large deviations in systems at criticality}, yet direct numerical evidence has been lacking. Here we provide the first such evidence in the two-dimensional Ising universality class. Using large-scale Tethered Monte Carlo simulations, we probe order-parameter fluctuations with probabilities as small as $10^{-200}$ and show that, once normalised by their characteristic width, the far tails collapse onto a single asymptotic law shared by distinct microscopic models in the same universality class. This collapse is governed by a universal amplitude, whose numerical extraction is compatible with the conjectured exact result we derive from conformal field theory, and points towards a generalised central limit theorem for critical fluctuations.

</details>


### [179] [Force-Dependent Change of Structural Behavior Upon Pulling a Grafted Flexible Polymer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14720)
*J. Matthew Smith,Michael Bachmann*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过广义系综蒙特卡洛方法与微正则拐点分析，研究了拉力对柔性聚合物粗粒化模型结构相变的影响，揭示了线圈-球状转变随拉力增强由二阶变一阶并与液-固转变合并的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解分子力在大分子结构中的作用对高分子科学至关重要，实验手段（如光镊）虽有助于观察，但拉力如何系统性地改变聚合物结构相变行为仍需从理论模拟层面厘清。

Method: 对柔性聚合物采用粗粒化模型，使用广义系综蒙特卡洛方法模拟拉力作用，并应用微正则拐点分析法识别和分类结构相，借助特定序参量刻画相变行为。

Result: 随拉力增大，线圈-球状(coil-globule)转变由二阶变为一阶，并与液-固(liquid-solid)转变合并；在特定拉力强度下识别出一个介于线圈相与球状相之间的中间相；并发现清晰的Gough-Joule效应证据；当拉力超过某一阈值时，相变不再发生。

Conclusion: 拉力可显著调控聚合物结构相变的阶次与位置：增强拉力使线圈-球状转变由连续变为不连续、与液-固转变融合，并诱导中间相与Gough-Joule效应，而过大的拉力则抑制相变发生。

Abstract: Manipulating polymer chains by means of experimental instruments such as optical tweezers has helped understand better the effects of molecular forces in macromolecular structures. In this study, we simulate the influence of a pulling force on structural phases of a coarse-grained model for flexible polymers using generalized-ensemble Monte Carlo methodologies. The recently introduced microcanonical inflection-point analysis method is employed to identify and classify structural phases of the system. Specific order parameters help characterize the transition behavior in response to the present pulling force. We show that, upon increasing the pulling strength, the coil-globule transition turns from second to first order, then merges with the liquid-solid transition. An intermediate phase, separating the coil and globule phases, is identified for certain force strengths. Clear evidence of the Gough-Joule effect is found as well. Transitions do not occur at all if the pulling force exceeds a certain threshold value.

</details>


### [180] [Optimal Driving between Nonequilibrium Steady States under a Dissipation Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14891)
*Keisuke Sugie,Dimitri Loutchko,Tetsuya J. Kobayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将非平衡稳态间驱动的热力学代价拓展为同时考虑超额熵产生与房务熵产生，在固定房务熵产生预算的约束下对协议及其时长联合优化，从而最小化总熵产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定时长优化只最小化超额熵产生（EP），忽略了维持稳态电流所需的房务 EP。作者希望同时考虑两者，在给定房务 EP 预算下真正最小化总 EP。

Method: 在慢驱动区域，将累积的房务 EP 视为“熵时间”，把问题映射为在由稳态 EP 率加权的摩擦度量下的测地线优化；并据此推导资源型几何，确定最优路径与时间表。

Result: 该框架揭示了房务 EP 与超额 EP 之间的内在权衡；在近平衡区域进一步给出总 EP 的几何下界，并在由修正热力学长度给出的最优预算处以能量均分达到该下界。对 ATP 驱动钙泵的三态模型数值模拟显示，与固定时长驱动相比得到不同的最优协议，并验证了总 EP 下界。

Conclusion: 通过引入房务 EP 预算下的联合优化，可得到不同于传统固定时长优化的最优驱动协议，并为持续耗散系统建立了基于资源型几何的总熵产生下界与解析的预算最优条件。

Abstract: Driving between nonequilibrium steady states incurs two thermodynamic costs: excess entropy production (EP) resulting from changing control parameters, and housekeeping EP required to sustain steady currents. Conventional fixed-duration optimization minimizes the former while neglecting the latter. Here, we instead optimize excess EP over both the protocol and its duration subject to a fixed leading order of housekeeping-EP budget, thereby minimizing total EP for that budget constraint. In the slow-driving regime, accumulated housekeeping EP acts as an entropic time, mapping the problem to a geodesic optimization under a friction metric weighted by the steady-state EP rate. This resource-based geometry determines optimal paths and schedules for systems that continue to dissipate even in steady states, establishing an intrinsic trade-off between housekeeping and excess EPs. Near equilibrium, this framework further yields geometric lower bounds on total EP, attained with equipartition at the optimal budget given by a modified thermodynamic length. Numerical simulations of a three-state model for an ATP-driven calcium pump reveal distinct optimal protocols compared to fixed-duration driving, confirming the total-EP bounds.

</details>


### [181] [Going beyond the standard quasiparticle picture: Entanglement spreading after quenching from highly entangled states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15409)
*Elisa Sabadini,Vincenzo Alba*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了XXZ自旋链中从n-rainbow态（由长程Bell对构成的宏观均匀态，n>1时非可积）出发的量子淬火。作者发现局域稳态在n→∞时趋于无穷温度态（有1/n修正），在XX链中淬火产生2n种准粒子纠缠多重态，虽种类间关联以1/n消失，却强烈抑制纠缠熵增长速率（n→∞时趋于零），可用广义准粒子图像描述。而在相互作用的XXZ链中，tDMRG模拟表明纠缠熵以热力学熵交换速率增长，在可模拟时间尺度内无多重态抑制迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统准粒子图像在可积系统中描述淬火后的纠缠扩散，但依赖"淬火产生纠缠准粒子对"的假设，仅对所谓可积初态成立。本文旨在突破这一限制，研究不可积的n-rainbow初态（n>1），并探究准粒子图像在超出可积初态情形下的有效性，特别是纠缠熵增长的机制是否会因多重态结构而改变。

Method: 以XXZ自旋链为模型，从n-rainbow态淬火。解析上：证明局域稳态在n→∞时趋于无穷温度态，并给出1/n阶修正；在XX（非相互作用）极限下，通过广义准粒子图像分析2n种准粒子纠缠多重态的动力学，推导纠缠熵增长速率与物种间关联（∝1/n）的关系。数值上：采用含时密度矩阵重整化群（tDMRG）模拟相互作用的XXZ链，包括均匀淬火以及将n-rainbow态与无穷温度态拼接的两种情形，并猜想拼接情形下冯·诺依曼熵的公式。

Result: （1）解析证明n-rainbow态淬火后的局域稳态在n→∞时趋近无穷温度态，修正为1/n量级。（2）XX链中：淬火产生2n种准粒子的纠缠多重态；物种间关联以1/n衰减，但足以强烈抑制纠缠熵增长，其速率在n→∞时趋于零，该行为可由广义准粒子图像精确刻画。（3）XXZ链中：tDMRG显示纠缠熵以热力学熵跨越切面的交换速率增长，在可及时间尺度内观测不到多重态抑制；该结论同时适用于均匀淬火和与无穷温度态拼接的情形。对拼接情形猜想了一个冯·诺依曼熵公式。

Conclusion: n-rainbow态提供了超越可积初态、检验准粒子图像适用边界的框架：在非相互作用的XX链中，准粒子多重态结构导致纠缠增长被强烈抑制，需用广义准粒子图像描述；而在相互作用XXZ链中，纠缠熵的扩散速率由热力学熵交换决定，tDMRG在可观时间窗内未见多重态抑制，表明相互作用彻底改变了纠缠输运的多重态图景。这揭示了可积性与相互作用在纠缠扩散中的不同角色。

Abstract: The quasiparticle picture provides a quantitative description of entanglement spreading after quantum quenches in integrable systems. In interacting models, however, it relies on the assumption that the quench creates entangled pairs of quasiparticles, which is justified only for the so-called integrable initial states. Here we go beyond this setting by studying quenches in the XXZ spin chain from the $n$-rainbow states, a family of macroscopically homogeneous states formed by increasingly long-range Bell pairs, which are not integrable for $n>1$. The local stationary state after the quench approaches the infinite-temperature state as $n\to\infty$, with corrections of order $1/n$, as we show analytically. Nevertheless, in the noninteracting XX chain the quench produces entangled multiplets of $2n$ species of quasiparticles. Although interspecies correlations vanish as $1/n$, they strongly suppress the growth of the entanglement entropy, whose rate vanishes as $n\to\infty$. We capture this behaviour within a generalized quasiparticle picture. In the interacting XXZ chain, instead, time-dependent density matrix renormalization group (tDMRG) simulations show that the entanglement entropy grows at the rate at which thermodynamic entropy is exchanged across the cut. Within the time scales accessible to tDMRG, the data show no trace of the multiplet suppression. This holds both after homogeneous quenches and after joining the $n$-rainbow state with an infinite-temperature state, for which we conjecture a formula for the von Neumann entropy.

</details>


### [182] [Cumulant dynamics of the ferromagnetic dipolar XY model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15485)
*Jinxin Yang,Alberto R. Cavallar,Lode Pollet*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用二阶截断累积量动力学研究方形晶格上偶极铁磁XY模型中动力学态的制备，发现有限尺寸、有限磁化的相干区域可以在非绝热但足够慢的斜坡过程中形成，且其尺度由斜坡常数、初始熵和时间共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在大尺寸系统中实验研究偶极XY模型的平衡物理，需要理解动力学态制备过程，尤其是相干区域的形成条件与尺度限制。

Method: 采用二阶截断累积量动力学方法，对方形晶格上的偶极铁磁XY模型进行数值模拟，系统线度最大达 L = 60，并考察不同斜坡常数、初始熵与时间下的演化。

Result: 观测表明存在一个有限延展、有限磁化的相干区域，即使在非绝热但足够慢的斜坡下也能形成；该相干区域的尺寸与磁化强度是斜坡常数、系统初始熵和时间的函数。

Conclusion: 这些发现对未来在更大系统尺寸中研究偶极XY平衡物理的实验提出了很强的要求，揭示了动力学态制备的尺度和相干性限制。

Abstract: We study the second-order truncated cumulant dynamics for the preparation of dynamical states in the dipolar ferromagnetic XY model on the square lattice for system sizes up to linear size L = 60. The observations lead to a transparent picture in which a coherent region of finite extent and finite magnetization forms, which is possible even for diabatic but sufficiently slow ramps. These two scales are functions of the ramp constant, the initial entropy of the system, and time. Our findings put strong requirements on future experiments addressing dipolar XY equilibrium physics in bigger system sizes.

</details>


### [183] [Probe Dynamics in Active Baths via Trajectory-Response Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15518)
*Chul-Ung Woo,Jiwon Choi,Heiko Rieger*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用轨迹-响应关系研究弱耦合于理想活性浴的自由运动探针的动力学，刻画由浴施加的平均力所定义的"速度空间势"及其稳定态（静止态与运动态），揭示不同相互作用几何（椭圆与四重对称）下的方向选择与多稳态现象，并将该方法推广到探针强度的高阶项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解活性浴中被动探针在外观上"自发"运动背后的非平衡力机制，特别是探针速度与相互作用几何如何决定运动的稳定性与方向选择，并希望建立一种基于自由粒子位移统计的普适分析方法，避免对活性浴微观细节的复杂建模。

Method: 采用轨迹-响应关系（trajectory-response relations），将活性浴对探针的响应表示为自由粒子位移统计的函数。将浴施加的平均力定义为速度空间中的势，其极小值对应于稳定状态。通过分析平均力对探针速度的依赖，识别稳定的静止态与运动态及其共存参数区间。针对椭圆相互作用研究线性响应层面的方向选择（窄轴方向先失稳）；针对四重对称相互作用研究非线性力对轴向/对角运动的选取及方向多稳态。进一步将轨迹-响应关系推广到探针强度的高阶项，引入多区间自由粒子轨迹统计，并用高斯型探针分析高阶项对稳定运动态速度的修正。

Result: 椭圆相互作用下，静止态首先沿窄轴方向失稳，方向选择在线性响应层面即已出现；四重对称相互作用下，线性摩擦保持各向同性，但平均力对探针速度的非线性依赖会选择轴向或对角运动并产生方向多稳态；将方法推广到高阶后，多区间自由粒子轨迹统计变得相关，且对高斯探针而言这些高阶项会移动稳定运动态的速度。

Conclusion: 基于轨迹-响应关系的方法能够有效刻画弱耦合于理想活性浴的自由探针的运动学，通过速度空间势的极小值识别稳定态，并揭示相互作用几何决定的方向选择机制；该方法可系统推广到高阶，从而对稳定运动态速度进行定量修正。

Abstract: Passive probes in active baths can undergo spontaneous motion driven by nonequilibrium forces that depend on probe velocity and interaction geometry. Using trajectory-response relations that express the bath response to a probe in terms of free-particle displacement statistics, we investigate the dynamics of freely moving probes weakly coupled to ideal active baths. The mean force exerted by the bath defines a velocity-space potential whose minima describe the stable states. By analyzing how the mean force depends on the probe velocity, we identify stable resting and moving states and determine the parameter regime in which they coexist. For elliptical interactions, the resting state becomes unstable first along the narrow axis, resulting in direction selection already at the linear-response level. For fourfold interactions, linear friction remains isotropic, while the nonlinear dependence of the mean force on probe velocity selects axial or diagonal motion and produces directional multistability. We further show that the trajectory-response relations extend to higher orders in the probe strength, where multi-interval free-particle trajectory statistics become relevant. For a Gaussian probe, these higher-order terms shift the speed of the stable moving state.

</details>


### [184] [Spin Structure Factor of the classical Ising Model on the Centered Pyrochlore Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15677)
*L. Xu,T. Kreißel,N. Davier,L. D. C. Jaubert,L. Pollet*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用推广的 Henley 方法计算中心烧绿石晶格上 Ising 模型的自旋结构因子，所得自旋关联函数与蒙特卡罗结果高度一致，能捕捉尖锐及展宽的 pinch point，并适用于有限温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心烧绿石（centered pyrochlore）晶格中中心自旋与顶点自旋不等价，传统 Henley 方法无法直接处理这种不等价性，因此需要将其推广，以准确计算自旋结构因子和自旋关联函数，从而理解该阻挫磁体中的 pinch point 特征。

Method: 将 Henley 的方法（Phys. Rev. B 71, 014424）推广到中心自旋与顶点自旋不等价的情形，解析计算中心烧绿石晶格上 Ising 模型的自旋结构因子与自旋关联函数，并与蒙特卡罗模拟结果进行对比验证，同时考察有限温度下的表现。

Result: 所得自旋关联函数与蒙特卡罗结果具有极好的一致性，能够准确捕捉尖锐的以及展宽的 pinch point 特征，并且在有限温度下依然有效。

Conclusion: 该方法成功推广至不等价自旋的中心晶格结构，并可进一步拓展应用于经典阻挫磁性中的其他中心晶格体系。

Abstract: We present the spin structure factor of the Ising model on the centered pyrochlore lattice. Generalizing Henley's method [Phys. Rev. B 71, 014424] to the case of inequivalent center and vertex spins, we obtain spin correlation functions that are in excellent agreement with Monte Carlo results and can capture sharp and broadened pinch points, also at finite temperature. Our approach can be extended to other centered lattice structures in classical frustrated magnetism.

</details>


### [185] [Large deviations of spatial moments of the Kipnis-Marchioro-Presutti lattice gas following a localized energy release](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15739)
*Timo Schorlepp,Baruch Meerson,Pavel Sasorov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 利用宏观涨落理论(MFT)研究一维 Kipnis-Marchioro-Presutti(KMP)模型中能量瞬时释放后空间矩的长时间统计，计算了各阶矩的方差，刻画了非高斯分布尾部，并揭示了最优路径上的动力学相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解扩散系统中能量(或密度)输运的涨落统计，特别是空间矩这一宏观可观测量的长时间非高斯行为。现有研究多聚焦于积分流或局部密度，而对空间矩(如能量分布宽度)的大偏差行为缺乏系统刻画，且其尾部可能包含奇异的孤立波(孪波)结构与对称性破缺机制。

Method: 以一维 Kipnis-Marchioro-Presutti 模型为对象，单点瞬时释放能量后考察空间矩 M_n(T)=Σ_i x_i^n u_i(T)。采用宏观涨落理论(MFT)，将大偏差问题化为对 MFT 作用量的变分(直接数值最小化)。对 n=1 建立与确定性平直初始密度下积分流统计的对偶关系；对 n=1、2 解析并数值刻画大、小矩尾部的孪波(doublon)解，比较单孪波与双孪波历史以预测临界点上的动力学相变，并推广至任意 n。

Result: 对任意正整数 n 计算了 M_n 的方差；对 n=1、2 确定了非高斯分布尾部，大矩尾部由单个绝热演化的孪波(能量密度与共轭场梯度的快速传播窄孤立脉冲对)主导，小二阶矩尾部对应能量在原点附近的集中。通过比较单、双孪波历史预测 M_2 存在临界值，最优路径在此处打破 x→-x 反射对称性；直接变分计算验证该相变为二阶，并支持解析预测。延伸至任意 n 的大、小矩尾部，并在 n=4 数值发现同类型相变。

Conclusion: 在 KMP 模型中，空间矩的大偏差统计可由 MFT 精确刻画，非高斯尾部由孪波机制主导，并伴随打破反射对称性的二阶动力学相变。该框架揭示了扩散输运中宏观可观测量的普适涨落结构，并可推广至任意阶矩。

Abstract: We study the long-time statistics of spatial moments of the time-dependent energy distribution in a one-dimensional Kipnis-Marchioro-Presutti model following an instantaneous release of energy on a single lattice site. The moments are $M_n(T)=\sum_i x_i^n u_i(T)$, where $u_i(T)$ is the energy at site $i$ at the observation time $T$. Employing macroscopic fluctuation theory (MFT), we calculate the variance of $M_n$ for every positive integer $n$ and determine the non-Gaussian distribution tails for the representative cases $n=1$ and $2$. For the first moment, we establish an exact duality, within MFT, to integrated-current statistics for a deterministic flat initial density. For both $n=1$ and $n=2$, the large-moment tails are dominated by a single adiabatically evolving doublon: a rapidly propagating pair of narrow solitary pulses of the energy density and of the gradient of the conjugate field. The small-second-moment tail is associated with concentration of the energy near the origin at time $T$. Comparing single- and two-doublon histories, we predict a dynamical phase transition at a critical value of $M_2$ at which the optimal path breaks reflection symmetry $x\to -x$. Numerical solutions obtained by direct minimization of the MFT action establish the second-order character of this transition and support our analytical predictions. Finally, we extend calculations of the large- and small-moment tails to arbitrary $n$ and numerically find a transition of the same type for $n=4$.

</details>


### [186] [Heterogeneous ice nucleation with TIP4P/Ice: the role of lattice mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15806)
*M. Camarillo,J. Oller-Iscar,M. M. Conde,J. Ramírez,S. Blazquez,E. Sanz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究利用分子动力学模拟探究基底晶格失配如何影响异质冰成核能力，发现成核温度每随晶格拉伸1%下降约5-6 K，并运用经典成核理论估算了成核能垒、动力学前因子和接触角等关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰的形成对地球生命至关重要，主要依赖固体基底上的异质成核，但基底结构与其诱导成核能力之间的关联尚不清楚。作者旨在定量揭示基底晶格失配程度对冰成核温度的具体影响。

Method: 采用原子级水模型的分子动力学模拟，使用具有拉伸冰结构的基底以无歧义地量化晶格失配，将基底与过冷水接触并测定模拟过程中冰自发成核的温度；基底诱导出亚临界冰状团簇的涨落网络，从中随机生成球冠形超临界晶核。进而结合此前报道的均相成核速率与本文计算的异相成核速率，运用经典成核理论估算成核能垒、动力学前因子和接触角。

Result: 基底晶格相对于冰每拉伸1%，成核温度下降约5-6 K，这一趋势与此前粗粒化水模型得到的结果相似。所估接触角与对成核轨迹的微观可视化一致。

Conclusion: 晶格失配以每百分点约5-6 K的幅度线性降低成核温度；经典成核理论可用于描述异质冰成核这一微观现象，其估得的接触角得到微观可视化验证，从而支持该理论的适用性。

Abstract: Ice formation, a crucial phase transition for life on Earth, mainly occurs through heterogeneous nucleation on a solid substrate. Despite the importance of this phenomenon, the correlation between the structure of the substrate and its ability to induce ice nucleation remains poorly understood. In this work, we use molecular dynamics simulations with an atomistic model of water to investigate how the lattice mismatch affects the ability of the substrate to nucleate ice. To unambiguously quantify the mismatch, we use substrates with a stretched ice structure. We put the substrate in contact with supercooled water and determine the temperature at which ice spontaneously nucleates during the course of the simulations. We find that the nucleation temperature decreases by $\sim$ 5-6 K for each percent by which the substrate lattice is stretched relative to that of ice. This trend is similar to that previously obtained with a coarse-grained water model [Camarillo et al., J. Chem. Phys. 160, 134505 (2024)]. \textcolor{black}{The substrate induces a fluctuating network of subcritical ice-like clusters, from which a spherical-cap-shaped supercritical nucleus eventually emerges stochastically.} Using Classical Nucleation Theory, combine previously reported homogeneous nucleation rates with heterogeneous nucleation rates computed in this work to estimate key heterogeneous nucleation parameters, including the nucleation barrier, the kinetic pre-factor, and the contact angle. The latter is consistent with a microscopic visualization of nucleation trajectories, supporting the applicability of Classical Nucleation Theory to describe a microscopic phenomenon such as heterogeneous ice nucleation.

</details>


### [187] [U(1)-Symmetric Spin SYK Models: Ordering, Chaos, and Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15896)
*Shiyong Guo,Enrico Rinaldi,Jad C. Halimeh,Brian Swingle*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种U(1)对称的自旋版复SYK模型，它由随机电荷守恒的q-自旋相互作用构成，天然映射到量子比特希尔伯特空间。在大N、大q极限下，该模型复现了复费米子SYK的先导鞍点结构（化学势对应磁场、电荷密度对应磁化）。通过精确对角化发现：q=2,4时U(1)不变序敏感度随系统增大而增大，q=6时减小；能谱统计在半填充时符合GOE、相邻磁化扇区符合GUE。耦合链上的畴壁动力学显示磁化输运在小簇区域呈扩散行为。这些结果确立了自旋-SYK模型的解析基准与有限尺寸特征，并推动对低温、大系统区域的量子模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子形式的SYK模型在量子比特处理器上存在非局域编码开销，作者希望构建一个原生作用于量子比特希尔伯特空间的U(1)对称自旋版复SYK模型，以更便于量子模拟，同时检验其在有限N、有限q区域是否保持复费米子SYK的关键动力学与量子混沌特征。

Method: 构造由随机电荷守恒q-自旋相互作用组成的U(1)对称自旋模型；在大N、大q极限下解析推导其复现复费米子SYK的鞍点结构；用精确对角化计算U(1)不变序敏感度的尺寸标度及能谱能级间距统计（比对GOE/GUE）；构造耦合簇链并用矩阵乘积态模拟研究畴壁动力学与磁化输运。

Result: 大N、大q极限下模型解析复现复费米子SYK的先导鞍点结构（化学势↔磁场、电荷密度↔磁化）。有限N、有限q下：q=2,4时序敏感度随系统尺寸增大，q=6时减小；半填充能谱统计符合GOE，相邻磁化扇区符合GUE；小簇区域畴壁动力学与扩散性磁化输运一致。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了U(1)对称自旋-SYK模型在序竞争、量子混沌和磁化输运方面的解析基准与有限尺寸特征，表明其可作为复费米子SYK的量子比特原生替代；并为低温、大系统极限的量子模拟提供了实验动机，该区域是经典方法难以触及的。

Abstract: The Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) model provides an analytically controlled setting for strongly interacting quantum dynamics and holography, but its fermionic formulation leads to nonlocal encoding overheads on qubit-based quantum processors. We study a U(1)-symmetric spin analogue of the complex SYK model, constructed from random charge-conserving $q$-spin interactions that are native to a qubit Hilbert space. In the large-$N$, large-$q$ limit, the model reproduces the leading saddle-point structure of complex fermionic SYK under the identification of chemical potential with magnetic field and charge density with magnetization. We investigate the finite-$N$, finite-$q$ regime using exact diagonalization. A U(1)-invariant ordering susceptibility increases with system size for $q = 2, 4$ but decreases for $q = 6$, while bulk-spectrum level statistics are consistent with the Gaussian orthogonal ensemble at half filling and the Gaussian unitary ensemble in the adjacent magnetization sector. We further construct coupled-cluster chains and obtain domain-wall dynamics consistent with diffusive magnetization transport in a small-cluster regime accessible to matrix-product-state simulations. These results establish analytical benchmarks and finite-size signatures of ordering, quantum chaos, and magnetization transport in U(1)-symmetric spin-SYK models, and they motivate quantum simulation of the low-temperature, large-system regime, which remains difficult for classical methods.

</details>


### [188] [Eigenvalue Crossings and Discontinuous BBP Transitions in Spiked Euclidean Random Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15927)
*Pasquale Casaburi,Pierpaolo Vivo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究嵌入对称欧几里得随机矩阵中的秩一信号的恢复问题，揭示其由噪声与信号主方向竞争所驱动的非连续BBP型相变，并用复本方法将其化为Fredholm特征值方程，给出恢复阈值的解析判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称欧几里得随机矩阵的条目稠密且几何相关，通常缺乏旋转不变性，这使经典的BBP相变（基于旋转不变的Wigner噪声）不再适用；作者希望理解在此类结构化、非旋转不变噪声下，秩一信号能否通过主导特征向量被恢复，以及相变的性质与阈值条件。

Method: 将信号恢复问题刻画为噪声主方向与信号方向的竞争；采用复本方法（replica method），在热力学极限下把谱问题约化为一个Fredholm特征值方程，进而解析计算最大特征值、特征向量分量分布及与信号的重叠；并用数值对角化验证二次距离核与秩二核等显式例子。

Result: 信号可能已编码于次主导特征方向，却未被主导主成分探测到；随着信号强度增大，竞争特征值发生交叉并改变排序，导致主导特征向量从噪声主导方向突变为携带信号的方向，产生非连续BBP型相变，重叠从零跳变到有限值。给出了存在正恢复阈值的判据（取决于未扰动谱边缘、尖峰均值及主特征函数空间平均），并解析得到极限最大特征值、特征向量分量分布与信号重叠，与数值对角化高度吻合。

Conclusion: 在缺乏旋转不变性的对称欧几里得随机矩阵中，秩一信号恢复由噪声与信号主方向竞争驱动，表现为非连续、BBP型的相变；复本方法结合Fredholm特征值方程能给出精确的阈值判据与极限谱量，为几何相关噪声下的主成分信号恢复提供了完整的解析理论。

Abstract: We study the recovery of a rank-one signal embedded in a symmetric Euclidean random matrix, whose dense and geometrically correlated entries generally lack rotational invariance. We show that signal recovery through the leading eigenvector is governed by a competition between the principal directions of the noise and the signal. In particular, the signal may already be encoded in a subleading eigendirection, while remaining undetected by the leading principal component. As the signal strength increases, a crossing between competing eigenvalues can change their ordering, causing the leading eigenvector to switch abruptly from a noise-dominated to a signal-bearing direction. This mechanism gives rise to a discontinuous BBP-type transition, in which the overlap with the signal jumps from zero to a finite value. Using the replica method, we reduce the problem to a Fredholm eigenvalue equation and derive general criteria for the existence of a positive recovery threshold, expressed in terms of the unperturbed spectral edge, the spike mean and the spatial averages of the principal eigenfunctions. We obtain analytically the limiting largest eigenvalue, eigenvector-component distribution and signal overlap, and illustrate the results with explicit solutions for quadratic distance and rank-two kernels, finding excellent agreement with numerical diagonalisation.

</details>


### [189] [Entropy production decomposition and fluctuation theorems under continuous feedback: From prescribed to stochastic protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16070)
*Long Him Cheung*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 文章将熵产生分解为“室内保持(houskeeping)”与“非自主(non-autonomous)”两部分，并推广到反馈控制情形。作者把低通滤波测量信号D视作随机协议，推导出x与D动力学的涨落定理，并用信息流解释；在可解的谐振子模型中，把测量-滤波过程映射为有效冷热库，从而将反馈冷却理解为热传导，信息流即x与D之间的传热熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统熵产生分解假设系统由“规定协议”驱动，此时室内保持项与非自主项各自满足涨落定理；但在反馈控制中，反馈参数依赖于测量结果而非预先给定的协议。作者希望研究这种反馈情形下分解如何改变，以及信息流与熵产生的对应关系。

Method: 考虑一个处于位置x的过阻尼布朗粒子，以低通滤波测量信号D作为反馈参数；将D的每条实现视为随机协议。推导x动力学与D动力学各自熵产生的涨落定理，并以信息流加以解释。针对可解的谐振子模型，将动力学映射为一个自主系统，其中测量与滤波过程充当有效热库，从而用热传导语言描述反馈冷却。

Result: 在滤波反馈下，x与D的熵产生分别满足涨落定理，并可由信息流解释。谐振子模型中，反馈冷却等价于热量从物理热库经粒子流向由测量-滤波过程构成的有效“更冷”热库；信息流可解释为x与D之间热传导所对应的熵。数值模拟验证了上述结果。

Conclusion: 反馈控制改变了熵产生的室内保持/非自主分解，但通过对测量信号动力学的涨落定理与信息流分析，反馈冷却可统一理解为向有效冷库的热传导，建立了熵产生分解与信息热力学之间的对应关系。

Abstract: When a system is driven by a prescribed protocol, the entropy production can be decomposed into the housekeeping and non-autonomous contributions, each satisfying a fluctuation theorem. Under feedback control, however, the feedback parameter depends on measurement outcomes rather than following a prescribed protocol. To study how the decomposition changes, we consider an overdamped Brownian particle at position $x$ with a low-pass-filtered measurement signal $D$ as the feedback parameter. Regarding each realization of $D$ as a stochastic protocol, we derive fluctuation theorems for the entropy production associated with the dynamics of $x$ and of $D$, and interpret them in terms of the information flow. For a solvable harmonic model, we map the dynamics onto an autonomous system in which the measurement and filtering process acts as an effective thermal bath. Feedback cooling thus corresponds to heat conduction from the physical bath through the particle to this colder bath, and the information flow can be interpreted as the entropy associated with the heat conduction between $x$ and $D$. We verify these results by numerical simulations.

</details>


### [190] [Anti-Kibble-Zurek behavior driven by dichotomous Markov noise in a quantum Ising chain: Generalized stochastic resonance in the defect density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16237)
*Mingwei Yang,Peng Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究量子伊辛链在非高斯二分马尔可夫噪声（DMN）下的淬灭动力学，发现噪声诱导的缺陷密度随关联时间呈非单调的广义随机共振行为，区别于高斯色噪声的单调变化，揭示了高斯与非高斯噪声在非平衡临界动力学中的本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对反Kibble-Zurek行为的研究主要局限于高斯噪声，高斯色噪声（GCN）下噪声诱导缺陷密度随关联时间单调变化。作者希望探究典型非高斯色噪声——二分马尔可夫噪声（DMN）如何影响量子伊辛链的临界淬灭动力学，以揭示噪声统计性质对非平衡相变的影响。

Method: 以量子伊辛链为模型，引入二分马尔可夫噪声（DMN）作为非高斯色噪声源，将噪声诱导的缺陷密度视为响应函数，分析其在快速噪声与慢速噪声两个区间随关联时间的变化趋势，并与高斯色噪声（GCN）情形对比；从DMN慢速噪声区间实现的稀疏性角度解释物理机制。

Result: 噪声诱导缺陷密度表现出广义随机共振：随关联时间非单调变化，在快速噪声区增大、在慢速噪声区减小。该共振源于慢速噪声区的反常AKZ行为，DMN诱导的趋势是非单调的，与GCN的单调性相反，其根源在于DMN慢速噪声下实现的稀疏性，这是功率谱无法刻画的特征。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了量子伊辛链非平衡临界淬灭动力学中高斯与非高斯噪声之间的根本区别，说明非高斯噪声的统计特性（如DMN实现的稀疏性）会显著改变反Kibble-Zurek行为，无法仅由功率谱描述。

Abstract: The anti-Kibble-Zurek behavior may arise under noise, adding a noise-induced contribution to the defect density beyond the conventional Kibble-Zurek term. Most investigations of such behavior have so far been restricted to Gaussian noise. For Gaussian colored noise (GCN), the power spectrum dictates a monotonic variation of this term with the correlation time. Here, we focus on dichotomous Markov noise (DMN), a prototypical non-Gaussian colored noise, in the quantum Ising chain. We show that the noise-induced defect density, taken as the response function, exhibits generalized stochastic resonance: it is nonmonotonic in the correlation time, growing in the fast-noise regime and decreasing in the slow-noise regime. This resonance stems from the anomalous AKZ behavior in the slow-noise regime, where the DMN-induced trend is nonmonotonic, in contrast to the monotonic GCN case. The underlying mechanism lies in the sparse realizations of DMN in the slow-noise regime, a feature not captured by the power spectrum. Our work thereby reveals a fundamental distinction between Gaussian and non-Gaussian noises in the nonequilibrium critical quench dynamics of the quantum Ising chain.

</details>


### [191] [Optimal staircase structure for bacterial adaptation in antibiotic landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16325)
*Kento Matsukawa,Shin-ichi Sasa,Namiko Mitarai*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究抗生素浓度空间异质性的离散结构如何影响细菌耐药性进化所需的时间，通过二维格点上的随机模型发现中间步数可产生最优适应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗生素环境的空间异质性驱动细菌耐药进化已广为人知，但这些环境离散结构（空间/浓度分多少步变化）如何控制细菌适应所需时间仍不清楚，亟需建立定量理论。

Method: 作者构建二维格点随机模型，两个轴分别代表物理空间与表型空间；在固定总体空间范围和浓度范围的前提下，比较不同步数，并按端到端首达时间对迁移率和突变率进行标度（scaling）；同时用直接 Gillespie 模拟验证预测。

Result: 增加步数会加速对下一个适应状态的搜索，但同时增大因重复种群饱和带来的累积代价；两种效应竞争可在中间步数处产生最优适应时间。Gillespie 模拟再现了适应时间对步数的依赖。

Conclusion: 离散环境结构通过“搜索加速”与“饱和代价累积”的权衡决定进化适应的时间尺度，中间步数最优，为环境离散结构如何塑造进化适应时间提供了可检验的预测。

Abstract: Spatial heterogeneity in antibiotic environments drives the evolution of bacterial resistance. However, how the discrete structure of these environments controls the time required for adaptation of bacteria remains unclear. We study this question using a stochastic model on a two-dimensional lattice whose axes represent physical space and phenotype space, comparing different numbers of steps while holding the overall spatial and concentration ranges fixed and scaling the migration and mutation rates by their end-to-end first-passage times. We find that increasing the number of steps accelerates the search for the next adapted state but increases the cumulative cost of repeated population saturation; this competition can produce an optimum at an intermediate number of steps. Direct Gillespie simulations reproduce the predicted dependence of the adaptation time on the number of steps, providing testable predictions for how discrete environmental structure shapes the timescale of evolutionary adaptation.

</details>


### [192] [Information Thermodynamics in Closed Quantum Many-Body Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16329)
*Yusuke O. Nakai,Shoki Sugimoto,Ryusuke Hamazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文为处于微观热平衡的闭合量子多体系统建立了信息热力学框架，在局域测量与局域哈密顿量有限时间控制的约束下，证明局域反馈所提取的功在热力学极限下与对应正则系综一致，并可用量子-经典互信息与Holevo信息表示；而基于远距测量结果的反馈则可利用隐藏的长程关联，使提取功相对于正则系综有广延性增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息热力学虽已解释麦克斯韦妖获取信息如何修正第二定律，但其在量子多体系统中的表述仍基本空白，作者旨在填补这一空缺。

Method: 针对初始处于微观热平衡（MITE）的闭合量子多体系统，施加物理上合理的限制（局域量子测量、由局域哈密顿量生成的有限时间控制），并额外假设每个局域反馈项仅依赖附近测量结果；随后严格证明热力学极限下可提取功与对应正则系综相当，并将其表示为量子信息量（量子-经典互信息、Holevo信息）。

Result: 证明了局域反馈下可提取功在热力学极限与正则系综一致，且可由量子-经典互信息与Holevo信息表示；同时证明利用远距测量结果的反馈能利用MITE中隐藏的长程关联，使可提取功相对正则系综产生广延性（正比于系统尺度）的增加。

Conclusion: 该框架严格界定了多体量子系统中麦克斯韦妖的信息热力学界限，揭示了反馈的局域性（相对于关联范围）决定能否超越正则系综的功提取，长程关联中的隐藏信息可带来广延性的功增益。

Abstract: Although information thermodynamics explains how the second law is modified due to the information acquired by Maxwell's demon, its formulation in many-body systems remains largely open. Here, we develop an information-thermodynamic framework for closed quantum many-body systems initially in microscopic thermal equilibrium (MITE), under physically motivated restrictions: local quantum measurements and finite-time controls generated by local Hamiltonians. By additionally assuming that each local feedback term depends only on nearby measurement outcomes, we rigorously prove that the extractable work coincides in the thermodynamic limit with that extractable from the corresponding canonical ensemble. We further express this extractable work in terms of quantum-information quantities, including the quantum-classical mutual information and the Holevo information for the corresponding canonical ensemble. We also show that feedback based on distant measurement outcomes can exploit long-range correlations hidden in MITE, yielding an extensive increase in extracted work relative to the canonical ensemble.

</details>


### [193] [Continuous-Time Random Walks with Internal Stochastic Renewal Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16383)
*Boris Z. Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过引入一种带内部状态的连续时间随机游走过程，推导出一类非局部（分数阶）主控方程，并展示其如何由微观机制产生异常扩散等宏观行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立微观随机机制与非局部宏观主控方程之间的对应关系，统一解释长等待时间与大跳跃耦合所导致的异常扩散现象，并拓展到有限时间收敛、自激/自抑制等更丰富的动力学情形。

Method: 作者构造一个连续时间随机游走：粒子跳跃时其内部状态按更新过程（renewal process）递增或重置为零，即通过"结构化密度"跟踪系统状态。由该公式可导出一系列积分-微分主控方程；针对特定重置概率、等待时间与跳跃分布，显式推导出模式依赖的记忆核（mode-dependent memory kernels）并考察其连续极限。

Result: 该框架可恢复时间-空间分数阶Fokker-Planck方程（经典地刻画长等待时间与大跳跃耦合导致的异常扩散）；得到一类通过几何递减的等待时间序列、在有限时间内收敛到平衡分布的过程；并构造了在各状态等待时间中引入自激与自抑制的过程，使其均方位移正比于 t^ν（对任意 ν>0）。

Conclusion: 通过上述例子阐明了这些过程的微观机制与其非局部宏观主控方程之间的联系；所提结构化连续时间随机游走框架具有广泛的生成能力，能统一涵盖多种异常输运与收敛行为。

Abstract: We introduce a continuous-time random walk process via a structured density by tracking the state of the particle as the system evolves. Whenever the particle makes a jump, its internal state either increases by one or resets to zero, so that the internal state evolves according to a renewal process. A wide range of governing integro-differential equations may be obtained from this formulation, some of which exhibit various types of anomalous behaviour. For certain choices of reset probabilities, waiting times and jump distributions, we explicitly derive the mode-dependent memory kernels and investigate their continuum limits. The framework can recover the time-and-space-fractional Fokker-Planck equation, which traditionally models the interplay between long wait times and large jumps and exhibits anomalous diffusion. Furthermore, we obtain a process which converges toward the equilibrium distribution as time approaches some finite value through a sequence of waiting times which decrease geometrically. Finally, we investigate a process that incorporates self-excitation and self-inhibition into the waiting times for each state to produce a mean square displacement proportional to $t^ν$ for any $ν> 0$. Through these examples, we illustrate the link between the microscopic mechanisms in these processes and their non-local macroscopic governing equations.

</details>


### [194] [Freezing transition and shock-like transients in ballistic random walks under threshold resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16733)
*Rahul Das,Satya N Majumdar,Arnab Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了一维弹道随机行走者在阈值重置下的瞬态动力学：稳态均匀分布且与速度分布无关，但弛豫速率却强依赖于速度分布，出现空间依赖的弛豫率、冻结转变、代数弛豫及冲击波式间断等丰富非平衡现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索速度涨落如何在不改变稳态的情况下深刻影响瞬态空间结构，揭示阈值重置系统中丰富的非平衡弛豫行为。

Method: 对从x_0>0出发、以速度分布φ(v)向原点阈值运动并在到达时重置的一维弹道随机行走者进行解析分析，结合蒙特卡洛模拟验证。分析各类φ(v)下弛豫率的空间依赖、冻结转变，以及幂律速度分布导致的代数弛豫和速度分布截断产生的冲击式间断。

Result: 稳态在[0,x_0]上均匀且与φ(v)无关；弛豫率显式依赖于空间坐标（区别于传统弛豫），并在临界位置之外冻结为与位置无关的常数值（冻结转变）；幂律速度分布导致代数弛豫；速度分布的尖锐截断在粒子传播子中产生向阈值传播的冲击式间断，其跳变高度随时间衰减。

Conclusion: 速度涨落虽不改变稳态，却能深刻影响瞬态空间结构，展示出丰富的非平衡行为；解析结果与蒙特卡洛模拟高度吻合。

Abstract: We investigate the transient dynamics of a one-dimensional ballistic random walker under threshold resetting (TR). The walker starts at $x_0>0$, moves towards a threshold at the origin with a velocity drawn from a distribution $φ(v)$, and on reaching the threshold is reset to $x_0$ with a new sampled velocity. Whenever the mean first-passage time to the threshold is finite, the walker reaches a steady state that is uniform on $[0,x_0]$ and independent of $φ(v)$. The relaxation towards it, in contrast, is highly sensitive to the behavior of $φ(v)$. For a large class of $φ(v)$-s, we find, rather remarkably, that the relaxation rate depends explicitly on the spatial coordinate, in contrast to conventional relaxation phenomena, where the rate is typically position-independent. Furthermore, this space-dependent relaxation rate eventually freezes to a constant, position-independent value beyond a critical position, a phenomenon we identify as \textit{freezing transition}. Additionally, for a power-law velocity distribution, the system exhibits an anomalously slow, algebraic relaxation towards the steady state. We also showed that a sharp cutoff in $φ(v)$ generates a shock-like discontinuity in the particle propagator that travels towards the threshold, with a jump height that decays with time. These results demonstrate how velocity fluctuations, despite leaving the steady state unchanged, can profoundly influence the transient spatial structures and showcase rich non-equilibrium behavior. Our analytical results are corroborated by Monte Carlo simulations, showing an excellent agreement.

</details>


### [195] [Work extraction from a single negative-temperature bath: a temperature gradient-driven heat flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16743)
*Wolfgang Rudolf Bauer*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过引入由弱耦合工作系统构成的大型虚拟集合，并为其赋予基于香农/信息熵的规范温度，将负温度热浴中的"纯功提取"重新解释为从高温系统（负温度浴）到低温系统（工作集合）的标准热流，从而调和了热力学第二定律与负温度浴功提取之间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负绝对温度热浴中仅做功提取（无净状态变化）看似违背开尔文-普朗克表述的第二定律，引发对其热力学稳定性的持续争论；本文旨在为这一矛盾提供一致的解释框架。

Method: 不将机器视为孤立系统，而是考虑由弱耦合工作系统组成的大型虚拟集合，并基于该集合的香农/信息熵为其赋予一个规范温度。在此框架下，框架自然推广到分子尺度机器，并通过典型示例展示集合的规范温度低于负温度浴的温度。

Result: 研究表明，集合的规范温度比负温度浴更"冷"，因此纯功提取可视为从高温系统（负温度浴）向低温系统（工作集合）的标准热流，与第二定律完全一致。该信息论-统计力学框架也自然解释了"毛驴行为"这一反直觉现象，即在负温度浴中增加工作量反而增强了功提取。

Conclusion: 通过为工作集合引入基于香农熵的规范温度，负温度浴中的纯功提取被重新诠释为常规的热流过程，成功调和了第二定律，并为"毛驴行为"等现象提供了自然解释。

Abstract: Work dissipation into a thermal bath is standard, whereas the reverse process -- sole work extraction from a thermal bath without a net state change -- contradicts the Kelvin-Planck formulation of the second law of thermodynamics. However, this prohibition is bypassed for baths at negative absolute temperature, sparking ongoing debate regarding their thermodynamic stability. Here, we reconcile the second law with sole work extraction from a negative-temperature bath by considering a large virtual assembly of weakly coupled working systems rather than an isolated machine. By assigning this assembly a canonical temperature derived from its Shannon/information entropy, the framework naturally extends to molecular-scale machines. We demonstrate paradigmatic examples where the assembly's canonical temperature is cooler than the negative-temperature bath. Consequently, sole work extraction represents standard heat flow from a hot system (the negative-temperature bath) to a cooler system (the working assembly), rendering the process fully consistent with the second law. This informational and statistical-mechanical framework also naturally explains the counter-intuitive phenomenon of "donkey behavior", where increasing the workload in a negative-temperature bath leads to enhanced work extraction.

</details>


### [196] [Detecting non-Hermitian topology from long-time dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16745)
*Antonio D'Abbruzzo,Rosario Fazio*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协议，通过协方差矩阵的弛豫过程来测量具有手征对称性的开放高斯系统Lindbladian谱的非厄米拓扑不变量，该方法在长时域工作且不依赖孤立边缘态，能克服Liouvillian趋肤效应的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统的非厄米拓扑性质通常因Liouvillian趋肤效应导致边缘态被掩盖，现有方法往往依赖孤立边缘态，难以可靠探测拓扑不变量，亟需一种在长时域、无需隔离边缘态即可工作的测量方案。

Method: 作者提出利用协方差矩阵的弛豫动力学来测量与手征对称开放高斯系统Lindbladian谱关联的非厄米拓扑不变量；该方法在长时域有效，不依赖边缘态。并以具有库诱导非互易性的Kitaev链为例进行验证，首先给出其拓扑相图的完整解析描述，随后展示该协议能区分所有不同相。

Result: 在库诱导非互易的Kitaev链上，作者获得了此前未知的完整解析拓扑相图，并证实所提协议能够可靠地区分系统所有不同的拓扑相。

Conclusion: 该协议为开放高斯系统中非厄米拓扑不变量的探测提供了一种稳健且实用的途径，即使在Liouvillian趋肤效应存在、边缘态被掩盖的情况下依然适用。

Abstract: We propose a protocol to measure the non-Hermitian topological invariant associated with the Lindbladian spectrum of chiral-symmetric open Gaussian systems from the relaxation of the covariance matrix. Importantly, the method works in the long-time regime and does not rely on isolating edge modes, so it remains applicable even when the Liouvillian skin effect may obscure them. We illustrate the technique on a Kitaev chain with reservoir-induced nonreciprocity, for which we first obtain a fully analytical and previously unknown description of its topological phase diagram. We then show that our protocol reliably distinguishes all the different phases.

</details>


### [197] [A deterministic theory of kinetic fluctuations in long-range interacting homogeneous systems without collective effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16893)
*Anwar El Rhirhayi,Jean-Baptiste Fouvry,Julien Barré*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文证明：在无集体效应的均匀长程相互作用系统中，描述有限N涨落的统计问题可约化为三个确定性动力学方程，从而用单次确定性的偏微分方程积分替代大规模N体模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程相互作用系统的慢弛豫由有限N涨落主导，但获取其完整统计需要巨量、昂贵的模拟集成；作者希望将这一统计问题在领头阶1/N下精确压缩为少数确定性方程，从而大幅降低计算代价。

Method: 从Landau-Dean-Kawasaki方程（即Landau方程大偏差原理的随机对应物）出发，推导经验分布函数n点矩在1/N领头阶的无穷层级；证明该层级可由准高斯闭包精确求解，得到由Landau方程、两点矩Lyapunov方程和三点矩动力学方程构成的极小Landau涨落层级，高阶矩由前三者代数地确定。

Result: 该层级精确保持碰撞不变量及其涨落，并能预测中心涨落区域内观测量的完整概率分布；与N体实现对比，它能够复现均值、方差及高阶矩，且精度随N单调提升。

Conclusion: 对任意大的N，仅需对三个耦合偏微分方程做一次确定性时间积分，即可替代大规模模拟集成，为长程相互作用系统的有限N涨落统计提供了一种高效且精确的求解途径。

Abstract: Finite-$N$ fluctuations govern the slow relaxation of long-range interacting systems, yet accessing their full statistics demands huge ensembles of costly simulations. We show that, in homogeneous systems without collective effects, this statistical problem collapses onto just three deterministic kinetic equations. Starting from the Landau-Dean-Kawasaki equation, i.e. the stochastic counterpart of the large deviation principle for the Landau equation, we derive the infinite hierarchy of $n$-point moments of the empirical distribution function at leading order in $1/N$. We prove that it is solved exactly by a quasi-Gaussian closure. The resulting minimal Landau fluctuation hierarchy is formed by the Landau equation, a Lyapunov equation for the two-point moment, and a kinetic equation for the three-point moment. From these three equations, all higher-order moments follow algebraically. The hierarchy exactly preserves the collisional invariants and their fluctuations, and predicts the full probability distribution of observables in the central fluctuation regime. Benchmarked against $N$-body realizations, it reproduces the mean, variance and higher-order moments with an accuracy that improves systematically with $N$. A single deterministic time-integration of three coupled partial differential equations thus replaces large ensembles of simulations, for any large $N$.

</details>


### [198] [Discrete Time Crystals from Anti-Unitary Floquet Driving and Dynamical Freezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17333)
*Anirban Das,Asmi Haldar,Arnab Das*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出反幺正周期驱动通过动力学冻结直接稳定离散时间晶体，磁化成为涌现守恒算符，双周期准能量精确配对锁定亚谐波响应，且有效哈密顿量保持准局域性，无限链模拟显示任意有限磁化态无衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种通过反幺正周期驱动直接稳定离散时间晶体的机制，解决时间晶体态在驱动下易衰减或非局域化的问题，并利用动力学冻结与准能量配对来实现稳健的亚谐波响应。

Method: 采用反幺正周期驱动方案，借助动力学冻结使磁化成为涌现守恒算符；利用双周期准能量的精确配对来钉扎亚谐波响应；保持有效哈密顿量的准局域性，并用无限链数值模拟验证动力学稳定性。

Result: 磁化作为涌现守恒算符出现，两周期准能量精确配对锁定亚谐波响应；有效哈密顿量保持准局域；无限链模拟显示任意有限磁化的普适初态均无可检测衰减。

Conclusion: 反幺正周期驱动为稳定离散时间晶体提供了直接途径，兼具亚谐波响应的稳定性与有效哈密顿量的准局域性，并给出了实验实现方案。

Abstract: We show anti-unitary periodic drive paves a direct path for stabilizing a Discrete Time-Crystal by Dynamical Freezing (DF). The magnetization appears as an emergent conserved operator, and an exact pairing of two-cycle quasi-energies pins the subharmonic response. DF keeps the effective Hamiltonian $\textit{quasi-local}$; infinite-chain simulations show no detectable decay for a generic state with any finite magnetization. We outline an experimental realization of the anti-unitary drive.

</details>
